## How to Use This Guide

### Document Structure

This user guide is organized into six main parts:

- **Part 1:** Introduction & Getting Started
- **Part 2:** Public User Guide (Frontend)
- **Part 3:** Administrator Guide (Backend)
- **Part 4:** Advanced Features & Customization
- **Part 5:** Troubleshooting & FAQs
- **Part 6:** Appendices

### Navigation Tips

- Use the **Table of Contents** to jump to specific sections
- Look for **visual indicators** throughout the document:
  - 💡 **Tip:** Helpful suggestions and best practices
  - ⚠️ **Warning:** Important cautions to prevent errors
  - ℹ️ **Note:** Additional information or clarification
  - ✅ **Success:** Confirmation of correct actions

### Conventions Used

- **Bold text:** Important terms, button names, menu items
- `Code text`: URLs, file paths, technical terms
- **[Links]:** Clickable references to other sections
- **Step 1, Step 2:** Numbered procedures to follow in order

# PART 1: INTRODUCTION & GETTING STARTED

---

## Chapter 1: Introduction to AHC Platform

### 1.1 What is Africa Health Collaborative (AHC)?

The **Africa Health Collaborative (AHC)** at Addis Ababa University is a pioneering initiative dedicated to strengthening health systems across the African continent. AHC serves as a hub for collaboration, innovation, and knowledge exchange among healthcare professionals, researchers, policymakers, and institutions.

**Mission:** To transform healthcare delivery in Africa through collaborative research, capacity building, and innovative solutions.

**Vision:** A healthier Africa where accessible, quality healthcare is available to all through sustainable partnerships and evidence-based interventions.

### 1.2 Purpose of the Platform

The AHC web platform serves as the digital gateway to the collaborative's activities, resources, and network. It provides:

- **Information Hub:** Comprehensive information about AHC's mission, programs, and impact
- **Resource Repository:** Access to research documents, educational materials, and toolkits
- **Event Management:** Information about conferences, workshops, and training opportunities
- **Collaboration Network:** Connections with partners, leaders, and team members
- **News & Updates:** Latest developments, announcements, and success stories
- **Contact Portal:** Direct communication channels with AHC team

### 1.3 Key Features Overview

#### For Public Users (Frontend)
- Browse news articles and announcements
- Explore health pillars (Employment, Entrepreneurship, Ecosystems)
- Access document repository and educational resources
- View upcoming events and register
- Meet AHC leaders and team members
- Connect with international and local partners
- Subscribe to newsletters
- Submit contact inquiries
- Dark/Light mode toggle for comfortable viewing

#### For Administrators (Backend)
- Content management system (News, Pages, Events, Announcements)
- AHC Leaders & Teams management with LinkedIn integration
- Media library and folder organization
- Contact message management with status tracking
- Email subscriber management with export capabilities
- Partner management (International & Local)
- User and role management
- Comprehensive permissions system
- Action logs and system monitoring
- Notification center
- AI-powered content generation
- Translation management
- Settings and configuration

### 1.4 Who Should Use This Guide?

This guide is designed for three main audiences:

**Public Users:**
- Visitors browsing the AHC website
- Researchers seeking resources
- Healthcare professionals
- Partner organizations
- Students and academics
- Event attendees
- Newsletter subscribers

**Content Managers:**
- Staff responsible for updating news and events
- Resource librarians
- Communications team members

**System Administrators:**
- IT staff managing the platform
- Superadmins with full system access
- User managers
- Settings administrators

### 1.5 System Requirements & Browser Compatibility

#### Minimum System Requirements

**For Public Users:**
- Internet connection (Broadband recommended)
- Modern web browser (see compatibility chart below)
- Screen resolution: 1024x768 or higher
- JavaScript enabled
- Cookies enabled

**For Administrators:**
- All public user requirements plus:
- Recommended screen resolution: 1366x768 or higher
- Stable internet connection
- Modern web browser with latest updates

#### Browser Compatibility

**Fully Supported Browsers:**

| Browser | Minimum Version | Recommended |
|---------|----------------|-------------|
| Google Chrome | 90+ | Latest |
| Mozilla Firefox | 88+ | Latest |
| Microsoft Edge | 90+ | Latest |
| Safari (macOS/iOS) | 14+ | Latest |

**Mobile Browsers:**
- Chrome for Android (Latest)
- Safari for iOS (iOS 14+)
- Samsung Internet (Latest)

**Not Supported:**
- Internet Explorer (All versions)
- Browsers older than 2 years

ℹ️ **Note:** For the best experience, always use the latest version of your preferred browser.

#### Required Browser Settings

- **JavaScript:** Must be enabled
- **Cookies:** Must be enabled (for login sessions)
- **Pop-ups:** Allow pop-ups from the AHC domain (for certain features)
- **Local Storage:** Must be enabled

### 1.6 Accessing the Website

#### Public Website Access

**Website URL:** `https://yourdomain.com` (Replace with actual AHC URL)

Simply enter the URL in your browser's address bar and press Enter. The homepage will load automatically.

#### Admin Panel Access

**Admin Login URL:** `https://yourdomain.com/admin`

Administrators must use the `/admin` path to access the backend panel.

**Login Requirements:**
- Valid email address (registered in the system)
- Password
- Two-factor authentication code (if enabled)

**First-Time Login:**
1. Navigate to the admin URL
2. Enter your email and temporary password (provided by system administrator)
3. You will be prompted to change your password
4. Create a strong password following the requirements
5. Click "Change Password" to proceed

💡 **Tip:** Bookmark the admin login page for quick access.

⚠️ **Security Warning:** Never share your admin credentials with anyone. Always log out when using shared or public computers.

## Chapter 2: Understanding User Roles

### 2.1 Public Visitors (No Login Required)

**Who They Are:**
Public visitors are anyone accessing the AHC website without logging in. This includes researchers, healthcare professionals, students, partner organizations, and general public interested in AHC's work.

**What They Can Do:**
- Browse all public pages (Home, About, Leaders, Partners, etc.)
- Read news articles and announcements
- View upcoming and past events
- Download publicly available documents and resources
- Browse media gallery
- View AHC leaders and team member profiles
- Submit contact form inquiries
- Subscribe to newsletter
- Register for events (if open registration)
- Share content on social media
- Switch between light and dark mode

**What They Cannot Do:**
- Access admin panel
- Create or edit content
- View restricted/private content
- Access contact message details
- View subscriber lists
- Manage users or settings

### 2.2 Registered Users

**Who They Are:**
Registered users have created an account on the platform. They may include event registrants, scholarship applicants, or community members.

**What They Can Do:**
- All public visitor capabilities, plus:
- Login to their account
- View their profile
- Track event registrations
- Save favorite content (if feature enabled)
- Receive personalized notifications
- Access member-only resources (if applicable)

**Authentication:**
- Email/password login
- Password reset via email
- Profile management

ℹ️ **Note:** The extent of registered user features may vary based on platform configuration.

### 2.3 Administrators

**Who They Are:**
Administrators are staff members with backend access to manage the AHC platform. They include content managers, system administrators, and superadmins.

**Administrator Types:**

#### Superadmin
- **Full system access**
- Manage all content types
- Manage users and roles
- Configure system settings
- View action logs
- Access all modules
- Cannot be restricted

#### Admin
- Manage most content (News, Events, Pages, Resources)
- Manage media library
- View reports and analytics
- Manage contact messages
- Limited user management
- Cannot access critical system settings

#### Editor
- Create and edit content
- Upload media
- Cannot publish (requires approval)
- Cannot manage users
- Cannot access settings

#### Contributor
- Create draft content only
- Cannot publish
- Cannot upload media
- Read-only access to most areas

ℹ️ **Note:** Actual roles and permissions can be customized. See Chapter 24 for detailed role management.

**What Administrators Can Do:**
- Access admin panel (`/admin`)
- Manage content (based on role)
- Upload and organize media
- Respond to contact messages
- Export subscriber lists
- Create and manage users
- Configure settings
- View system logs and monitoring
- Receive admin notifications

**Login Requirements:**
- Registered admin email
- Strong password
- Two-factor authentication (if enabled)
- Active status (not suspended)

### 2.4 Role-Based Permissions Overview

The AHC platform uses a **role-based access control (RBAC)** system. Permissions are grouped into roles, and roles are assigned to users.

#### How Permissions Work

**Permission Structure:**
Permissions follow the format: `module.action`

Examples:
- `news.view` - Can view news list
- `news.create` - Can create news articles
- `news.edit` - Can edit news articles
- `news.delete` - Can delete news articles

**Permission Inheritance:**
Higher-level roles typically include permissions from lower-level roles.

Example Hierarchy:
```
Superadmin (All Permissions)
    ↓
Admin (Most Permissions)
    ↓
Editor (Content Permissions)
    ↓
Contributor (Read + Draft Only)
```

#### Key Permission Categories

| Category | Examples | Who Has Access |
|----------|----------|----------------|
| Dashboard | `dashboard.view` | All admins |
| Content | `news.create`, `page.edit` | Editor and above |
| Media | `media.upload`, `media.delete` | Editor and above |
| Users | `user.create`, `user.edit` | Admin and Superadmin |
| Settings | `setting.edit` | Superadmin only |
| Logs | `actionlog.view` | Admin and Superadmin |

#### Checking Your Permissions

As an administrator:
1. Log in to the admin panel
2. Navigate to your **Profile**
3. View **My Roles** section
4. Click "View Permissions" to see your full permission list

💡 **Tip:** If you cannot access a feature you need, contact your system administrator to review your role permissions.

⚠️ **Important:** Never grant unnecessary permissions. Follow the principle of least privilege - users should only have permissions required for their job function.

# PART 2: PUBLIC USER GUIDE (FRONTEND)

## Chapter 3: Navigating the Homepage

### 3.1 Homepage Layout Overview

The AHC homepage serves as the main entry point to the platform, providing quick access to key information and features.

#### Hero Section

The **hero section** is the large, prominent area at the top of the homepage featuring:

- **Headline:** AHC's main tagline or current campaign message
- **Subheading:** Brief description of AHC's mission
- **Call-to-Action Buttons:**
  - "Learn More" → About AHC page
  - "Get Involved" → Contact or Partnership page
  - "Explore Resources" → Resources Library
- **Background:** High-quality image or video showcasing AHC's work
- **Rotating Slides:** May feature multiple hero slides highlighting different initiatives

💡 **Tip:** Hero slides typically auto-rotate every 5-7 seconds. Click navigation dots to jump to specific slides.

#### Featured Content Sections

Below the hero, you'll find organized sections:

**Latest News:**
- 3-4 most recent news articles
- Thumbnail images
- Headlines and brief excerpts
- "Read More" links
- "View All News" button

**Upcoming Events:**
- Next 3-4 scheduled events
- Event dates and locations
- Quick registration links
- "View All Events" button

**Our Impact:**
- Key statistics (e.g., "500+ Healthcare Professionals Trained")
- Impact metrics
- Infographics or counter animations

**Featured Partners:**
- Logos of key partner organizations
- Rotating carousel (if many partners)
- Click logos to view partner details

**Resource Highlights:**
- Recently added or featured resources
- Quick download links
- Resource type icons (PDF, Video, Document)

#### Quick Links Section

Located typically in the middle or sidebar:

- About AHC
- Contact Us
- Subscribe to Newsletter
- Upcoming Events
- Latest Publications
- Social Media Links

#### Footer Navigation

The footer appears at the bottom of every page and includes:

**Column 1: About**
- About AHC
- Mission & Vision
- Our Team
- Leadership

**Column 2: Programs**
- Health Employment
- Health Entrepreneurship
- Health Ecosystems
- Research Initiatives

**Column 3: Resources**
- Document Repository
- Educational Hub
- Publications
- Media Gallery

**Column 4: Connect**
- Contact Us
- Newsletter
- Social Media
- Events

**Bottom Footer:**
- Copyright notice
- Privacy Policy
- Terms of Service
- Site credits

### 3.2 Using the Main Navigation Menu

The **main navigation menu** is located at the top of every page, providing consistent access to major sections.

#### Desktop Navigation

**Menu Structure:**

```
[AHC Logo]  Home | About ▾ | Partners | Health Pillars ▾ | Resources ▾ | News | Events | Media | Contact
                                                                                    [Search] [Mode Toggle]
```

**Dropdown Menus (indicated by ▾):**

**About ▾**
- Mission & Vision
- Values & Principles
- AHC Leaders
- AHC Team
- Organizational Structure

**Health Pillars ▾**
- Health Employment
- Health Entrepreneurship
- Health Ecosystems

**Resources ▾**
- Document Repository
- Educational Resource Hub
- Other Resources
- Media Gallery

**How to Navigate:**
1. **Hover** over menu items with dropdown arrows (▾)
2. Submenu appears below
3. Click the desired submenu item
4. Page loads

**Direct Links:**
Menu items without dropdowns (Home, Partners, News, Events, Contact) load immediately when clicked.

#### Mobile Navigation

On mobile devices (tablets, smartphones), the navigation collapses into a **hamburger menu** (☰).

**How to Use:**
1. Tap the **☰ icon** (top-right corner)
2. Side menu slides in from the right
3. Tap menu items to expand dropdowns
4. Tap again to navigate
5. Tap **X icon** or outside menu to close

**Mobile Menu Features:**
- Full-screen or overlay panel
- Touch-friendly spacing
- Collapsible sections
- Smooth animations
- Search bar at top
- Dark/Light mode toggle at bottom

### 3.3 Search Functionality

The **search feature** helps you quickly find content across the AHC website.

**Search Bar Location:**
- **Desktop:** Top-right corner of navigation bar
- **Mobile:** Inside hamburger menu

**How to Search:**

**Step 1:** Click the search icon (🔍) or search bar

**Step 2:** Type your search query
- Keywords (e.g., "malaria prevention")
- Phrases (e.g., "health entrepreneurship program")
- Names (e.g., "Dr. Samuel Kifle")

**Step 3:** Press **Enter** or click the search icon

**Step 4:** View search results page

**Search Results Page:**

Results are displayed with:
- **Title:** Linked headline
- **Excerpt:** Text snippet with search term highlighted
- **Content Type:** Badge (News, Event, Resource, Page)
- **Date:** Publication or update date
- **Thumbnail:** Preview image (if available)

**Filtering Results:**
- **All Results:** Shows everything
- **News:** Articles only
- **Events:** Events only
- **Resources:** Documents and materials
- **Pages:** Static pages

**Sorting Options:**
- Relevance (default)
- Date (Newest First)
- Date (Oldest First)
- Alphabetical (A-Z)

**No Results:**
If search returns no results:
- Check spelling
- Try different keywords
- Use fewer words
- Try broader terms
- Browse categories instead

💡 **Tip:** Use quotation marks for exact phrase matching: `"health employment program"`

### 3.4 Dark/Light Mode Toggle

The AHC platform supports both **light mode** (default) and **dark mode** for comfortable viewing.

**Benefits of Dark Mode:**
- Reduces eye strain in low-light environments
- Saves battery on OLED screens
- Preferred aesthetic for some users
- Better for nighttime browsing

**How to Toggle:**

**Desktop:**
1. Locate the **theme toggle icon** (☀️/🌙) in the top-right corner of the navigation bar
2. Click the icon
3. Page instantly switches to dark/light mode
4. Your preference is saved for future visits

**Mobile:**
1. Open the hamburger menu (☰)
2. Scroll to the bottom
3. Tap the **Dark Mode toggle** switch
4. Menu and page switch themes
5. Close menu to continue browsing

**Visual Changes in Dark Mode:**

| Element | Light Mode | Dark Mode |
|---------|-----------|-----------|
| Background | White/Cream | Dark Gray/Black |
| Text | Dark Gray/Black | White/Light Gray |
| Cards | White | Dark Slate |
| Borders | Light Gray | Dark Border |
| Links | Blue | Light Blue |
| Buttons | Colored | Same (adjusted) |

**Persistence:**
Your theme preference is saved in your browser's local storage. When you return to the AHC website, your chosen theme will be remembered.

**System Preference Detection:**
If you haven't manually selected a theme, the website may detect your operating system's theme preference and match it automatically.

⚠️ **Note:** Some images or graphics may have optimized versions for dark mode for better contrast.

### 3.5 Responsive Mobile Navigation

The AHC platform is **fully responsive**, adapting to different screen sizes and devices.

#### Breakpoints

The design adapts at these common breakpoints:

- **Desktop:** 1200px and above (full layout)
- **Laptop:** 992px - 1199px (adjusted columns)
- **Tablet:** 768px - 991px (stacked content)
- **Mobile:** Below 768px (mobile-optimized)

#### Mobile-Specific Features

**Touch-Friendly Design:**
- Larger tap targets (minimum 44x44px)
- Increased spacing between elements
- Swipeable carousels
- Pull-to-refresh (on some pages)

**Optimized Content:**
- Single-column layout
- Collapsible sections
- Simplified navigation
- Reduced image sizes for faster loading

**Mobile Navigation Enhancements:**
- **Sticky Header:** Navigation bar stays visible while scrolling
- **Back to Top:** Floating button appears after scrolling down
- **Simplified Footer:** Collapsible footer sections
- **Touch Gestures:** Swipe left/right on image galleries

**Tablet Mode:**
Tablets (iPad, Android tablets) display a hybrid layout:
- 2-column grid instead of 4-column
- Larger text and images than mobile
- Touch-optimized but more spacious

💡 **Tip:** Rotate your mobile device to landscape mode for a wider view on some content-heavy pages.

**Performance on Mobile:**
- Lazy loading images (images load as you scroll)
- Reduced animations for slower connections
- Compressed images
- Optimized fonts

## Chapter 4: About AHC

### 4.1 About Page Overview

The **About AHC** page provides comprehensive information about the Africa Health Collaborative, its origins, mission, and organizational structure.

**How to Access:**
- Click **"About"** in the main navigation
- Select a submenu option from the dropdown
- Or visit the main About page directly

**Page Sections:**

1. **Introduction:** Overview of AHC and its establishment
2. **Mission Statement:** Core purpose and goals
3. **Vision Statement:** Future aspirations
4. **Core Values:** Guiding principles
5. **History:** Timeline of major milestones
6. **Leadership:** Link to AHC Leaders page
7. **Organizational Structure:** Departments and teams
8. **Partnerships:** Collaboration approach

### 4.2 Mission, Vision & Goals

#### Our Mission

**"To transform healthcare delivery in Africa through collaborative research, capacity building, and innovative solutions that strengthen health systems and improve health outcomes for all."**

**Mission Breakdown:**

**Transform Healthcare Delivery:**
- Modernize health service provision
- Implement evidence-based practices
- Leverage technology and innovation

**Collaborative Research:**
- Multi-institutional partnerships
- Cross-border studies
- Community-engaged research

**Capacity Building:**
- Training healthcare professionals
- Strengthening institutions
- Knowledge transfer programs

**Innovative Solutions:**
- Novel approaches to health challenges
- Technology integration
- Sustainable interventions

#### Our Vision

**"A healthier Africa where accessible, quality healthcare is available to all through sustainable partnerships and evidence-based interventions."**

**Vision Elements:**

**Healthier Africa:**
- Reduced disease burden
- Improved health indicators
- Enhanced quality of life

**Accessible Healthcare:**
- Geographic reach
- Affordable services
- Equity in health access

**Quality Healthcare:**
- Skilled workforce
- Modern facilities
- Evidence-based care

**Sustainable Partnerships:**
- Long-term collaborations
- Mutual benefit
- Shared resources

#### Strategic Goals

**Goal 1: Strengthen Health Systems**
- Build capacity in health workforce
- Improve health infrastructure
- Enhance health information systems

**Goal 2: Advance Research Excellence**
- Conduct high-impact research
- Generate actionable evidence
- Disseminate findings widely

**Goal 3: Foster Innovation**
- Support health entrepreneurship
- Implement digital health solutions
- Pilot new service delivery models

**Goal 4: Expand Partnerships**
- Engage international collaborators
- Strengthen local partnerships
- Build multi-sectoral alliances

**Goal 5: Ensure Sustainability**
- Diversify funding sources
- Build local ownership
- Create lasting impact

### 4.3 Values and Principles

AHC operates guided by core values that shape our work and relationships:

#### Excellence
**Definition:** Commitment to the highest standards in all we do

**In Practice:**
- Rigorous research methodologies
- Continuous quality improvement
- Professional development
- Performance accountability

#### Collaboration
**Definition:** Working together for greater impact

**In Practice:**
- Partnership-driven approach
- Knowledge sharing
- Multi-disciplinary teams
- Community engagement

#### Innovation
**Definition:** Embracing new ideas and approaches

**In Practice:**
- Encouraging creative solutions
- Piloting new models
- Technology adoption
- Learning from failure

#### Integrity
**Definition:** Honesty, transparency, and ethical conduct

**In Practice:**
- Ethical research practices
- Financial transparency
- Accountability to stakeholders
- Honest communication

#### Equity
**Definition:** Fair and just access to opportunities and resources

**In Practice:**
- Inclusive participation
- Addressing disparities
- Reaching underserved populations
- Gender equality

#### Sustainability
**Definition:** Building lasting solutions and capacity

**In Practice:**
- Local ownership
- Capacity building
- Environmental responsibility
- Long-term planning

**Administration & Finance**

### 4.5 Understanding AHC's Impact

AHC measures impact across multiple dimensions:

#### Research Impact
- **Publications:** Peer-reviewed articles in high-impact journals
- **Citations:** Influence on scientific community
- **Policy Influence:** Evidence informing health policies
- **Innovations:** New tools, methods, or interventions developed

#### Capacity Building Impact
- **Professionals Trained:** Number of healthcare workers skilled
- **Institutions Strengthened:** Organizations with enhanced capacity
- **Knowledge Products:** Training materials, guidelines, toolkits developed
- **Career Advancement:** Trainees progressing in their careers

#### Health Outcomes Impact
- **Lives Saved:** Direct health impact
- **Disease Reduction:** Decreased incidence or prevalence
- **Service Improvements:** Enhanced healthcare delivery
- **Access Expansion:** More people reached with services

#### Partnership Impact
- **Collaborations Formed:** New institutional partnerships
- **Resources Leveraged:** Funding and in-kind contributions mobilized
- **Knowledge Exchanged:** Cross-learning experiences
- **Joint Products:** Co-created solutions and publications

**Impact Stories:**
The website features regular impact stories highlighting:
- Individual beneficiary testimonials
- Partner success stories
- Research breakthroughs
- Training alumni achievements

💡 **Tip:** Visit the "News" section to read the latest impact stories and success cases.

### 4.6 Dynamic Pages

The AHC platform includes **dynamic pages** that are created and managed by administrators from the backend.

#### Understanding Dynamic Content

**What are Dynamic Pages?**
Dynamic pages are custom content pages created by AHC administrators to provide additional information that doesn't fit into standard categories like news or events.

**Examples of Dynamic Pages:**
- "Our History" - Detailed timeline of AHC's development
- "Awards & Recognition" - Honors and accolades received
- "Research Ethics Policy" - Institutional policies
- "Partnership Opportunities" - Information for potential partners
- "FAQ" - Frequently Asked Questions
- "Annual Reports" - Published yearly summaries

#### How Dynamic Pages Work

**Content Creation:**
- Administrators create pages using the backend content management system
- Pages can include rich text, images, videos, tables, and embedded content
- SEO-friendly URLs are generated (e.g., `/pages/partnership-opportunities`)

**Content Updates:**
- Pages are updated by administrators as needed
- No developer intervention required
- Changes appear immediately after publishing

**Content Organization:**
- Pages can be organized in a hierarchical structure
- Parent and child pages (e.g., About > History > Milestones)
- Flexible categorization

#### Navigating Custom Pages

**Pages in Main Navigation:**
Some dynamic pages appear in the main navigation menu. Look for them under relevant sections:

- About menu (e.g., "Our History", "Governance")
- Resources menu (e.g., "Policies", "Guidelines")
- New top-level menu items (if added by administrators)

**Pages in Footer:**
Additional dynamic pages often appear in the footer navigation:

- "Privacy Policy"
- "Terms of Service"
- "Cookie Policy"
- "Accessibility Statement"
- "Site Map"

**Direct Access:**
Pages have clean, descriptive URLs:
```
https://yourdomain.com/pages/partnership-opportunities
https://yourdomain.com/pages/annual-report-2024
https://yourdomain.com/pages/research-ethics
```

**Finding Pages:**
1. Check the About dropdown in main navigation
2. Browse footer links
3. Use the search function
4. Follow links from news articles or events

#### Page Features

**Rich Text Content:**
- Formatted text (headings, bold, italic, lists)
- Hyperlinks to internal and external resources
- Blockquotes and callout boxes
- Text alignment and styling

**Embedded Media:**
- Images with captions
- YouTube or Vimeo videos
- PDF documents (inline viewers)
- Infographics and charts

**Call-to-Action Buttons:**
- "Download PDF"
- "Contact Us"
- "Apply Now"
- "Learn More"
- "Register for Event"

**Custom Layouts:**
Some dynamic pages may have specialized layouts:
- Two-column text
- Image galleries
- Timeline visualizations
- Accordion/collapsible sections
- Tab-based content

**SEO-Friendly Slugs:**
Each page has a human-readable URL:
- Good: `/pages/partnership-opportunities`
- Not: `/pages/page?id=123`

**Breadcrumb Navigation:**
Pages display breadcrumbs showing your location:
```
Home > About > Our History
```
Click any breadcrumb to navigate back.

ℹ️ **Note:** The specific dynamic pages available on your AHC website depend on what administrators have created. Content is tailored to AHC's specific needs and may be updated regularly.

💡 **Tip:** Bookmark frequently visited dynamic pages for quick access.

### 5.5 Dark Mode Support

All leadership pages fully support dark mode for comfortable viewing.

**Dark Mode Features:**

**Background Colors:**

- Card backgrounds: Dark gray (#1e293b)
- Borders: Subtle dark borders

**Text Colors:**
- Headings: White (#ffffff)
- Body text: Light gray (#cbd5e1)
- Position titles: Light green
- Links: Light blue

**Image Handling:**
- Profile photos unchanged
- Border colors adjusted
- Shadows adapted for dark background

**Button Styling:**
- LinkedIn buttons remain blue
- Back buttons remain green
- Hover effects adjusted

**Contrast:**
All text maintains WCAG AA accessibility standards in both modes.

**Toggle Between Modes:**
Click the sun/moon icon in the header to switch themes. Your preference is saved for future visits.

💡 **Tip:** Dark mode is especially useful when browsing leader profiles in low-light environments or at night.

### 6.3 How to Become a Partner

Interested organizations can learn about partnership opportunities.

**Partnership Criteria:**

**Alignment:**
- Shared mission and values
- Complementary strengths
- Geographic or thematic fit

**Capacity:**
- Organizational stability
- Technical expertise
- Resource contribution potential

**Commitment:**
- Long-term engagement
- Active participation
- Mutual benefit focus

**Partnership Process:**

**Step 1: Express Interest**
- Submit initial inquiry via contact form
- Provide organization background
- Describe partnership interest

**Step 2: Initial Discussion**
- AHC team reviews inquiry
- Preliminary meeting/call
- Discuss potential collaboration areas

**Step 3: Proposal Development**
- Joint concept development
- Define roles and responsibilities
- Identify resources

**Step 4: Formalization**
- Memorandum of Understanding (MOU)
- Partnership agreement
- Workplan development

**Step 5: Implementation**
- Launch activities
- Regular communication
- Progress monitoring

**Contact for Partnerships:**
- Email: partnerships@ahc.edu.et (example)
- Phone: +251-XXX-XXXX
- Contact form: `/contact`

💡 **Tip:** Review existing partnerships to understand AHC's collaboration model before reaching out.

## Chapter 7: Health Pillars

AHC's work is organized around three interconnected **Health Pillars** that address critical health system challenges in Africa.

### 7.1 Health Employment Pillar

The **Health Employment** pillar focuses on strengthening the health workforce across Africa.

**How to Access:**
- Main Navigation → **Health Pillars** → **Health Employment**
- Or visit: `https://yourdomain.com/health-pillars/health-employment`

**Overview & Objectives:**

**Mission:**
To build a skilled, motivated, and adequately distributed health workforce capable of delivering quality healthcare services.

**Key Objectives:**

1. **Increase Health Workforce Capacity**
   - Train more healthcare professionals
   - Upgrade skills of existing workforce
   - Specialized training programs

2. **Improve Workforce Distribution**
   - Address urban-rural imbalances
   - Retention strategies for rural areas
   - Incentive programs

3. **Enhance Working Conditions**
   - Better facilities and equipment
   - Fair compensation
   - Career development opportunities

4. **Support Continuing Professional Development**
   - Regular training updates
   - Certification programs
   - Leadership development

**Programs & Initiatives:**

**Training Programs:**
- Pre-service training for students
- In-service training for practitioners
- Specialized certifications
- Leadership and management training

**Fellowships:**
- Clinical fellowships
- Research fellowships
- International exchange programs

**Mentorship:**
- Peer-to-peer mentoring
- Expert mentorship programs
- Online mentorship platforms

**Page Content:**

The Health Employment page typically includes:
- Program descriptions
- Success stories and testimonials
- Training calendar
- Application information
- Partner institutions
- Impact statistics
- Photo galleries of training events

**Available Opportunities:**

The page lists current opportunities such as:
- Open training programs
- Fellowship applications
- Job openings in partner institutions
- Volunteer opportunities
- Research positions

**How to Apply:**

**Step 1:** Review available opportunities
**Step 2:** Check eligibility criteria
**Step 3:** Prepare required documents
**Step 4:** Submit application via provided link or form
**Step 5:** Await confirmation and next steps

💡 **Tip:** Subscribe to the newsletter to receive notifications about new health employment opportunities.

### 7.2 Health Entrepreneurship Pillar

The **Health Entrepreneurship** pillar supports innovative health ventures and entrepreneurs.

**How to Access:**
- Main Navigation → **Health Pillars** → **Health Entrepreneurship**
- Or visit: `https://yourdomain.com/health-pillars/health-entrepreneurship`

**Overview & Objectives:**

**Mission:**
To foster a vibrant health innovation ecosystem by supporting entrepreneurs developing solutions to Africa's health challenges.

**Key Focus Areas:**

1. **Digital Health Solutions**
   - mHealth applications
   - Telemedicine platforms
   - Health information systems
   - AI/ML in healthcare

2. **Medical Devices & Diagnostics**
   - Low-cost diagnostic tools
   - Medical equipment innovations
   - Laboratory technologies

3. **Health Products & Services**
   - Pharmaceuticals and nutraceuticals
   - Health service delivery models
   - Supply chain innovations

4. **Health Financing Innovations**
   - Insurance models
   - Payment systems
   - Financing mechanisms

**Venture Showcase:**

**How to Access:**
- Click **"Venture Showcase"** link on Health Entrepreneurship page
- Or visit: `https://yourdomain.com/health-pillars/health-entrepreneurship/ventures`

**Venture Showcase Features:**

**Venture Cards:**
Each featured venture displays:
- Company/venture name

- Stage (Idea, Prototype, Pilot, Scale-up)
- Location
- "Learn More" button

**Venture Detail Page:**

Click any venture to view:
- Full venture description
- Problem being solved
- Solution overview
- Impact achieved
- Team members

- Photos or demo videos

**Applying for Entrepreneurship Support:**

**Support Offered:**

**Incubation:**
- Office space
- Mentorship
- Business development support
- Networking opportunities

**Acceleration:**
- Intensive 3-6 month programs
- Expert coaching
- Pitch preparation
- Investor connections

**Funding:**
- Seed grants
- Competition prizes
- Investor introductions
- Crowdfunding support

**Technical Assistance:**
- Regulatory guidance
- Clinical validation support
- Market research
- Partnership facilitation

**Application Process:**

**Step 1: Review Eligibility**
- Early-stage health venture
- Addressing African health challenge
- Scalable solution
- Committed team

**Step 2: Prepare Application**
- Business plan or concept note
- Team CVs
- Problem and solution description
- Impact projections

**Step 3: Submit Online Application**
- Click "Apply" button on page
- Fill out application form
- Upload required documents
- Submit

**Step 4: Selection Process**
- Initial screening
- Shortlist notification
- Interview/pitch
- Final selection

**Step 5: Onboarding**
- Sign agreement
- Orientation session
- Begin program

**Success Stories:**

The page features success stories of supported ventures:
- How they started
- Support received from AHC
- Milestones achieved
- Current status
- Lessons learned

**Requesting a Demo:**

**How to Access:**
- Health Entrepreneurship page → "Request a Demo" button
- Or visit: `https://yourdomain.com/health-pillars/health-entrepreneurship/demo`

**Demo Request Form:**

Fill out:
- Your name and organization
- Email and phone
- Type of demo interested in (which venture/solution)
- Preferred date/time
- Purpose of demo
- Number of attendees
- Special requirements

**What to Expect:**
- Confirmation email within 2 business days
- Demo scheduled at mutually convenient time
- Virtual or in-person (if local)
- 30-60 minute presentation and Q&A
- Follow-up materials provided

- French

- PDF

Each document shows:

- Author(s)
- Publication date
- Category badge
- Brief description/abstract
- File size
- Download button

**Grid View:**
- Card layout
- Thumbnail or icon
- Title and key info
- Download button

**Sorting Options:**
- Relevance (if searching)
- Date (Newest First) - Default
- Date (Oldest First)
- Title (A-Z)
- Title (Z-A)
- Most Downloaded

**Document Categories:**

**Research Publications:**
- Peer-reviewed journal articles
- Working papers
- Research reports
- Data reports
- Systematic reviews

**Policy Briefs:**
- Evidence summaries
- Policy recommendations

- Position papers

**Technical Reports:**
- Project reports
- Evaluation reports

- Feasibility studies

**Guidelines & Protocols:**
- Clinical guidelines
- Standard operating procedures

**Browsing Documents:**

**Step 1:** Navigate to Document Repository

**Step 2:** Use filters to narrow down
- Select category
- Choose topic(s)
- Set date range
- Pick document type

**Step 3:** Browse results
- Scroll through list
- Click titles to view details
- Preview description

**Step 4:** View document details (optional)
- Click "View Details"
- See full description
- View author information
- Read abstract
- Check citation information

**Downloading Files:**

**Step 1:** Locate desired document

**Step 2:** Click **"Download"** button
- Blue download icon
- File downloads to your default downloads folder

**Step 3:** Open downloaded file
- Use appropriate software (PDF reader, Word, etc.)
- Save for offline access

**File Formats:**
- **PDF:** Most common, requires PDF reader (Adobe, browser)
- **DOC/DOCX:** Microsoft Word or compatible software
- **PPT/PPTX:** PowerPoint presentations
- **XLS/XLSX:** Excel spreadsheets

**Citation Information:**

Each document detail page includes:
- Suggested citation format
- APA style
- Chicago style
- Vancouver style
- "Copy Citation" button

**Example:**
```
APA: Author, A. A. (Year). Title of document. 
Africa Health Collaborative. 
https://yourdomain.com/resources/document-slug
```

💡 **Tip:** Use the search function if you know specific keywords. Use filters if you're browsing by topic area.

- **Publication Date:** "Dec 10, 2025" or "2 days ago"
- **Read Time:** "5 min read" (estimated)
- **"Read More" Link:** Blue text with arrow

**News Categories:**

Articles are organized by category:
- **Research & Publications:** New studies, findings, papers
- **Events:** Event announcements, recaps, highlights
- **Partnerships:** New collaborations, MOU signings
- **Training:** Workshops, courses, capacity building
- **Awards & Recognition:** Honors, grants, achievements
- **Impact Stories:** Success stories, testimonials
- **Announcements:** General updates, changes

**Filter by Category:**

- Category pills/tabs at top of page
- Click category to filter
- "All News" shows everything
- Active category highlighted

**Sorting Options:**

- **Newest First** (default)
- **Oldest First**
- **Most Read**
- **Alphabetical**

**Pagination:**

- 12 articles per page (default)
- Page numbers at bottom
- "Previous" and "Next" buttons
- "Load More" button (alternative design)

### 9.2 Reading News Details

Click any news article to view the full story.

**Article Page URL:**
```
https://yourdomain.com/news/[article-slug]
https://yourdomain.com/news/ahc-launches-new-training-program
```

**Article Page Layout:**

**Header Section:**
- Full-width featured image
- Overlay with category badge
- Publication date and author
- Read time estimate

**Article Title:**
- Large, bold headline
- Subtitle (if applicable)

**Article Meta Information:**
- **Author:** Name and photo (if available)
- **Published:** December 10, 2025
- **Updated:** December 11, 2025 (if edited)
- **Category:** Badge link to category
- **Tags:** Clickable topic tags

**Article Body:**
- Formatted text with proper spacing
- Headings and subheadings
- Bulleted and numbered lists
- Blockquotes for emphasis
- Embedded images with captions
- Embedded videos
- Hyperlinks to related content
- Data tables (if applicable)

**Social Sharing Buttons:**

Located at top or side:
- **Facebook:** Share on Facebook
- **Twitter/X:** Tweet article
- **LinkedIn:** Share on LinkedIn
- **Email:** Send via email
- **Copy Link:** Copy URL to clipboard

**Related Articles:**

At the bottom:
- "Related News" section
- 3-4 similar articles
- Same category or tags
- Thumbnails and headlines
- Click to read

**Breadcrumb Navigation:**
```
Home > News > Research & Publications > [Article Title]
```

**Dark Mode Support:**
All news pages adapt to dark mode with:
- Dark backgrounds
- Light text
- Adjusted image contrast
- Readable formatting

You can easily share AHC news articles on social media platforms.

**Social Share Buttons:**

Located prominently (top-right or left sidebar):

- Opens Twitter share window

- Post

- Click LinkedIn icon
- Opens LinkedIn share
- Add commentary

**WhatsApp** (mobile):

Some articles may display share count:

### 9.5 Dark Mode Support

All news and announcement pages fully support dark mode.

**Dark Mode Features:**

- Dark background (#0f172a)
- Card backgrounds (#1e293b)
- White/light text
- Adjusted image overlays
- Dark borders

**Article Reading Experience:**
- Dark background for comfort
- High contrast text for readability
- Adjusted image brightness (if needed)
- Dark blockquotes and callouts
- Comfortable long-form reading

**Accessibility:**
- WCAG AA compliant contrast ratios
- Readable text sizes
- Proper line spacing
- Clear link differentiation

**Toggle:**
Click sun/moon icon in header to switch between light and dark mode. Preference persists across page visits.

💡 **Tip:** Dark mode is ideal for reading long articles in low-light environments or at night, reducing eye strain.

### 10.3 Registering for Events

**Registration Process:**

**Step 1:** Click "Register Now" button on event page

**Step 2:** Fill Registration Form

**Personal Information:**
- First Name
- Last Name
- Email Address
- Phone Number
- Organization/Institution
- Job Title
- Country

**Registration Type:**
- Physical Attendance
- Virtual Attendance
- Hybrid (both options)

**Fee Category:**
- Regular
- Student (requires proof)
- Partner Organization
- Group Registration (5+ people)

**Additional Information:**
- Dietary requirements
- Accessibility needs
- Session preferences
- Networking interests

**Payment** (if applicable):
- Credit/Debit Card
- Bank Transfer
- Mobile Money
- Invoice Request (for organizations)

**Step 3:** Review and Submit
- Review entered information
- Accept terms and conditions
- Submit registration

**Step 4:** Confirmation
- Confirmation email sent immediately
- Registration ID
- Payment instructions (if applicable)
- Calendar invite attachment

**Step 5:** Pre-Event Communication
- Reminder emails
- Zoom link (for virtual)
- Venue details
- Final agenda
- Pre-reading materials

**Cancellation Policy:**
- Full refund: > 14 days before event
- 50% refund: 7-14 days before
- No refund: < 7 days before
- Substitution allowed anytime

### 10.4 Adding Events to Personal Calendar

Make sure you don't miss registered events by adding them to your calendar.

**Add to Calendar Options:**

**From Event Page:**
- Click "Add to Calendar" button
- Choose calendar type:
  - Google Calendar
  - Apple Calendar (iCal)
  - Outlook Calendar
  - Yahoo Calendar
  - Download .ics file

**Google Calendar:**
- Click "Google Calendar"
- Opens Google Calendar
- Event details pre-filled
- Click "Save"
- Event appears in your calendar

**Apple/Outlook (.ics file):**
- Click "Download .ics"
- File downloads
- Open file
- Calendar app opens
- Import event
- Save

**From Confirmation Email:**
- Registration email includes .ics attachment
- Open attachment
- Import to your calendar app

**Calendar Event Details Include:**
- Event title
- Date and time
- Location (with map link)
- Description
- Registration link
- Reminder set (24 hours before)

## Chapter 12: Contact and Communication

### 12.1 Using the Contact Form

The **Contact Form** allows you to send inquiries, feedback, or messages directly to the AHC team.

**How to Access:**
- Main Navigation → **Contact**
- Or visit: `https://yourdomain.com/contact`
- Footer → "Contact Us" link

**Contact Page Layout:**

**Contact Information Section:**

Displays AHC's contact details:
- **Email:** info@ahc.edu.et
- **Phone:** +251-XXX-XXXX
- **Address:**
  ```
  Africa Health Collaborative
  Addis Ababa University
  [Street Address]
  Addis Ababa, Ethiopia
  ```
- **Office Hours:** Monday - Friday, 8:30 AM - 5:00 PM EAT

**Map:**
- Embedded Google Map
- Shows AHC office location
- Click to open in Google Maps
- Get directions

**Contact Form Section:**

**Form Title:** "Get in Touch" or "Send us a Message"

**Form Fields:**

**1. First Name** (Required)
- Text input
- Placeholder: "John"
- Validation: Required
- Error: "First name is required"

**2. Last Name** (Required)
- Text input
- Placeholder: "Doe"
- Validation: Required
- Error: "Last name is required"

**3. Email** (Required)
- Email input
- Placeholder: "john.doe@example.com"
- Validation: Required, valid email format
- Error: "Please enter a valid email address"

**4. Subject** (Required)
- Dropdown selection or text input
- Options:
  - General Inquiry
  - Partnership Opportunity
  - Media Request
  - Event Information
  - Technical Support
  - Other
- Or free text field
- Validation: Required

**5. Message** (Required)
- Textarea (multi-line)
- Placeholder: "Type your message here..."
- Minimum length: 10 characters
- Maximum length: 5000 characters
- Character counter: "0 / 5000"
- Validation: Required, min 10 characters

**Submit Button:**
- "Send Message" button
- Primary blue button
- Disabled until form valid
- Loading spinner while submitting

**Filling Out Contact Details:**

**Step 1:** Enter your first and last name
- Use your real name for credibility
- Required for response

**Step 2:** Enter your email address
- Use active email
- Double-check for typos
- Response will be sent here

**Step 3:** Select or enter subject
- Choose most relevant option
- Helps route message to right team
- Or write custom subject

**Step 4:** Compose your message
- Be clear and concise
- Include relevant details
- Provide context
- Mention any deadlines
- Add contact preference if urgent

**Example Message:**
```
Dear AHC Team,

I am a researcher at [Institution] interested in 
collaborating on health systems research in Ethiopia. 
I would like to discuss potential partnership 
opportunities focusing on primary healthcare strengthening.

Could we schedule a call next week to explore this further?

Best regards,
[Your Name]
```

**Submission Process:**

**Step 5:** Review your information
- Check for typos
- Verify email address
- Ensure message is clear

**Step 6:** Click "Send Message"
- Button shows loading spinner
- "Sending..." text appears
- Form disabled during submission

**Submission Confirmation:**

**Success Message:**

✅ **Success!**
- Green checkmark icon
- "Your message has been sent successfully!"
- "We'll get back to you within 2 business days."
- Form clears automatically

**What Happens Next:**
- Email sent to AHC team immediately
- You receive confirmation email
- Team reviews message
- Response sent within 1-2 business days
- Check spam folder if no response

**Error Messages:**

If submission fails:

❌ **Validation Errors:**
- Red text below field
- "Email is required"
- "Message must be at least 10 characters"
- Fix errors and resubmit

❌ **Network Error:**
- "Failed to send message. Please try again."
- Check internet connection
- Retry submission

❌ **Server Error:**
- "Something went wrong. Please try again later."
- Or email directly: info@ahc.edu.et

**Form Accessibility:**

- Tab navigation between fields
- Screen reader compatible
- Clear error announcements
- Focus indicators
- Keyboard-accessible submit

**Security:**
- CAPTCHA (if spam protection enabled)
- SSL encrypted transmission
- Privacy policy link
- Data protection notice

💡 **Tip:** For urgent matters, call the office directly. For media inquiries, use "Media Request" as subject for faster routing.

### 12.2 Newsletter Subscription

Stay updated with AHC's latest news, events, and opportunities.

**How to Subscribe:**

**Option 1: Newsletter Form (Homepage or Footer)**

Located in:
- Homepage (near bottom)
- Footer of every page
- Contact page

**Subscription Form:**

**Simple Form:**
- **Email Address** field
- "Subscribe" button

**Example:**
```
[  john.doe@example.com  ]  [Subscribe]
```

**Step 1:** Enter your email address
**Step 2:** Click "Subscribe" button
**Step 3:** Confirmation message appears

**Extended Form:**

Some subscription forms include:
- Email address (required)
- First name (optional)
- Last name (optional)
- Areas of interest (checkboxes):
  - Research
  - Training
  - Events
  - Partnerships
  - All Updates

**Subscription Confirmation:**

**Double Opt-In Process:**

**Step 1:** Fill out subscription form and submit

**Step 2:** Check your email
- Subject: "Confirm Your Subscription to AHC Newsletter"
- From: AHC Newsletter
- Contains verification link

**Step 3:** Click verification link
- Link valid for 24 hours
- Opens confirmation page
- "Subscription Confirmed!" message

**Step 4:** Start receiving newsletters
- Welcome email sent
- First newsletter in next distribution

**Newsletter Content:**

**Frequency:**
- Monthly newsletter (typical)
- Or quarterly, biweekly (check subscription page)

**Typical Newsletter Includes:**

**Featured Story:**
- Highlight of the month
- Major achievement or announcement

**Latest News:**
- Brief summaries of recent news
- Links to full articles

**Upcoming Events:**
- Event calendar
- Registration links
- Early bird notifications

**New Resources:**
- Recently published documents
- New training materials
- Research publications

**Opportunities:**
- Job openings
- Fellowships
- Calls for proposals
- Partnership opportunities

**Impact Story:**
- Success story or testimonial
- Photo feature

**From the Director:**
- Message from leadership
- Strategic updates

**Managing Email Preferences:**

**Update Preferences:**

**Option 1:** Click "Update Preferences" link in email footer

**Option 2:** Visit preferences page
- URL in newsletter footer
- Login or enter email
- Manage settings

**Preference Options:**

**Email Frequency:**
- Weekly
- Monthly
- Quarterly

**Content Preferences:**
- All content
- Specific topics only
  - [ ] Research & Publications
  - [ ] Events
  - [ ] Training Opportunities
  - [ ] Partnerships
  - [ ] Impact Stories

**Email Format:**
- HTML (with images and styling)
- Plain text (simple, smaller size)

**Unsubscribing from Newsletter:**

**Option 1:** Click "Unsubscribe" link
- Link in every email footer
- Opens unsubscribe page
- Click "Confirm Unsubscribe"
- Confirmation: "You've been unsubscribed"

**Option 2:** Update preferences to none
- Uncheck all content options
- Saves as unsubscribed

**Option 3:** Contact support
- Email: info@ahc.edu.et
- Subject: "Unsubscribe Request"
- Include email address to remove

**Re-subscribing:**
- Use subscription form again
- Go through confirmation process
- Preferences reset to default

ℹ️ **Note:** Unsubscribe requests are processed immediately. You may still receive 1-2 scheduled emails already in queue.

**Privacy & Data Protection:**
- Email not shared with third parties
- Used only for AHC communications
- Secure storage
- GDPR compliant (if applicable)
- Privacy policy link provided

# PART 3: BACKEND ADMINISTRATOR GUIDE

*Chapters 13-28: Comprehensive administrative controls and content management*

## Chapter 13: Admin Panel Overview

*Accessing and navigating the AHC administrative backend*

### 13.1 Accessing the Admin Panel

**Login URL:**

The administrative backend is accessed through a separate URL from the public website:

```
https://admin.ahc.edu.et/admin
```

Or if using subdomain structure:
```
https://backend.ahc.edu.et/admin
```

**Login Process:**

1. **Navigate to Admin URL**
   - Open your web browser
   - Enter the admin URL
   - Bookmark for future access

2. **Enter Credentials**
   - Email address (registered admin account)
   - Password (secure password you created)
   - Optional: Remember me checkbox (only on trusted devices)

3. **Two-Factor Authentication (if enabled)**
   - Enter 6-digit code from authenticator app
   - Or use backup code if app unavailable
   - Option to trust device for 30 days

4. **Successful Login**
   - Redirects to admin dashboard
   - Welcome message with your name
   - Last login time displayed

**Login Requirements:**

✅ **Valid admin account** - Must be created by Super Administrator

✅ **Active status** - Account must not be suspended or deactivated

✅ **Assigned role** - At least one role with backend permissions

✅ **Modern browser** - Chrome, Firefox, Safari, Edge (latest versions)

**Login Troubleshooting:**

**Forgot Password:**
1. Click "Forgot Password?" link on login page
2. Enter your registered email address
3. Check email for password reset link
4. Click link (valid for 60 minutes)
5. Create new strong password
6. Confirm new password
7. Login with new credentials

**Account Locked:**
- After 5 failed login attempts, account locks for 15 minutes
- Contact Super Administrator if you need immediate access
- Check that Caps Lock is off
- Verify email address spelling

**Two-Factor Issues:**
1. Ensure device time is synchronized
2. Try backup codes (provided during 2FA setup)
3. Contact administrator to temporarily disable 2FA
4. Reconfigure 2FA after regaining access

### 13.2 Dashboard Overview

After successful login, you'll land on the **Admin Dashboard** - your central command center.

**Dashboard Layout:**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Logo        [AHC Admin]           🔔 🌐 👤 [Your Name] │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ┌──────────┐                                            │
│ │          │  Statistics Cards                          │
│ │ Sidebar  │  ┌───────┐ ┌───────┐ ┌───────┐ ┌───────┐ │
│ │  Menu    │  │ News  │ │Events │ │Users  │ │ Media │ │
│ │          │  │  125  │ │  48   │ │  234  │ │ 1,456 │ │
│ │          │  └───────┘ └───────┘ └───────┘ └───────┘ │
│ │          │                                            │
│ │          │  Recent Activity                           │
│ │          │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│ │          │  │ • User John added news article       │ │
│ │          │  │ • Event "Workshop" updated           │ │
│ │          │  │ • New contact message received       │ │
│ │          │  └──────────────────────────────────────┘ │
│ │          │                                            │
│ │          │  Quick Actions                             │
│ │          │  [+ News] [+ Event] [+ Page] [+ Media]   │
│ └──────────┘                                            │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Key Dashboard Elements:**

**1. Top Navigation Bar:**
- **AHC Logo** - Clickable, returns to dashboard
- **Notification Bell Icon** - Shows unread notifications count (red badge)
- **Language Selector** - Globe icon to change backend language
- **Profile Dropdown** - Your name/avatar with menu:
  - My Profile
  - Account Settings
  - Activity Log
  - Logout

**2. Sidebar Menu:**
- Collapsible navigation menu
- Icons + text labels
- Active page highlighted
- Grouped by functionality
- Permission-based visibility (you only see what you can access)

**3. Statistics Cards:**
- Real-time counts of key content
- Color-coded by category
- Clickable to view full list
- Updates automatically

**4. Recent Activity Feed:**
- Last 10-20 actions in the system
- Who did what and when
- Filterable by action type
- Real-time updates

**5. Quick Actions:**
- One-click buttons to create new content
- Most common tasks
- Customizable based on your role

**6. Charts and Graphs (if enabled):**
- Website traffic trends
- Content publication timeline
- User engagement metrics
- Event attendance tracking

**Dashboard Customization:**

Some dashboard elements may be customizable based on your role and preferences:

- Widget visibility
- Default language
- Activity feed filters
- Statistics card order

### 13.3 Backend Language Selection

The AHC backend supports **multiple interface languages** for administrators, separate from the frontend public language selection.

**Changing Backend Language:**

1. **Locate Language Selector**
   - Top-right navigation bar
   - Globe icon (🌐)
   - Current language displayed

2. **Select Your Language**
   - Click globe icon
   - Dropdown menu appears
   - Available languages:
     - **English** (default)
     - **Amharic** (አማርኛ)
     - Other languages as configured

3. **Language Changes**
   - Interface immediately updates
   - Menu labels translate
   - Button text translates
   - Form labels translate
   - Help text translates
   - **Content you manage remains in its original language**

**What Changes:**

✅ **System Interface:**
- All menus and navigation
- Button labels
- Form field labels
- Validation messages
- Success/error notifications
- Help tooltips
- Datatable headers

✅ **Static Text:**
- Dashboard headings
- Section titles
- Breadcrumbs
- Placeholders

**What Doesn't Change:**

❌ **Your Content:**
- News articles you write
- Event descriptions
- Page content
- Media titles
- User-generated data

❌ **Database Content:**
- Leader names
- Partner information
- Contact messages
- Any previously saved content

**Language Persistence:**

- Your language selection is **saved to your account**
- Persists across login sessions
- Each administrator can have different language preference
- Does not affect other users

**Backend vs Frontend Language:**

⚠️ **Important Distinction:**

| Feature | Backend Language | Frontend Language |
|---------|-----------------|-------------------|
| Purpose | Admin interface translation | Public content translation |
| Scope | Menus, forms, buttons | Actual content |
| Who Controls | Each admin chooses | Website visitors choose |
| Persistence | Saved to admin account | Saved to browser session |
| Content Impact | None | Shows translated versions |

**Use Cases:**

**Scenario 1: Bilingual Administrator**
- Admin prefers Amharic interface
- Selects Amharic backend language
- Creates news article in English
- Creates second version in Amharic
- Public sees appropriate version based on their frontend language selection

**Scenario 2: International Collaboration**
- English-speaking consultant given temp admin access
- Uses English backend interface
- Reviews Amharic content (with translation help)
- Approves content for publication

**Adding New Backend Languages:**

Super Administrators can add new backend interface languages:

1. Language files must be translated
2. Uploaded to backend language directory
3. Configured in settings
4. Appears in all admin language selectors

### 13.4 Notification Center

The **Notification Center** keeps you informed of important system events, content updates, and pending tasks.

**Accessing Notifications:**

1. **Notification Bell Icon**
   - Located in top-right navigation
   - Red badge shows unread count
   - Click to open notification panel

2. **Notification Panel**
   - Dropdown panel appears
   - Shows most recent notifications
   - Scrollable list
   - Mark as read individually or all at once

**Notification Types:**

**🔵 Content Notifications:**
- New content published
- Content requires approval (if workflow enabled)
- Scheduled content published automatically
- Content updated by another user

**🟢 User Activity:**
- New user registered
- User role changed
- User account activated/deactivated
- New administrator added

**🟡 System Notifications:**
- System updates available
- Backup completed successfully
- Database optimization completed
- Scheduled maintenance upcoming

**🟠 Contact Notifications:**
- New contact message received
- New newsletter subscription
- Contact message marked as read/replied

**🔴 Security Alerts:**
- Multiple failed login attempts
- Password changed
- Two-factor authentication enabled/disabled
- Unusual activity detected

**📅 Event Reminders:**
- Upcoming event (24 hours before)
- Event registration deadline approaching
- Event capacity nearly full

**Notification Details:**

Each notification displays:

**Icon** - Type indicator (colored)

**Title** - Brief description

**Timestamp** - When it occurred (e.g., "5 minutes ago", "2 hours ago", "Yesterday")

**Read Status** - Unread (bold), Read (normal text)

**Action Button** - Quick link to relevant page (e.g., "View Message", "See Event", "Review User")

**Managing Notifications:**

**Mark as Read:**
- Click individual notification to mark read
- Notification stays in list but loses bold styling
- Badge count decreases

**Mark All as Read:**
- Button at top of notification panel
- Clears all unread notifications at once
- Badge disappears

**Delete Notification:**
- Hover over notification
- Click X or trash icon
- Permanently removes from list

**View All Notifications:**
- "See All" link at bottom of panel
- Opens full notification page
- Filterable by type
- Searchable
- Pagination for older notifications

**Notification Settings:**

Configure your notification preferences:

1. Navigate to **Profile** > **Notification Settings**

2. Choose notification channels:
   - ✅ In-app notifications (default, always on)
   - ☐ Email notifications
   - ☐ SMS notifications (if configured)

3. Select which events trigger notifications:
   - ☐ All content updates
   - ✅ Only content requiring my approval
   - ✅ New contact messages
   - ✅ Security alerts
   - ☐ Daily summary email
   - ☐ Weekly summary email

4. Quiet hours (optional):
   - Don't send email/SMS during specific hours
   - In-app notifications still appear when you login

**Notification Permissions by Role:**

Different roles receive different notifications:

**Super Administrator:**
- All system notifications
- Security alerts
- User management events
- All content updates

**Administrator:**
- Content in their assigned categories
- Contact messages
- User registrations
- Events

**Editor:**
- Content they created or can edit
- Comments on their content
- Approval requests for their submissions

**Author:**
- Status of their submitted content
- Comments on their articles
- Approval/rejection notifications

**Example Notification Workflow:**

**Scenario: New Contact Message**

1. Visitor submits contact form
2. System creates notification
3. Notification sent to:
   - Users with "View Contact Messages" permission
   - Super Administrators
   - Anyone subscribed to contact notifications
4. Notification appears:
   - Bell icon badge increments
   - In notification panel
   - Email sent (if enabled)
5. Administrator clicks notification
6. Redirected to contact message view
7. Notification marked as read
8. Administrator replies to message
9. New notification created: "Contact message replied"

**Troubleshooting Notifications:**

**Not Receiving Notifications:**
- Check notification settings in your profile
- Verify your role has permission to view that content type
- Check email spam folder for email notifications
- Ensure email address in profile is correct

**Too Many Notifications:**
- Adjust settings to receive only critical notifications
- Disable email notifications, keep in-app only
- Set up daily digest instead of real-time
- Use filters in full notification page

**Missed Notifications:**
- Notifications remain in system for 30 days
- View all notifications page for history
- Use search to find specific past notifications

### 13.5 Main Navigation Menu

The sidebar menu is your primary navigation tool, organized into logical sections based on functionality.

**Menu Structure:**

**💬 Communications**

- 📧 **Newsletter Subscribers** - All subscribers, export

**🔔 Notifications**
- 🔔 **Manage Notifications** - All notifications, create new
- 📬 **Notification Templates** - Email templates, SMS templates
- 📊 **Notification Logs** - Sent notifications, delivery status

**👤 User Management**
- 👥 **Users** - All users, add new
- 🛡️ **Roles & Permissions** - Manage roles, assign permissions
- 📝 **Activity Logs** - User actions, login history

**⚙️ Settings**
- 🌐 **General Settings** - Site name, logo, contact info
- 🎨 **Appearance** - Theme colors, layouts
- 🔧 **Advanced Settings** - Cache, backups, maintenance
- 📧 **Email Configuration** - SMTP settings, templates

**🔍 Monitoring & Reports**
- 📊 **Analytics** - Traffic, engagement, trends
- 📈 **Reports** - Content reports, user reports
- 🤖 **AI Assistant** - Chat with AI, content suggestions

**Menu Behavior:**

**Collapsible Sections:**
- Click section heading to expand/collapse
- Arrow indicator shows state
- Remembers your expansion preferences

**Active Page Highlight:**
- Current page highlighted in color
- Parent section auto-expands
- Easy to see where you are

**Icon Indicators:**
- Badge with count (e.g., unread messages)
- Red dot for items requiring attention
- Green checkmark for completed setup

**Responsive Menu:**
- On smaller screens, menu collapses to icons only
- Hover to see labels
- Hamburger button to show/hide

**Permission-Based Visibility:**
- Menu items only show if you have permission
- Reduces clutter
- Prevents confusion
- Authors don't see user management
- Editors don't see system settings

**Quick Search:**
- Search box at top of sidebar
- Type to filter menu items
- Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl/Cmd + K
- Finds pages by name or keyword

### 13.6 User Profile Management

Manage your administrator account details and preferences.

**Accessing Your Profile:**

1. Click your name/avatar in top-right corner
2. Select "My Profile" from dropdown
3. Or navigate to **Users** > Find yourself > Edit

**Profile Information:**

**Basic Information:**
- Full name
- Email address (login username)
- Phone number
- Job title/position
- Department
- Profile photo (optional)

**Account Settings:**
- Password (click to change)
- Two-factor authentication (enable/disable)
- Timezone preference
- Date format preference
- Language preference

**Notification Preferences:**
- Email notifications on/off
- Notification types to receive
- Quiet hours
- Digest frequency

**Session Management:**
- Active sessions list
- Device and location
- Last activity time
- Revoke session button

**Activity History:**
- Your recent actions
- Login history
- Content you created
- Content you modified

**Updating Your Profile:**

1. Navigate to My Profile
2. Click Edit button
3. Modify desired fields
4. Upload new photo (if changing)
5. Click Save Changes
6. Confirmation message appears

**Changing Your Password:**

1. Go to My Profile > Account Settings
2. Click "Change Password"
3. Enter current password
4. Enter new password (8+ characters, mix of letters, numbers, symbols)
5. Confirm new password
6. Click Update Password
7. Logged out and prompted to login with new password

**Security Best Practices:**

🔒 **Use strong, unique password**
- At least 12 characters
- Mix of uppercase, lowercase, numbers, symbols
- Not used on any other website
- Use password manager

🔒 **Enable two-factor authentication**
- Adds extra security layer
- Requires authenticator app
- Keep backup codes safe

🔒 **Review active sessions regularly**
- Revoke unknown sessions
- Logout from old devices
- Use "Logout All Other Sessions" if compromised

🔒 **Keep email address current**
- Used for password resets
- Receives security alerts
- Verify it's secure

**Profile Photo Guidelines:**

✅ **Recommended:**
- Professional headshot
- Clear, well-lit
- Neutral background
- Your face clearly visible
- Square aspect ratio (1:1)

📏 **Technical Specs:**
- Format: JPG, PNG
- Max file size: 2MB
- Recommended: 400x400 pixels minimum
- Displays as circle in most places

- Link GA account
- View GA data in dashboard
- Custom event tracking
- Goal conversions

**Custom Analytics:**
- Built-in analytics system
- Privacy-friendly (no cookies)
- GDPR compliant
- Own your data

**Generating Reports:**

1. Navigate to **Monitoring** > **Reports**
2. Select report type:
   - Traffic Report
   - Content Performance
   - User Engagement
   - SEO Health
3. Choose date range
4. Apply filters (optional)
5. View report
6. Export as PDF, CSV, or Excel
7. Schedule recurring reports (email delivery)

**Using Analytics for Decisions:**

💡 **Content Strategy:**
- Double down on popular topics
- Improve or remove low-performing content
- Publish at optimal times
- Target geographic audiences

💡 **User Experience:**
- Fix pages with high bounce rates
- Optimize for mobile (if traffic is mobile-heavy)
- Improve site speed for slow pages
- Add more content users are searching for

💡 **Event Planning:**
- Schedule events when traffic is highest
- Promote events on most visited pages
- Adjust event topics based on interests

---

## Chapter 15: Common Backend Features

*Essential tools and interfaces used throughout the admin panel*

This chapter covers features you'll encounter across multiple sections of the backend. Master these once, use them everywhere.

### 15.1 Datatables

**Datatables** are used throughout the backend to display lists of content (news, events, users, media, etc.). They provide powerful searching, filtering, and sorting capabilities.

**Standard Datatable Layout:**

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  [+ Add New]    🔍 Search...    [Filters ▼]   [Export ▼] │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ☐ │ ID │ Title          │ Author  │ Date       │ Actions │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ ☐ │ 45 │ Workshop Event │ John D  │ 2025-12-01 │ 👁️ ✏️ 🗑️  │
│ ☐ │ 44 │ Health Summit  │ Jane S  │ 2025-11-28 │ 👁️ ✏️ 🗑️  │
│ ☐ │ 43 │ Training Day   │ Mike T  │ 2025-11-25 │ 👁️ ✏️ 🗑️  │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Showing 1 to 25 of 156    [Prev] [1] [2] [3] ... [Next] │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Datatable Features:**

**1. Bulk Selection**
- Checkbox in header to select all on current page
- Individual checkboxes for each row
- Bulk actions dropdown appears when items selected:
  - Delete selected
  - Change status (publish, draft, archive)
  - Export selected
  - Assign to category

**2. Search Box**
- Searches across multiple columns
- Real-time filtering as you type
- Placeholder shows which fields are searchable
- Clear button (X) to reset search
- Example: Type "workshop" to find all items containing that word

**3. Filters**
- Click "Filters" button to show/hide filter panel
- Common filters:
  - **Status** - Published, Draft, Pending, Archived
  - **Date Range** - Pick start and end dates
  - **Author** - Filter by who created it
  - **Category** - Filter by category/type
  - **Tags** - Filter by tags (if applicable)
- Multiple filters work together (AND logic)
- "Clear Filters" button to reset all
- Active filters shown as colored badges

**4. Column Sorting**
- Click column header to sort ascending
- Click again to sort descending
- Click third time to remove sort (default order)
- Arrow indicators show sort direction (↑ ↓)
- Sortable columns indicated by cursor change on hover

**5. Pagination**
- Shows total count and current range
- Per-page selector: 10, 25, 50, 100, All
- Page number buttons
- Previous/Next buttons
- First/Last page quick links
- Keyboard navigation: ← → arrow keys

**6. Action Buttons**
- **👁️ View** - Preview/view details (opens in new tab or modal)
- **✏️ Edit** - Edit the item
- **🗑️ Delete** - Delete (confirms before deletion)
- **📋 Duplicate** - Create copy (some datatables)
- **Three-dot menu (⋮)** - More actions:
  - Change status
  - View history
  - Share
  - Export

**7. Export Options**
- Export current view or all data
- Formats: CSV, Excel, PDF
- Respects active filters
- Includes or excludes selected columns
- Filename auto-generated with timestamp

**Datatable Interactions:**

**Searching Example:**
1. Click in search box (or press Ctrl+F)
2. Type "health workshop"
3. Table instantly filters to matching rows
4. Shows "Showing 3 of 156 entries (filtered from 156 total)"
5. Clear search to see all again

**Filtering Example:**
1. Click "Filters" button
2. Status: Select "Published"
3. Date Range: Last 30 days
4. Category: Select "Events"
5. Click "Apply Filters"
6. Table shows only published events from last 30 days
7. Filter badges appear: [Published] [Last 30 days] [Events]
8. Click "Clear All Filters" to reset

**Bulk Actions Example:**
1. Check boxes next to 5 items
2. "5 items selected" message appears
3. Bulk actions dropdown becomes active
4. Select "Change Status" > "Archive"
5. Confirmation dialog: "Archive 5 items?"
6. Click "Yes, Archive"
7. Items moved to archive, success message appears
8. Checkboxes automatically unchecked

**Responsive Behavior:**

On smaller screens, datatables adapt:
- Fewer columns visible by default
- Horizontal scroll for more columns
- Action buttons collapse to three-dot menu
- Filters appear as drawer from bottom
- Pagination simplified

**Datatable Customization:**

**Column Visibility:**
- Click "Columns" button (if available)
- Toggle which columns to show/hide
- Saves your preference

**Column Reordering:**
- Drag column headers to reorder (some tables)
- Your layout is saved

**Saved Views (Advanced):**
- Save your filter/sort combinations
- Name the view (e.g., "Recent Published Events")
- Quick switch between saved views
- Share views with team

**Common Datatables in AHC:**

Each content type has its own datatable with specialized columns:

**News Datatable:**
- Columns: ID, Title, Category, Author, Featured Image, Status, Published Date, Views, Actions
- Filters: Status, Category, Author, Date Range, Tags

**Events Datatable:**
- Columns: ID, Event Name, Category, Start Date, Location, Attendees, Status, Actions
- Filters: Status, Category, Date Range, Location

**Users Datatable:**
- Columns: ID, Name, Email, Role, Status, Last Login, Registered Date, Actions
- Filters: Role, Status, Registration Date

**Media Datatable:**
- Columns: Thumbnail, Filename, Type, Size, Uploaded By, Upload Date, Actions
- Filters: File Type, Uploaded By, Date Range, Album

**Resources Datatable:**
- Columns: ID, Title, Category, File Type, Downloads, Uploaded, Status, Actions
- Filters: Status, Category, File Type, Date Range

**AHC Leaders & Teams Datatable:**
- Columns: Photo, Name, Position, Type (Leader/Team), LinkedIn, Order, Status, Actions
- Filters: Type, Status

**Partners Datatable:**
- Columns: Logo, Partner Name, Type, Website, Status, Featured, Actions
- Filters: Type, Status, Featured

**Contact Messages Datatable:**
- Columns: ID, Name, Email, Subject, Status (New/Read/Replied), Date, Actions
- Filters: Status, Date Range

**Performance Tips:**

💡 **Large Datasets:**
- Use filters to narrow results before searching
- Export in batches rather than all at once
- Adjust per-page count for faster loading

💡 **Finding Items:**
- Use ID number if you know it (fastest)
- Combine multiple filters for precision
- Sort by date to find recent items
- Use saved views for common searches

### 15.2 Rich Text Editor (WYSIWYG)

The **Rich Text Editor** is used for creating and editing formatted content (news articles, event descriptions, page content, etc.).

**Editor Interface:**

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ [B] [I] [U] | [H1▼] | [≡] [≡] [≡] | [🔗] [🖼️] | [</>] [...] │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                           │
│  Type your content here...                               │
│                                                           │
│  • Use the toolbar for formatting                        │
│  • Insert images, links, and more                        │
│                                                           │
│                                                           │
│                                                           │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  Word count: 156  |  Character count: 842
```

**Toolbar Buttons:**

**Text Formatting:**
- **B** - Bold (Ctrl+B)
- **I** - Italic (Ctrl+I)
- **U** - Underline (Ctrl+U)
- **S** - Strikethrough
- **A** - Text color picker
- **🎨** - Background highlight color
- **TT** - Monospace/code font
- **x₂** - Subscript
- **x²** - Superscript
- **Tx** - Clear formatting

**Paragraph Formatting:**
- **H1 ▼** - Heading level dropdown
  - Paragraph (normal text)
  - Heading 1 (largest)
  - Heading 2
  - Heading 3
  - Heading 4
  - Heading 5
  - Heading 6 (smallest)
- **≡** - Align left
- **≡** - Align center
- **≡** - Align right
- **≡** - Justify
- **→** - Increase indent
- **←** - Decrease indent

**Lists:**
- **• Bulleted list** - Unordered list
- **1. Numbered list** - Ordered list
- **☐ Checklist** - Task list with checkboxes

**Insert Elements:**
- **🔗 Link** - Insert/edit hyperlink
  - Enter URL
  - Choose: Open in new tab
  - Enter link text
  - Choose: NoFollow (SEO)
- **🖼️ Image** - Insert image
  - Upload new image
  - Select from media library
  - Enter image URL
  - Alt text (accessibility)
  - Alignment (left, center, right, inline)
  - Size (width/height or percentage)
  - Caption (optional)
  - Link image to URL (optional)
- **📹 Video** - Embed video
  - YouTube URL (auto-embeds)
  - Vimeo URL
  - Custom embed code
  - Responsive sizing
- **📊 Table** - Insert table
  - Choose rows × columns
  - Header row option
  - Border styling
  - Cell editing
  - Add/delete rows/columns
  - Merge/split cells
- **➖ Horizontal Rule** - Divider line
- **😀 Emoji** - Emoji picker
- **🔣 Special Characters** - Insert symbols (©, ®, €, etc.)

**Advanced Features:**

- **</>  Source Code** - View/edit HTML source
  - For advanced users
  - Make complex edits
  - Fix formatting issues
  - Paste HTML from other sources

- **[...] More Options**
  - **📋 Paste as Plain Text** - Remove formatting when pasting
  - **🧹 Clear Formatting** - Remove all formatting from selection
  - **⤺ Undo** - Undo last action (Ctrl+Z)
  - **⤻ Redo** - Redo (Ctrl+Y)
  - **✂️ Cut** - (Ctrl+X)
  - **📄 Copy** - (Ctrl+C)
  - **📋 Paste** - (Ctrl+V)
  - **🔍 Find & Replace** - Search within content
  - **📏 Word Count** - Detailed statistics
  - **🖨️ Print** - Print content
  - **⛶ Full Screen** - Distraction-free writing mode (Esc to exit)

**Working with Images:**

**Inserting Image:**
1. Click Image button (🖼️) or drag-and-drop image into editor
2. Choose method:
   - **Upload** - Select file from your computer (JPG, PNG, GIF, WebP)
   - **Media Library** - Choose from previously uploaded images
   - **URL** - Enter direct image URL
3. Wait for upload progress bar
4. Image appears in editor

**Editing Image:**
1. Click on image in editor
2. Resize handles appear at corners
3. Drag to resize (maintains aspect ratio)
4. Toolbar appears:
   - 📐 Edit (change source, alt text, etc.)
   - ⬅️ Align left (text wraps right)
   - ➡️ Align right (text wraps left)
   - ⬆️ Align center (own line)
   - 🔗 Link image to URL
   - 🗑️ Remove image
5. Right-click for more options

**Image Best Practices:**

✅ **Always add Alt Text**
- Describes image for screen readers
- Shows if image fails to load
- Helps SEO
- Example: "AHC workshop participants in conference room"

✅ **Optimize Before Upload:**
- Compress images (tools: TinyPNG, ImageOptim)
- Target file size < 200KB for web
- Use appropriate format:
  - JPG for photos
  - PNG for graphics/logos with transparency
  - WebP for best compression (if supported)
  - Avoid BMP, TIFF (too large)

✅ **Responsive Sizing:**
- Use percentage width (e.g., 100%) rather than fixed pixels
- Let height adjust automatically
- Test on mobile preview

**Working with Links:**
