## Chapter 23: Roles & Permissions

### 23.1 Overview

The Roles & Permissions system provides granular control over what users can do in the AHC admin panel. Instead of manually assigning dozens of permissions to each user, you assign them a **role** (like "Editor"), and the role determines their permissions.

**Key Concepts:**

- **Permission:** A specific action (e.g., "Edit News Articles")
- **Role:** A named collection of permissions (e.g., "Editor" role has 15 permissions)
- **Role Assignment:** Connecting a user to a role
- **Custom Roles:** Creating new roles beyond the defaults

**Benefits:**
- Consistency (all Editors have same permissions)
- Easy management (change role, all users updated)
- Security (principle of least privilege)
- Scalability (add new users quickly)
- Auditability (clear permission structure)

**Access:**
```
Admin Panel → System → Roles & Permissions
```

**Required Permission:** Manage Roles (Super Admin only)

### 23.2 Default Roles

The system comes with pre-configured roles:

**1. Super Admin**

**Description:** Full system access, highest authority

**Typical Users:** Director, IT Manager

**Permissions (all enabled):**
```
Content Management:
  ✅ Manage News & Announcements (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Events (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Dynamic Pages (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Resources (Create, Read, Update, Delete)

Community & Partners:
  ✅ Manage AHC Leaders & Teams (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Partners (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Media Gallery (Create, Read, Update, Delete)

Communications:
  ✅ Manage Contact Messages (Read, Reply, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Newsletter Subscribers (Create, Read, Update, Delete, Export)

System Administration:
  ✅ Manage Users (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Roles & Permissions (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ System Settings (View, Update)
  ✅ View Analytics & Reports
  ✅ View Activity Logs
  ✅ System Backup & Restore
```

**Cannot Do:** N/A (full access)

---

**2. Admin**

**Description:** High-level access, cannot modify system settings

**Typical Users:** Deputy Director, Senior Managers

**Permissions:**
```
Content Management:
  ✅ All content permissions (News, Events, Pages, Resources)

Community & Partners:
  ✅ All AHC Leaders, Partners, Media permissions

Communications:
  ✅ All Contact Messages, Newsletter permissions

User Management:
  ✅ Manage Users (Create, Read, Update)
  ✅ View Roles & Permissions
  ❌ Cannot edit roles/permissions
  ❌ Cannot delete Super Admins

System:
  ✅ View Analytics & Reports
  ✅ View Activity Logs
  ❌ Cannot modify System Settings
  ❌ Cannot access Backup/Restore
```

**Cannot Do:**
- Modify system-wide settings
- Create/edit/delete roles
- Delete Super Admin users
- Access backup/restore functions

---

**3. Editor**

**Description:** Content creation and management focus

**Typical Users:** Communications Officer, Content Manager

**Permissions:**
```
Content Management:
  ✅ Manage News & Announcements (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Events (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Dynamic Pages (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Resources (Create, Read, Update, Delete)

Community & Partners:
  ✅ Manage AHC Leaders & Teams (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Partners (Create, Read, Update, Delete)
  ✅ Manage Media Gallery (Create, Read, Update, Delete)

Communications:
  ✅ Manage Contact Messages (Read, Reply)
  ❌ Cannot delete contact messages
  ✅ View Newsletter Subscribers
  ❌ Cannot export or delete subscribers

System:
  ✅ View own profile and activity
  ❌ Cannot manage other users
  ❌ Cannot access system settings
  ❌ Cannot view full analytics
```

**Cannot Do:**
- Manage users or roles
- Modify system settings
- Delete contact messages
- Export newsletter subscribers
- View detailed analytics

---

**4. Moderator**

**Description:** Content review and message management

**Typical Users:** Junior Staff, Interns, Support Staff

**Permissions:**
```
Content Management:
  ✅ View News & Announcements (Read only)
  ✅ View Events (Read only)
  ✅ Edit existing content (with approval)
  ❌ Cannot create new content
  ❌ Cannot delete content
  ❌ Cannot publish/unpublish

Community & Partners:
  ✅ View AHC Leaders & Teams (Read only)
  ✅ View Partners (Read only)
  ✅ View Media Gallery (Read only)
  ❌ Cannot modify

Communications:
  ✅ Manage Contact Messages (Read, Reply, Mark as Replied)
  ❌ Cannot delete messages
  ✅ Manage Newsletter Subscribers (Read, Update status)
  ❌ Cannot delete subscribers

System:
  ✅ View own profile
  ❌ No access to user management
  ❌ No access to system settings
  ❌ No access to analytics
```

**Cannot Do:**
- Create or delete any content
- Publish/unpublish content
- Manage users
- Access analytics or system settings

---

**5. Viewer**

**Description:** Read-only access for reporting and oversight

**Typical Users:** Board Members, Consultants, Auditors

**Permissions:**
```
Content Management:
  ✅ View all content (Read only)
  ❌ Cannot edit, create, or delete

Community & Partners:
  ✅ View all data (Read only)

Communications:
  ✅ View Contact Messages (Read only)
  ✅ View Newsletter Subscribers (Read only)
  ❌ Cannot reply or modify

System:
  ✅ View Dashboard
  ✅ View Analytics & Reports (summary level)
  ✅ View own profile
  ❌ Cannot manage users
  ❌ Cannot modify anything
```

**Cannot Do:**
- Modify any data
- Create, edit, or delete content
- Manage communications
- Access user management or system settings

### 23.3 Viewing Roles

**Roles List Page:**

1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. View all roles in table:

| Role Name | Description | Users | Permissions | Actions |
|-----------|-------------|-------|-------------|---------|
| Super Admin | Full system access | 2 | All (45) | View / Edit |
| Admin | High-level access | 3 | 38 of 45 | View / Edit / Delete |
| Editor | Content management | 5 | 25 of 45 | View / Edit / Delete |
| Moderator | Review and moderation | 8 | 12 of 45 | View / Edit / Delete |
| Viewer | Read-only access | 4 | 8 of 45 | View / Edit / Delete |

**Viewing Role Details:**

1. Click "View" action on any role
2. See detailed permission breakdown:

```
Role: Editor
Description: Content creation and management
Users: 5 active users
Created: 2023-05-15
Last Modified: 2025-11-20

Permissions (25 of 45):

Content Management [All Selected]
  ✅ News: Create, Read, Update, Delete
  ✅ Events: Create, Read, Update, Delete
  ✅ Dynamic Pages: Create, Read, Update, Delete
  ✅ Resources: Create, Read, Update, Delete

Community & Partners [All Selected]
  ✅ AHC Leaders & Teams: All
  ✅ Partners: All
  ✅ Media Gallery: All

Communications [Partial]
  ✅ Contact Messages: Read, Reply
  ❌ Contact Messages: Delete
  ✅ Newsletter: View
  ❌ Newsletter: Export, Delete

User Management [None]
  ❌ All permissions disabled

System [None]
  ❌ All permissions disabled

Analytics & Reports [Partial]
  ✅ View Dashboard
  ❌ Detailed Analytics
```

### 23.4 Creating Custom Roles

**When to Create Custom Roles:**
- Default roles don't fit specific job function
- Need unique permission combination
- Temporary project with specific access needs
- Partner organization collaborator
- Intern with limited, specific responsibilities

**Example Scenarios:**

**Scenario 1: Event Coordinator Role**
- Needs: Full event management, limited news editing
- Doesn't need: AHC Leaders, Partners, User Management

**Scenario 2: Social Media Manager Role**
- Needs: Create news, upload media, read contact messages
- Doesn't need: Delete anything, user management

**Creating Custom Role:**

1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. Click "Create New Role" button
3. Fill in role details:

**Basic Information:**
```
Role Name: [text input]
  └─ Example: Event Coordinator

Display Name: [text input]
  └─ Example: Event Coordinator

Description: [textarea]
  └─ Example: Manages all aspects of AHC events, from creation to post-event reporting. Limited news editing for event announcements.
```

**Select Permissions:**

Organize permissions by category with checkboxes:

```
Content Management
  News & Announcements:
    ☐ Create News
    ☐ Read News
    ☐ Update News
    ☐ Delete News
    ☐ Publish/Unpublish News

  Events:
    ☑ Create Events
    ☑ Read Events
    ☑ Update Events
    ☑ Delete Events
    ☑ Publish/Unpublish Events
    ☑ Export Event Attendees

  Dynamic Pages:
    ☐ Create Pages
    ☑ Read Pages
    ☐ Update Pages
    ☐ Delete Pages

  Resources:
    ☐ All (none selected for this role)

Community & Partners
  AHC Leaders & Teams:
    ☑ Read Leaders & Teams
    ☐ Create/Update/Delete (not needed)

  Partners:
    ☑ Read Partners
    ☐ Create/Update/Delete

  Media Gallery:
    ☑ Create Media
    ☑ Read Media
    ☑ Update Media
    ☑ Delete Media

Communications
  Contact Messages:
    ☑ Read Messages
    ☑ Reply to Messages
    ☐ Delete Messages

  Newsletter Subscribers:
    ☑ Read Subscribers
    ☑ Export Subscribers (for event invitations)
    ☐ Delete Subscribers

System
  User Management:
    ☐ All (none selected)

  System Settings:
    ☐ All (none selected)

  Analytics & Reports:
    ☑ View Dashboard
    ☑ View Event Analytics
    ☐ Full System Analytics
```

4. Preview permissions summary
5. Click "Create Role"
6. Role now available for user assignment

**Permission Selection Tips:**

✅ **Group Related Permissions:**
- If giving "Update" permission, usually need "Read" too
- Delete permission is powerful - use sparingly
- Publish/Unpublish affects public website - control carefully

✅ **Test Permissions:**
- Create test user with new role
- Try various actions
- Verify permissions work as intended
- Adjust if needed

✅ **Document Purpose:**
- Write clear description
- Explain why role exists
- List typical users who should have this role
- Note any special considerations

### 23.5 Editing Roles

**When to Edit Roles:**
- Job responsibilities changed
- Security requirements updated
- Grant new feature access
- Remove outdated permissions
- Fix permission errors

**Steps:**

1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. Find role to edit
3. Click "Edit" action
4. Modify:
   - Role name/description
   - Permissions (check/uncheck boxes)
5. Review changes
6. Click "Update Role"
7. **Important:** Changes affect ALL users with this role immediately

**Example: Granting Newsletter Export to Editors**

**Current State:**
- Editors can view newsletter subscribers
- Editors cannot export subscriber list
- Request: Editors need export for event promotions

**Process:**
1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. Find "Editor" role
3. Click "Edit"
4. Scroll to Communications → Newsletter Subscribers
5. Current permissions:
   ☑ Read Subscribers
   ☐ Export Subscribers ← Currently unchecked
6. Check the box: ☑ Export Subscribers
7. Click "Update Role"
8. Success message: "Role updated. 5 users affected."
9. All 5 Editors can now export subscribers

**Warning When Editing:**
```
⚠️ Warning: You are editing the "Editor" role.
   This change will affect 5 active users immediately.

   Users affected:
   - Sarah Johnson (sarah.johnson@ahc.org)
   - Michael Tesfaye (michael.t@ahc.org)
   - Fatuma Ahmed (fatuma.a@ahc.org)
   - David Getachew (david.g@ahc.org)
   - Rebecca Yohannes (rebecca.y@ahc.org)

   Are you sure you want to proceed? [Cancel] [Update Role]
```

### 23.6 Deleting Roles

**When to Delete:**
- Custom role no longer needed
- Project completed
- Role consolidated with another
- Cleanup of unused roles

**Cannot Delete:**
- Default system roles (Super Admin, Admin, Editor, Moderator, Viewer)
- Roles currently assigned to users

**Before Deleting:**

1. Check role usage:
   ```
   Role: Event Coordinator
   Users: 2 active users
   
   Action Required: Reassign users before deleting
   ```

2. Reassign all users:
   - Find all users with this role
   - Assign them to different role
   - Verify no users remain

3. Delete role:
   - Click "Delete" action
   - Confirm deletion
   - Role removed from system

**Deletion Process:**

1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. Find custom role to delete
3. Click "Delete" action
4. Confirmation dialog:
   ```
   Delete Role: Event Coordinator?

   This role has 2 active users. You must reassign them first.

   [View Users] [Cancel]
   ```
5. Click "View Users"
6. For each user:
   - Click "Edit User"
   - Change Role dropdown to different role
   - Click "Update User"
7. Return to Roles & Permissions
8. Click "Delete" on Event Coordinator role
9. New confirmation:
   ```
   Delete Role: Event Coordinator?

   This role has 0 users. Safe to delete.

   Are you sure? This cannot be undone. [Cancel] [Delete]
   ```
10. Click "Delete"
11. Role removed

### 23.7 Assigning Roles to Users

**Three Methods:**

**Method 1: During User Creation**
1. Create new user (see Chapter 22)
2. Select role from dropdown
3. User created with that role

**Method 2: Edit Existing User**
1. Navigate to User Management
2. Find user
3. Click "Edit"
4. Change "Role" dropdown
5. Click "Update User"
6. New permissions applied immediately

**Method 3: Bulk Role Assignment**
1. Navigate to User Management
2. Select multiple users (checkboxes)
3. Click "Bulk Actions" → "Change Role"
4. Select new role
5. Confirm change
6. All selected users updated

**Permission Changes Take Effect:**
- Immediately for new actions
- Active sessions: Some changes apply on next page load, others require logout/login
- Best practice: Notify users when their role/permissions change

### 23.8 Permission Inheritance

Some systems support **permission inheritance** where roles can inherit permissions from other roles.

**Example Hierarchy:**
```
Super Admin (all permissions)
  ↓ inherits down
Admin (most permissions)
  ↓ inherits down
Editor (content permissions)
  ↓ inherits down
Moderator (basic permissions)
  ↓ inherits down
Viewer (read-only)
```

**How It Works:**
- Viewer role: 8 base permissions
- Moderator role: Inherits Viewer's 8 + adds 4 more = 12 total
- Editor role: Inherits Moderator's 12 + adds 13 more = 25 total
- Admin role: Inherits Editor's 25 + adds 13 more = 38 total
- Super Admin: All 45 permissions

**Benefits:**
- Easier to maintain (change Viewer, affects all higher roles)
- Logical permission progression
- Prevents permission gaps

**If Your System Doesn't Support Inheritance:**
- Manually ensure higher roles have all lower role permissions
- Regular audits to maintain consistency

### 23.9 Special Permissions

Some permissions have special behaviors:

**Bypass Permissions:**
- **Bypass Content Approval:** Publish without review
- **Bypass Draft Status:** Content goes live immediately
- **Bypass Delete Protection:** Delete protected items

**Ownership Permissions:**
- **Edit Own Content Only:** Can only edit items they created
- **Delete Own Content Only:** Can only delete their items
- **View All vs. View Own:** See everyone's content vs. only own

**Time-Limited Permissions:**
- Grant permission for specific time period
- Auto-expires after date
- Useful for temporary contractors

**IP-Restricted Permissions:**
- Only allow from office IP addresses
- Enhanced security for sensitive permissions
- Block access from public networks

**Configuring Special Permissions:**

If available in your system:
1. Edit role
2. Expand "Advanced Permissions"
3. Configure special rules:
   ```
   Content Ownership Rules:
     ☑ Users can only edit their own content
     ☐ Users can edit all content
   
   Content Approval:
     ☑ Require approval before publishing
     ☐ Bypass approval (publish immediately)
   
   Delete Protection:
     ☑ Cannot delete content older than 30 days
     ☑ Cannot delete content by other users
   
   Time Restrictions:
     ☐ Enable time-limited access
     Start Date: [date picker]
     End Date: [date picker]
   
   IP Restrictions:
     ☐ Restrict to specific IP addresses
     Allowed IPs: [text input]
     Example: 192.168.1.0/24, 10.0.0.50
   ```

### 23.10 Best Practices

✅ **Start Restrictive:**
- Give minimum permissions needed
- Users can request more if needed
- Easier to add than remove permissions

✅ **Regular Reviews:**
- Quarterly: Review all roles and permissions
- Verify permissions still appropriate
- Remove unused custom roles
- Update descriptions to match current responsibilities

✅ **Naming Conventions:**
- Use clear, descriptive role names
- Avoid abbreviations
- Match organizational job titles when possible
- Examples: "Event Coordinator" not "EvtCoord"

✅ **Documentation:**
- Document each role's purpose
- List typical users for each role
- Note any special considerations
- Keep permission matrix updated

✅ **Testing:**
- Test new roles with test user account
- Verify permissions work as intended
- Check both what they CAN do and what they CAN'T do
- Test edge cases

✅ **Communication:**
- Notify users when roles change
- Explain new permissions (or removed ones)
- Provide training if significant changes
- Document changes in activity log

✅ **Security:**
- Protect sensitive permissions (Delete, User Management, System Settings)
- Limit number of Super Admins (2-3 maximum)
- Regular permission audits
- Monitor permission changes in activity logs
- Implement role-based approval workflows

### 23.11 Common Workflows

**Workflow 1: Creating Social Media Manager Role**

**Request:** Need role for social media staff to create news posts and manage media, but no delete permissions.

**Analysis:**
- Needs: Create/Edit News, Upload/Edit Media
- Doesn't Need: Delete anything, Events, User Management

**Steps:**
1. Navigate to Roles & Permissions
2. Click "Create New Role"
3. Fill in:
   - Name: "Social Media Manager"
   - Description: "Creates and edits news content and media for social media channels. Cannot delete content."
4. Select permissions:
   ```
   Content Management:
     News & Announcements:
       ☑ Create News
       ☑ Read News
       ☑ Update News
       ☐ Delete News
       ☑ Publish News
     
     Events: ☐ All (not needed)
     Pages: ☐ All (not needed)
     Resources: ☐ All (not needed)
   
   Media Gallery:
     ☑ Create Media
     ☑ Read Media
     ☑ Update Media
     ☐ Delete Media
   
   Communications:
     Contact Messages:
       ☑ Read
       ☐ Reply, Delete
     
     Newsletter: ☐ All (not needed)
   
   System: ☐ All (not needed)
   ```
5. Click "Create Role"
6. Test with test user
7. Verify: Can create news, upload media, but cannot delete
8. Assign to social media staff

**Workflow 2: Temporary Consultant Access**

**Scenario:** External health policy consultant needs 3-month access to review content and provide feedback, but not edit.

**Requirements:**
- Read-only access to all content
- Access only from Dec 1, 2025 to Feb 28, 2026
- Cannot modify or delete anything
- Can add comments/notes

**Solution 1: Use Existing "Viewer" Role**
1. Create user account for consultant
2. Assign "Viewer" role
3. Set account expiration: Feb 28, 2026
4. Status: Active
5. Consultant can login and view, but not edit
6. Account auto-deactivates March 1

**Solution 2: Create Custom "Consultant" Role** (if need commenting)
1. Create new role: "External Consultant"
2. Permissions:
   - Read all content: ✅
   - Add comments/notes: ✅
   - Edit/Delete: ❌
3. Create user, assign this role
4. Set expiration date
5. After project, delete role

**Workflow 3: Promoting User from Moderator to Editor**

**Scenario:** Moderator performed well, getting promoted to Editor

**Current State:**
- User: Jane Smith
- Role: Moderator
- Permissions: Read content, reply to messages

**New State:**
- Role: Editor
- Permissions: Full content management

**Process:**
1. Navigate to User Management
2. Find Jane Smith
3. Click "Edit"
4. Change Role dropdown: Moderator → Editor
5. Click "Update User"
6. Success message: "Jane Smith updated. Role changed from Moderator to Editor."
7. Jane immediately has Editor permissions
8. Send notification email:
   ```
   Subject: Your Account Permissions Updated

   Dear Jane,

   Congratulations! Your role has been updated to Editor.

   You now have the following new capabilities:
   - Create and publish news articles
   - Manage events
   - Upload and manage media
   - Full content management

   Your login credentials remain the same.

   If you have questions about your new permissions, please contact: admin@ahc.org

   Best regards,
   AHC Admin Team
   ```
9. Schedule training on new features (if needed)
10. Monitor Jane's first week with new permissions

**Workflow 4: Annual Permission Audit**

**Frequency:** Every December

**Steps:**

1. **Generate Current State Report:**
   - Navigate to Roles & Permissions
   - Click "Export Role Matrix"
   - Spreadsheet shows all roles and permissions

2. **Review Each Role:**
   - Super Admin: 2 users (Director, IT Manager) ✅ Correct
   - Admin: 4 users - verify all should be Admins
   - Editor: 6 users - verify all still in content roles
   - Moderator: 8 users - some may need promotion
   - Viewer: 5 users - check if still need access
   - Custom "Event Coordinator": 1 user - still needed? ✅ Yes

3. **Review Each User:**
   - Export user list with roles
   - Compare to current HR roster
   - Identify mismatches:
     - John Doe: Role=Editor, Current Job=Manager → Should be Admin
     - Sarah Kim: Role=Moderator, Current Job=Senior Officer → Should be Editor
     - Mike Chen: Role=Viewer, Left Organization Aug 2025 → Delete account

4. **Make Adjustments:**
   - Promote: John to Admin, Sarah to Editor
   - Delete: Mike's account
   - Update 3 other users

5. **Review Permissions:**
   - Are any permissions too broad?
   - Are any too restrictive (causing workarounds)?
   - Do custom roles still make sense?
   - Any new permissions needed for new features?

6. **Document Findings:**
   ```
   2025 Annual Permission Audit - Summary

   Date: December 15, 2025
   Conducted by: IT Manager

   Findings:
   - Total Users: 22 (down from 26 last year)
   - Role Distribution:
     - Super Admin: 2
     - Admin: 4 (was 3, promoted 1)
     - Editor: 7 (was 6, promoted 1)
     - Moderator: 7 (was 8, promoted 1)
     - Viewer: 2 (was 5, removed 3 inactive)
   
   Changes Made:
   - Promoted 2 users (performance-based)
   - Deleted 4 accounts (left organization)
   - Updated 3 user details
   - No role/permission changes needed
   
   Recommendations:
   - Continue quarterly mini-reviews
   - Implement 2FA for all Admins (security)
   - Create training materials for new Editors
   
   Next Audit: December 2026
   ```

7. **Communicate Changes:**
   - Email all affected users
   - Update documentation
   - Brief management on findings

---

## Chapter 24: Settings

### 24.1 Overview

The Settings module controls system-wide configurations for the AHC admin panel and frontend website. This includes general information, appearance customization, email configuration, and advanced technical settings.

**Access:**
```
Admin Panel → System → Settings
```

**Required Permission:** Manage System Settings (Super Admin only)

**Settings Categories:**
1. General Settings
2. Appearance Settings
3. Email Settings
4. Advanced Settings

**Important:** Settings changes affect the entire system and all users. Make changes carefully and test thoroughly.

### 24.2 General Settings

**Site Information:**

```
Site Name: [text input]
  └─ African Health Catalysts (AHC)

Tagline: [text input]
  └─ Transforming Health in Africa

Site Description: [textarea]
  └─ AHC is a leading organization dedicated to improving health outcomes across Africa through research, advocacy, and partnership...

Administrator Email: [email input]
  └─ admin@ahc.org
  └─ Receives system notifications and contact form submissions
```

**Contact Information:**

```
Organization Phone: [text input]
  └─ +251 11 123 4567

Organization Email: [email input]
  └─ info@ahc.org

Physical Address: [textarea]
  └─ Example:
      African Health Catalysts
      Bole Sub-City, Woreda 03
      Addis Ababa, Ethiopia
      P.O. Box 12345

Office Hours: [text input]
  └─ Monday - Friday, 8:00 AM - 5:00 PM EAT
```

**Social Media Links:**

```
Facebook URL: [URL input]
  └─ https://facebook.com/AfricanHealthCatalysts

Twitter/X URL: [URL input]
  └─ https://twitter.com/AHC_Official

LinkedIn URL: [URL input]
  └─ https://linkedin.com/company/african-health-catalysts

Instagram URL: [URL input]
  └─ https://instagram.com/ahc_official

YouTube URL: [URL input]
  └─ https://youtube.com/@AfricanHealthCatalysts
```

**Timezone Settings:**

```
Timezone: [dropdown]
  └─ Africa/Addis_Ababa (EAT - UTC+3)
  
  Options:
  - Africa/Addis_Ababa (Ethiopia)
  - Africa/Nairobi (Kenya)
  - Africa/Cairo (Egypt)
  - UTC
  - Other...

Date Format: [dropdown]
  └─ d/m/Y (10/12/2025)
  └─ m/d/Y (12/10/2025)
  └─ Y-m-d (2025-12-10)

Time Format: [dropdown]
  └─ 12-hour (2:30 PM)
  └─ 24-hour (14:30)
```

**Saving General Settings:**
1. Make desired changes
2. Click "Save General Settings" button
3. Success message: "General settings updated successfully"
4. Changes reflected immediately across admin panel and frontend

**Logo & Branding:**

```
Site Logo: [image upload]
```

### 24.4 Email Settings

Configure email functionality for system notifications, contact form responses, and newsletters.

**SMTP Configuration:**

```
Mail Driver: [dropdown]
  ├─ SMTP (recommended for reliability)
  ├─ SendGrid (if using SendGrid service)
  ├─ Mailgun (if using Mailgun service)
  └─ PHP Mail (not recommended for production)

SMTP Host: [text input]
  └─ Example: smtp.gmail.com
  └─ Example: smtp.office365.com
  └─ Example: mail.ahc.org

SMTP Port: [number input]
  ├─ 587 (TLS - recommended)
  ├─ 465 (SSL)
  └─ 25 (unencrypted - not recommended)

SMTP Encryption: [dropdown]
  ├─ TLS (recommended)
  ├─ SSL
  └─ None (not secure)

SMTP Username: [text input]
  └─ Your email address or SMTP username
  └─ Example: notifications@ahc.org

SMTP Password: [password input]
  └─ Your email password or app-specific password
  └─ Stored encrypted
```

**From Address:**

```
From Name: [text input]
  └─ African Health Catalysts
  └─ Appears as sender name in emails

From Email: [email input]
  └─ noreply@ahc.org
  └─ Must be valid email address
  └─ Should match SMTP account

Reply-To Email: [email input]
  └─ info@ahc.org
  └─ Where recipients can reply
```

**Email Templates:**

Configure automated email content:

```
Contact Form Notification Email:
  Subject: [text input]
    └─ New Contact Message Received

  Body: [rich text editor]
    └─ Template with variables:
        Hello Admin,

        A new contact message has been received:

        From: {sender_name} ({sender_email})
        Subject: {message_subject}
        Message: {message_content}

        View in admin panel: {admin_url}

Welcome Email (New User):
  Subject: Welcome to AHC Admin Panel

  Body: [customizable template]

Password Reset Email:
  Subject: Password Reset Request

  Body: [customizable template]

Newsletter Confirmation:
  Subject: Confirm Your AHC Newsletter Subscription

  Body: [customizable template with opt-in link]
```

**Testing Email Configuration:**

1. Configure SMTP settings
2. Click "Send Test Email" button
3. Enter test recipient email
4. Click "Send"
5. Check if email received
6. If successful: "Test email sent successfully!"
7. If failed: Check error message, verify settings

**Common SMTP Settings:**

**Gmail:**
```
Host: smtp.gmail.com
Port: 587
Encryption: TLS
Username: your-email@gmail.com
Password: App-specific password (not regular Gmail password)

Note: Enable "Less secure app access" or use App Password
```

**Office 365 / Outlook:**
```
Host: smtp.office365.com
Port: 587
Encryption: TLS
Username: your-email@organization.com
Password: Your Office 365 password
```

**Custom Domain (cPanel/WHM):**
```
Host: mail.yourdomain.com
Port: 587
Encryption: TLS
Username: Full email address
Password: Email account password
```

### 24.5 Advanced Settings

**Performance & Caching:**

```
Enable Caching: [toggle]
  └─ ● Enabled (recommended for production)
  └─ Caches database queries and page content
  └─ Improves performance

Cache Duration: [number input]
  └─ 3600 seconds (1 hour)
  └─ How long to keep cached data

Clear Cache: [button]
  └─ Click to clear all cached data
  └─ Use after major content updates
```

**Security Settings:**

```
Enable Two-Factor Authentication: [toggle]
  └─ ○ Disabled
  └─ ● Enabled (highly recommended)
  └─ Requires users to enter code from phone app

Session Timeout: [number input]
  └─ 120 minutes
  └─ Users auto-logged out after this period of inactivity

Max Login Attempts: [number input]
  └─ 5 attempts
  └─ Account locked after this many failed logins

Lockout Duration: [number input]
  └─ 30 minutes
  └─ How long account stays locked

IP Whitelist: [textarea] (optional)
  └─ Restrict admin panel access to specific IP addresses
  └─ One IP or IP range per line
  └─ Example:
      192.168.1.0/24
      10.0.0.50
      41.202.xxx.xxx
```

**Maintenance Mode:**

```
Enable Maintenance Mode: [toggle]
  └─ ○ Disabled (site operational)
  └─ ● Enabled (site shows maintenance page)

Maintenance Message: [textarea]
  └─ We're currently performing scheduled maintenance.
      The site will be back online shortly.
      Thank you for your patience.

Allowed IPs During Maintenance: [textarea]
  └─ IP addresses that can access site during maintenance
  └─ Typically: Admin/developer IPs
  └─ Example:
      192.168.1.50 (Office)
      41.xxx.xxx.xxx (IT Manager home)
```

**Backup & Restore:**

```
Automatic Backups: [toggle]
  └─ ● Enabled

Backup Frequency: [dropdown]
  ├─ Daily (recommended)
  ├─ Weekly
  └─ Monthly

Backup Storage: [dropdown]
  ├─ Local Server
  ├─ Cloud Storage (Google Drive, Dropbox)
  └─ FTP Server

Backup Retention: [number input]
  └─ Keep last 30 backups
  └─ Older backups automatically deleted

Manual Backup: [button]
  └─ Click "Create Backup Now"
  └─ Downloads .zip file with database + uploads

Restore from Backup: [file upload]
  └─ Upload previous backup .zip file
  └─ ⚠️ Warning: This will overwrite current data
```

**API Settings:**

```
Enable API: [toggle]
  └─ ● Enabled
  └─ Allows frontend to fetch data

API Rate Limiting: [number input]
  └─ 100 requests per minute per IP
  └─ Prevents abuse

API Key: [text display]
  └─ abc123xyz789... (auto-generated)
  └─ [Regenerate] button to create new key
  └─ Use in API requests for authentication
```

**Language Settings:**

```
Default Language: [dropdown]
  ├─ ● English
  └─ ○ Amharic

Available Languages: [checkboxes]
  ☑ English
  ☑ Amharic
  ☐ French
  ☐ Arabic

Note: Frontend content is managed in backend (single language)
Backend UI can be in English or Amharic based on user preference
```

### 24.6 Saving and Resetting Settings

**Saving Settings:**

Each settings section has its own save button:
- "Save General Settings"
- "Save Appearance Settings"
- "Save Email Settings"
- "Save Advanced Settings"

**Important:** Always click the appropriate save button after making changes, or changes will be lost.

**Reset to Defaults:**

Some settings can be reset to system defaults:

1. Scroll to bottom of settings page
2. Click "Reset to Defaults" button (if available)
3. Confirmation dialog:
   ```
   ⚠️ Reset Settings to Defaults?

   This will restore all settings to their default values.
   Current custom settings will be lost.

   This includes:
   - Colors (back to default AHC colors)
   - Email templates (back to system templates)
   - Timezone (back to UTC)
   - All custom configurations

   Are you sure? [Cancel] [Reset Settings]
   ```
4. Click "Reset Settings" to confirm
5. Settings restored to defaults
6. Review and reconfigure as needed

**Exporting Settings:**

Back up your current settings configuration:

1. Click "Export Settings" button
2. JSON file downloads: `ahc-settings-2025-12-10.json`
3. Save file securely
4. Use for:
   - Backup before major changes
   - Migrating to new server
   - Documentation of current configuration

**Importing Settings:**

Restore previously exported settings:

1. Click "Import Settings" button
2. Upload settings JSON file
3. Preview changes
4. Confirm import
5. Settings applied

### 24.7 Best Practices

✅ **Before Making Changes:**
- Export current settings as backup
- Test changes in staging environment (if available)
- Notify team of upcoming changes
- Schedule during low-traffic period

✅ **Email Settings:**
- Always send test email after configuration
- Use dedicated email account for system notifications (not personal email)
- Enable SPF/DKIM records to prevent emails going to spam
- Monitor email delivery rates

✅ **Security:**
- Enable 2FA for all administrators
- Use strong session timeout (not too long)
- Implement IP whitelisting if office has static IP
- Regular security audits
- Keep lockout duration reasonable (not too short)

✅ **Performance:**
- Enable caching in production
- Clear cache after major updates
- Monitor cache hit rates
- Balance cache duration (too long = stale content, too short = poor performance)

✅ **Backups:**
- Enable automatic daily backups
- Test restore process quarterly
- Store backups offsite (cloud or FTP)
- Keep at least 30 days of backups
- Document backup/restore procedures

✅ **Documentation:**
- Document all custom settings
- Note reasons for non-default configurations
- Keep change log
- Share critical settings with team

### 24.8 Common Workflows

**Workflow 1: Setting Up Email for New Installation**

**Scenario:** Fresh AHC installation, need to configure email notifications

**Steps:**
1. Navigate to Settings → Email Settings
2. Choose email provider: Gmail for testing, later migrate to Office 365

**Gmail Configuration (Development):**
1. Go to Gmail account: ahc.dev.notifications@gmail.com
2. Enable 2-Step Verification
3. Generate App Password:
   - Google Account → Security → App Passwords
   - Generate password for "Mail"
   - Copy password: "xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx"

4. Return to AHC Settings
5. Fill in:
   ```
   Mail Driver: SMTP
   SMTP Host: smtp.gmail.com
   SMTP Port: 587
   SMTP Encryption: TLS
   SMTP Username: ahc.dev.notifications@gmail.com
   SMTP Password: [paste app password]
   From Name: AHC Development
   From Email: ahc.dev.notifications@gmail.com
   Reply-To Email: dev.team@ahc.org
   ```

6. Click "Save Email Settings"
7. Click "Send Test Email"
8. Enter your email: your.email@ahc.org
9. Click "Send"
10. Check inbox: "Test email from AHC" received ✅
11. Email configuration successful

**Migration to Production:**
- Switch to Office 365 corporate email
- Update settings with production SMTP details
- Test thoroughly
- Update templates with production branding

**Workflow 2: Enabling Maintenance Mode for Updates**

**Scenario:** Need to update database, perform server maintenance

**Preparation:**
1. Schedule maintenance window: Sunday 2:00 AM - 4:00 AM EAT
2. Notify users via email Friday:
   ```
   Subject: Scheduled Maintenance - Dec 15

   The AHC website and admin panel will be unavailable on
   Sunday, December 15 from 2:00 AM to 4:00 AM for scheduled maintenance.

   During this time:
   - Website will display maintenance message
   - Admin panel will be inaccessible
   - All services will resume automatically after maintenance

   Thank you for your patience.
   ```

**Sunday 1:55 AM:**
1. Login to admin panel
2. Navigate to Settings → Advanced Settings
3. Scroll to Maintenance Mode
4. Configure:
   ```
   Enable Maintenance Mode: ● Enabled
   
   Maintenance Message:
     The AHC website is currently undergoing scheduled maintenance.
     We'll be back online by 4:00 AM EAT.
     
     For urgent matters, contact: emergency@ahc.org
     
     Thank you for your patience.
   
   Allowed IPs During Maintenance:
     41.xxx.xxx.xxx (IT Manager IP)
     192.168.1.0/24 (Office network)
   ```
5. Click "Save Advanced Settings"
6. Test from different IP: Maintenance page shows ✅
7. Test from allowed IP: Site accessible ✅

**Perform Maintenance:**
- 2:00 AM: Database updates
- 2:30 AM: Server patches
- 3:15 AM: Testing
- 3:45 AM: Final verification

**Sunday 3:50 AM:**
1. Return to Settings → Advanced Settings
2. Maintenance Mode: ○ Disabled
3. Click "Save Advanced Settings"
4. Test site from public IP: Back online ✅
5. Send completion email:
   ```
   Subject: Maintenance Complete
   
   Scheduled maintenance completed successfully.
   All AHC systems are now back online.
   
   Thank you for your patience.
   ```

**Workflow 3: Updating Site Logo After Rebranding**

**Scenario:** AHC completed rebranding, have new logo files

**Received from Design Team:**
- `AHC-Logo-2025-Horizontal.png` (400x100px)
- `AHC-Logo-2025-Square.png` (200x200px)
- `AHC-Favicon.ico` (32x32px)
- New brand colors: Primary #FF6600 → #E85D00 (darker orange)

**Steps:**
1. Navigate to Settings → Appearance Settings
2. Upload new logo:
   - Click "Upload New Logo"
   - Select: AHC-Logo-2025-Horizontal.png
   - Preview shows new logo
3. Upload favicon:
   - Click "Upload Favicon"
   - Select: AHC-Favicon.ico
4. Update colors:
   - Primary Color: #FF6600 → #E85D00
   - Secondary Color: (keep current)
5. Click "Save Appearance Settings"
6. Success message shown
7. Clear browser cache (Ctrl+Shift+R)
8. Verify frontend:
   - Homepage shows new logo ✅
   - Favicon updated in browser tab ✅
   - Buttons use new primary color ✅
9. Check admin panel:
   - Login page shows new logo ✅
   - Dashboard header updated ✅
10. Send announcement:
    ```
    Subject: AHC New Brand Identity Live!
    
    Our new AHC branding is now live on the website.
    
    Changes include:
    - Updated logo
    - Refined color palette
    - Modern favicon
    
    Please clear your browser cache to see updates.
    ```

---

## Chapter 25: Monitoring & Reports

### 25.1 Overview

The Monitoring & Reports module provides insights into your AHC platform's performance, user engagement, content effectiveness, and system health. Use these tools to make data-driven decisions, identify trends, and demonstrate impact.

**Access:**
```
Admin Panel → Reports & Analytics → Dashboard
```

**Required Permission:** View Analytics & Reports

**Key Features:**
- Real-time dashboard with key metrics
- Content performance analytics
- User engagement tracking
- Traffic and visitor statistics
- System health monitoring
- Custom report generation
- Data export capabilities
- Scheduled reports

**Exporting Analytics Data:**

Export data for external analysis or presentations:

**Quick Export:**

   - PDF (formatted report)

For comprehensive data analysis:

```
Navigate to: Reports → Data Export
```

**Export Options:**

```
Data to Export: [checkboxes]
  ☑ Traffic Analytics
  ☑ Content Performance
  ☑ User Engagement
  ☑ Contact Messages
  ☑ Newsletter Subscribers
  ☐ System Logs

Date Range: [date picker]
```

### 25.10 Best Practices

✅ **Regular Review:**
- Check dashboard daily for anomalies
- Weekly review of traffic trends
- Monthly content performance analysis
- Quarterly comprehensive review

✅ **Data-Driven Decisions:**
- Use analytics to guide content strategy
- Identify and replicate successful content
- Optimize underperforming pages
- Schedule content for peak traffic times

✅ **Goal Setting:**
- Set monthly traffic targets
- Track content KPIs
- Monitor engagement metrics
- Measure against objectives

✅ **Report Sharing:**
- Weekly summary to management
- Monthly detailed reports to team
- Quarterly presentations to board
- Annual impact report

✅ **Performance Monitoring:**
- Set up alerts for critical issues
- Monitor page load times
- Track error rates
- Regular security scans

### 25.11 Common Workflows

**Workflow 1: Monthly Performance Review**

**First Monday of Month:**

1. Navigate to Dashboard
2. Set date range: Last 30 days
3. Review Quick Stats - compare to previous month
4. Document key findings:
   ```
   November 2025 Performance Summary:
   
   Traffic: 12,456 visitors (↑15% vs October)
   Top performer: New Health Initiative (3,456 views)
   Engagement: Newsletter grew by 6.5%
   Response time: 6.2 hours average
   System health: 99.98% uptime
   ```

5. Navigate to Reports → Content Performance
6. Export top 10 articles
7. Analyze what made them successful:
   - Topic relevance
   - Publishing time
   - Promotion strategy
   - Headlines and images

8. Navigate to Reports → Traffic Analytics
9. Check traffic sources
10. Note changes in referral sources
11. Identify opportunities:
    - Social media growing → increase posting
    - Organic search strong → continue SEO

12. Create Monthly Report:
    - Use Custom Report builder
    - Include all key metrics
    - Add executive summary
    - Export as PDF

13. Schedule team meeting:
    - Present findings
    - Discuss opportunities
    - Set next month's goals
    - Assign action items

14. Archive report in shared drive

**Workflow 2: Optimizing Underperforming Content**

**Scenario:** Events section getting low traffic

**Analysis:**
1. Navigate to Reports → Content Performance → Events
2. Review metrics:
   - Events page: 1,892 views (low compared to news: 2,123)
   - Average event detail page: 67 views
   - Registration conversion: only 12.3%

3. Compare to news section:
   - News gets 2,123 views
   - Average article: 542 views

4. Check traffic sources for events:
   - Direct: 45% (people searching specifically)
   - Organic: 20% (low - SEO issue?)
   - Social: 15% (underutilized)
   - Email: 5% (not promoted in newsletter?)

**Action Plan:**

1. **Improve SEO:**
   - Review event titles - make more descriptive
   - Add keywords to descriptions
   - Include location and date in meta tags

2. **Increase Promotion:**
   - Feature upcoming events on homepage
   - Include events section in newsletter
   - Create social media posts for each event
   - Add events to partner websites

3. **Enhance Content:**
   - Add more compelling event images
   - Include speaker bios and photos
   - Add testimonials from past events
   - Create video teasers

4. **Track Improvements:**
   - Set goal: 50% increase in event views next month
   - Monitor weekly progress
   - A/B test promotional strategies
   - Measure registration conversion

5. **Follow-up in 30 Days:**
   - Review new metrics
   - Document what worked
   - Continue successful strategies
   - Adjust what didn't work

## Chapter 26: AI Assistant (Droid)

### 26.1 Overview

The AHC admin panel includes an AI-powered assistant (Droid) to help you work more efficiently. Droid can help with content creation, editing, data analysis, and administrative tasks.

**Note:** AI Assistant availability and features may vary based on your system configuration and subscription level.

**Key Capabilities:**
- Content writing assistance
- Grammar and spelling correction
- Text summarization
- Translation support
- Data analysis
- Report generation
- Task automation
- Question answering

### 26.2 Accessing the AI Assistant

**Location:**

The AI Assistant is accessible from multiple locations:

1. **Global Access:**
   - Click AI Assistant icon in top navigation
   - Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+K (Cmd+K on Mac)
   - Opens chat interface overlay

2. **Context-Specific:**
   - Content editor: Click "AI Help" button
   - Data tables: Right-click → "Analyze with AI"
   - Reports: "Generate Summary" button

**Interface:**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🤖 AHC AI Assistant                     │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                         │
│ [Chat conversation appears here]        │
│                                         │
│ You: Help me write a news article      │
│ about our new health program           │
│                                         │
│ AI: I'd be happy to help! I can        │
│ create a draft news article for your   │
│ new health program. Could you provide: │
│ - Program name                          │
│ - Key objectives                        │
│ - Target audience                       │
│ - Launch date                           │
│                                         │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Type your message...            [Send] │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 26.3 Content Creation Assistance

**Writing News Articles:**

**Example Interaction:**

```
You: Write a news article about our partnership with WHO

AI: Here's a draft news article:

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
AHC Announces Strategic Partnership with WHO

Addis Ababa, Ethiopia - African Health Catalysts
(AHC) is proud to announce a new strategic partnership
with the World Health Organization (WHO) aimed at
strengthening health systems across Africa.

This collaboration will focus on:
• Capacity building for healthcare workers
• Disease surveillance and response
• Health policy development
• Community health initiatives

"This partnership represents a significant milestone
in our mission to transform health outcomes across
Africa," said [Director Name], AHC Executive Director.

The partnership will officially launch in January 2026
with pilot programs in three countries.

For more information, contact: info@ahc.org
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Would you like me to:
- Adjust the tone (more formal/casual)?
- Add more details about specific programs?
- Include quotes from WHO representatives?
- Make it shorter or longer?
```

**Available Commands:**
- "Make it more formal"
- "Add more details about [topic]"
- "Simplify the language"
- "Make it longer/shorter"
- "Add a call-to-action"
- "Include statistics"

### 26.4 Editing and Proofreading

**Grammar and Style Check:**

Select text in any editor, click "AI Check", AI reviews and suggests:

```
Original Text:
"The program will helps many peoples in rural area 
to access healthcare services more easier."

AI Suggestions:
✗ "will helps" → "will help" (verb agreement)
✗ "many peoples" → "many people" (plural form)
✗ "rural area" → "rural areas" (plural)
✗ "more easier" → "easier" (double comparative)

Corrected:
"The program will help many people in rural areas
to access healthcare services more easily."

Apply All Changes? [Yes] [Review One-by-One]
```

**Tone Adjustment:**

```
You: Make this announcement more professional

Original:
"Hey everyone! We're super excited to tell you about
our awesome new health program. It's gonna be great!"

AI Suggested Revision:
"We are pleased to announce the launch of our new
comprehensive health program, designed to deliver
significant improvements in community health outcomes."

[Use This] [Try Different Tone] [Keep Original]
```

### 26.5 Content Summarization

**Summarizing Long Articles:**

```
You: Summarize this 2000-word report into 3 bullet points

AI: Here's a concise summary:

• AHC conducted health assessments in 15 rural 
  communities, reaching 3,400 individuals

• Key findings: 67% lack access to basic health 
  services, malaria and malnutrition are primary 
  concerns

• Recommendations include mobile clinics, health 
  worker training, and community education programs

Would you like:
- More detailed summary?
- Executive summary (1 paragraph)?
- Key statistics extracted?
```

**Creating Excerpts:**

```
You: Create a 150-character excerpt for this article
     suitable for social media

AI: "AHC partners with WHO to strengthen African 
health systems. New initiative launches Jan 2026 
across 3 countries. #HealthForAll"

Character count: 149 ✓
[Copy] [Try Another Version]
```

### 26.6 Translation Support

**Translating Content:**

**Note:** Translation quality depends on language pairs and AI model capabilities.

```
You: Translate this news headline to Amharic

English: "AHC Launches New Community Health Program"

AI: "ኤ ኤች ሲ አዲስ የማህበረሰብ ጤና ፕሮግራም አስጀምሯል"

Romanization: "AHC adis ye'mahibereseb t'ena program 
asijemiral"

[Copy Translation] [Hear Pronunciation] 
[Translate Full Article]
```

**Batch Translation:**

For translating multiple items:
1. Select multiple news articles
2. Click "Bulk Actions" → "Translate with AI"
3. Select target language
4. AI translates all selected items
5. Review and approve translations

**Important:** Always have translations reviewed by native speakers before publishing.

### 26.7 Data Analysis

**Analyzing Reports:**

```
You: Analyze this month's traffic data and give insights

AI: Based on November 2025 traffic data:

Key Insights:
📈 Traffic up 15% - driven by new health initiative 
   announcement (viral on social media)

📱 Mobile traffic increased to 58% (was 52% in Oct)
   → Consider mobile-first content strategy

⏰ Peak engagement: Tuesdays 10-11 AM
   → Schedule important announcements for Tuesday mornings

🌍 Geographic shift: Kenya traffic up 45%
   → Partnership announcement resonating in East Africa

⚠ Bounce rate increased on Events page (55%, was 42%)
   → Investigate: slow load time? confusing navigation?

Recommendations:
1. Create more social-media-optimized content
2. Improve mobile experience
3. Schedule key posts for Tuesday mornings
4. Optimize Events page performance
5. Explore Kenya partnership opportunities

Would you like deeper analysis on any specific metric?
```

**Comparative Analysis:**

```
You: Compare this quarter's performance to last quarter

AI: Q4 2025 vs Q3 2025 Comparison:

Traffic:          ↑ 23%  (Excellent growth)
Content Published: ↑ 15%  (Increased output)
Engagement:       ↑ 8%   (Positive trend)
Newsletter Growth: ↑ 18%  (Strong performance)
Page Load Time:   ↓ 12%  (Faster - good!)

Biggest Wins:
• Social media traffic doubled
• Newsletter subscriber growth accelerated
• Mobile experience improvements paid off

Areas for Improvement:
• Event attendance flat despite more events
• Resource downloads decreased 5%
• Contact form abandonment rate increased

Strategic Recommendations: [View Full Analysis]
```

### 26.8 Report Generation

**Auto-Generating Reports:**

```
You: Create a monthly report for November 2025

AI: Generating comprehensive monthly report...

[Progress bar]

Report Ready! 
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

AHC Monthly Performance Report
November 2025

Executive Summary:
November was a strong month with 15% traffic growth
driven by successful new health initiative launch...

[Full report continues with sections:]
1. Traffic Overview
2. Content Performance
3. User Engagement
4. Key Achievements
5. Challenges Identified
6. Recommendations for December

Total Pages: 8
Generated in: 12 seconds

[Download PDF] [Edit in Word] [Email to Team]
```

### 26.9 Task Automation

**Workflow Automation:**

AI can help automate repetitive tasks:

**Example 1: Content Publishing Checklist**

```
You: Create a checklist for publishing a news article

AI: 
News Article Publishing Checklist
─────────────────────────────────────

Before Publishing:
☐ Spell check and grammar review
☐ SEO: Title includes keywords
☐ SEO: Meta description (150-160 chars)
☐ Featured image uploaded (1200x630px)
☐ Image alt text added
☐ Categories and tags assigned
☐ Related articles linked
☐ Social media excerpt created
☐ Review in preview mode

After Publishing:
☐ Share on Twitter
☐ Share on Facebook
☐ Share on LinkedIn
☐ Add to newsletter draft
☐ Notify relevant partners
☐ Update internal tracker

48 Hours Later:
☐ Review analytics
☐ Respond to comments
☐ Share top-performing posts

[Save as Template] [Create Automated Workflow]
```

**Example 2: Event Follow-up**

```
You: Remind me to follow up on upcoming events

AI: Event Follow-up Reminders Set:

📅 Annual Health Symposium (Jan 15, 2026)
   ├─ 30 days before: Promotion campaign
   ├─ 14 days before: Registration reminder
   ├─ 7 days before: Final details email
   ├─ 1 day before: Confirmation SMS
   └─ 3 days after: Thank you + survey

[Configure Reminders] [Set Calendar Alerts]
```

### 26.10 Question Answering

**Getting Help:**

Ask the AI assistant questions about your data or the system:

```
You: How many news articles did we publish in November?

AI: In November 2025, you published 23 news articles.

Breakdown by category:
• COVID-19 Response: 7 articles
• Partnership News: 6 articles
• Research & Publications: 5 articles
• Events & Programs: 3 articles
• Other: 2 articles

This is up from 20 articles in October (+15%).
Your most viewed November article was "New Health
Initiative Launch" with 3,456 views.
```

```
You: What are our most popular content categories?

AI: Based on all-time data, your top content categories
by total views are:

1. COVID-19 Response       45,678 views (28%)
2. Research & Publications 38,456 views (24%)
3. Partnership News        32,123 views (20%)
4. Events & Programs       28,234 views (17%)
5. Community Health        18,456 views (11%)

COVID-19 Response has been your strongest performer,
but views are trending down (↓15% this quarter) as
pandemic concerns ease. Consider shifting focus to
emerging health topics.
```

### 26.11 Best Practices

✅ **Effective AI Use:**
- Be specific in your requests
- Provide context when needed
- Review AI outputs - don't publish without checking
- Use for drafts and ideas, not final content
- Combine AI assistance with human expertise

✅ **What AI Does Well:**
- First drafts and outlines
- Grammar and spelling corrections
- Summarizing long documents
- Generating ideas and alternatives
- Data analysis and pattern finding
- Repetitive formatting tasks

✅ **What Requires Human Review:**
- Factual accuracy (AI can make mistakes)
- Cultural sensitivity and context
- Brand voice and tone consistency
- Strategic decisions
- Sensitive communications
- Final approval before publishing

✅ **Privacy and Security:**
- Don't share confidential information
- Review AI suggestions before accepting
- Don't rely on AI for sensitive data
- Verify facts independently
- Keep human oversight

### 26.12 Limitations and Cautions

⚠️ **AI Limitations:**

**Accuracy:**
- AI can generate plausible but incorrect information
- Always fact-check important claims
- Verify statistics and dates
- Double-check names and titles

**Context:**
- AI may not understand nuanced situations
- May miss organizational context
- Doesn't know recent events unless updated
- Limited understanding of local culture

**Creativity:**
- Can be formulaic or generic
- May lack original insights
- Might miss creative angles
- Human creativity still essential

**Bias:**
- AI trained on internet data may reflect biases
- Review for fairness and inclusion
- Ensure diverse perspectives
- Apply ethical judgment

**Best Practice:** Use AI as an assistant, not a replacement for human judgment, creativity, and expertise.

## Chapter 27: Health Pillars Management

### 27.1 Overview

Health Pillars represent the core focus areas of AHC's work. The Health Pillars module allows you to manage these strategic priorities, showcase your organization's expertise, and help visitors understand your areas of focus.

**Typical Health Pillars:**
- Maternal & Child Health
- Infectious Disease Control
- Non-Communicable Diseases
- Health Systems Strengthening
- Research & Innovation
- Policy & Advocacy

**Access:**
```
Admin Panel → Content → Health Pillars
```

**Required Permission:** Manage Health Pillars

### 27.2 Health Pillars Datatable

**Columns Displayed:**

| Column | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| Icon | Visual icon representing the pillar |
| Title | Pillar name |
| Description | Short description (excerpt) |
| Order | Display order on frontend |
| Status | Active / Inactive |
| Actions | View / Edit / Delete / Reorder |

**Features:**
- Drag-and-drop reordering
- Quick status toggle
- Icon preview
- Bulk actions

### 27.3 Creating a Health Pillar

**Steps:**

1. Navigate to Health Pillars
2. Click "Add New Pillar" button
3. Complete the form:

**Basic Information:**
```
Pillar Title: [text input]
  └─ Example: Maternal & Child Health

Slug: [text input]
  └─ Auto-generated: maternal-child-health
  └─ Used in URL: /health-pillars/maternal-child-health

Short Description: [textarea - 200 chars]
  └─ Brief summary for cards and previews
  └─ Example: "Improving health outcomes for mothers
               and children through comprehensive care,
               nutrition programs, and community support."

Full Description: [rich text editor]
  └─ Detailed explanation of this pillar
  └─ Include:
      • Overview of focus area
      • Why it's important
      • AHC's approach
      • Key initiatives
      • Impact and outcomes
```

**Visual Elements:**
```
Icon: [icon picker or upload]
  ├─ Choose from icon library
  │  (FontAwesome, Material Icons, etc.)
  ├─ Or upload custom SVG icon
  └─ Example: 👶 for Maternal & Child Health

Featured Image: [image upload]
  ├─ Recommended: 1200x800px
  ├─ Used on pillar detail page
  └─ Alt text required for accessibility

Color Theme: [color picker]
  └─ Primary color for this pillar
  └─ Used for accents, buttons, highlights
  └─ Example: #FF6B9D (pink for maternal health)
```

**Content Sections:**
```
Key Focus Areas: [repeater field]
  Add multiple focus areas:
  
  Focus Area 1:
    Title: Prenatal Care
    Description: Comprehensive antenatal services...
    Icon: [icon picker]
  
  Focus Area 2:
    Title: Child Nutrition
    Description: Addressing malnutrition...
    Icon: [icon picker]
  
  [+ Add Another Focus Area]

Statistics: [repeater field]
  Add key metrics:
  
  Stat 1:
    Number: 15,000
    Label: Children Reached
    Icon: 👶
  
  Stat 2:
    Number: 85%
    Label: Improved Outcomes
    Icon: 📈
  
  [+ Add Another Statistic]
```

**Related Content:**
```
Related Programs: [checkbox list]
  ☑ Mother-Child Health Initiative
  ☑ Nutrition Program
  ☐ Vaccination Campaign

Related News: [search and select]
  └─ Auto-suggest news articles related to this pillar

Related Resources: [search and select]
  └─ Publications, guidelines, toolkits
```

**SEO Settings:**
```
Meta Title: [text input]
  └─ Max 60 characters
  └─ Example: "Maternal & Child Health | AHC"

Meta Description: [textarea]
  └─ Max 160 characters
  └─ Example: "AHC's maternal and child health programs
               improve outcomes through comprehensive care,
               nutrition, and community support across Africa."

Keywords: [tags input]
  └─ maternal health, child health, prenatal care,
     nutrition, Africa health
```

**Settings:**
```
Display Order: [number input]
  └─ Order on frontend (1 = first)

Status: [radio buttons]
  ● Active (visible on frontend)
  ○ Draft (hidden from frontend)

Featured: [checkbox]
  ☐ Feature on homepage

Allow Comments: [checkbox]
  ☐ Enable comments on pillar page (if applicable)
```

4. Click "Save Pillar" or "Save & Publish"

### 27.4 Managing Health Pillars

**Editing:**
1. Find pillar in datatable
2. Click "Edit" action
3. Update fields
4. Click "Update Pillar"

**Reordering:**

Method 1: Drag and Drop
1. In datatable, click and hold drag handle (⋮⋮)
2. Drag pillar to new position
3. Release mouse
4. Order automatically saved

Method 2: Manual Order
1. Edit pillar
2. Change "Display Order" number
3. Save

**Deleting:**
1. Click "Delete" action
2. Confirmation dialog:
   ```
   Delete Health Pillar: Maternal & Child Health?
   
   This pillar has:
   - 12 related news articles
   - 8 related programs
   - 234 page views
   
   Content will be preserved (pillar reference removed).
   
   Are you sure? [Cancel] [Delete]
   ```
3. Click "Delete" to confirm

**Status Management:**

Quick toggle in datatable:
- Click status badge to toggle Active ⟷ Draft
- Active pillars visible on frontend
- Draft pillars hidden from public

### 27.5 Frontend Display

**Health Pillars Page:**

Visitors see all active pillars:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│         Our Health Pillars                  │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────┤

│  ┌────────┐  ┌────────┐  ┌────────┐       │
│  │   👶   │  │   🦠   │  │   ❤️   │       │
│  │Maternal│  │Infectious│ │  NCDs  │       │
│  │& Child │  │ Disease │  │        │       │
│  │ Health │  │ Control │  │        │       │
│  └────────┘  └────────┘  └────────┘       │
│                                             │
│  ┌────────┐  ┌────────┐  ┌────────┐       │
│  │   🏥   │  │   🔬   │  │   📋   │       │
│  │ Health │  │Research │  │ Policy │       │
│  │Systems │  │   &     │  │   &    │       │
│  │Strength│  │Innova- │  │Advocacy│       │
│  └────────┘  └────────┘  └────────┘       │
│                                             │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Pillar Detail Page:**

```
════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Maternal & Child Health
════════════════════════════════════════════════

[Featured Image]

Overview:
Improving health outcomes for mothers and children
through comprehensive care, nutrition programs, and
community support...

─────────────────────────────────────────────────

─────────────────────────────────────────────────

▸ Prenatal Care
  Comprehensive antenatal services ensuring healthy
```

### 27.6 Best Practices

✅ **Strategic Pillars:**
- Limit to 4-8 core pillars (too many dilutes focus)
- Align with organizational strategic plan
- Ensure pillars are distinct but complementary
- Review annually - are they still relevant?

✅ **Content Quality:**
- Write clear, compelling descriptions
- Use concrete examples and data
- Include impact metrics
- Update regularly with new achievements

✅ **Visual Consistency:**
- Choose cohesive icon set
- Use complementary color scheme
- High-quality images
- Consistent formatting

✅ **User Experience:**
- Logical ordering (most important first)
- Easy navigation between pillars
- Link to related content
- Clear calls-to-action

### 27.7 Common Workflows

**Workflow 1: Annual Pillar Review**

**Every January:**

1. Review current health pillars
2. Check alignment with strategic plan
3. Assess relevance:
   - Still core to mission? ✓
   - Adequate resources allocated? ✓
   - Strong performance metrics? ✓
   - Stakeholder interest? ✓

4. Update content:
   - Refresh descriptions with latest info
   - Update statistics
   - Add new programs/initiatives
   - Remove outdated content

5. Consider changes:
   - Should any pillars be merged?
   - New emerging priorities?
   - Reorder based on current focus?

6. Get stakeholder input:
   - Staff feedback
   - Board review
   - Partner consultation

7. Implement approved changes
8. Update frontend
9. Communicate changes to stakeholders

## Chapter 28: Notifications Management

### 28.1 Overview

The Notifications system keeps administrators informed about important events, activities, and updates within the AHC platform. Effective notification management ensures you stay on top of critical tasks without being overwhelmed by alerts.

**Access:**
```
Admin Panel → Notifications (bell icon in top navigation)
```

**All users have access** to their own notifications, though notification types may vary by role.

**Key Features:**
- Real-time alerts
- Email notifications
- In-app notification center
- Notification preferences
- Read/unread status
- Notification history
- Custom notification rules
- Push notifications (if enabled)

### 28.2 Notification Center

**Accessing Notifications:**

Click the bell icon (🔔) in the top navigation bar:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🔔 Notifications                    [3] │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                         │
│ ● New contact message received         │
│   From: John Doe                        │
│   2 minutes ago                  [View] │
│                                         │
│ ● User "Jane Smith" created an event   │
│   "Annual Health Symposium"             │
│   15 minutes ago                 [View] │
│                                         │
│ ○ Newsletter subscriber +12 today       │
│   Total subscribers: 1,259              │
│   1 hour ago                     [View] │
│                                         │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ [Mark All as Read] [View All]          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘

Legend:
● = Unread notification
○ = Read notification
```

**Notification Badge:**

The bell icon shows a badge with the count of unread notifications:
- 🔔 (no badge) = No unread notifications
- 🔔 [3] = 3 unread notifications
- 🔔 [9+] = 9 or more unread

### 28.3 Types of Notifications

**System Notifications:**

```
Critical System Alerts:
  ● Server error detected
  ● Disk space low (90% full)
  ● SSL certificate expires in 30 days
  ● Failed backup detected
  ● Security alert: Multiple failed logins
```

**Content Notifications:**

```
Content Activity:
  ○ New news article published by [User]
  ○ Event "[Event Name]" updated
  ○ Draft saved: "[Article Title]"
  ○ Comment awaiting moderation
  ○ Content scheduled for publishing
```

**User Activity Notifications:**

```
User Management:
  ○ New user registered: [Name]
  ○ User "[Name]" changed role to Admin
  ○ Password reset requested by [User]
  ○ User account suspended: [Name]
```

**Communication Notifications:**

```
Messages & Engagement:
  ● New contact message from [Sender]
  ● Newsletter subscriber count milestone: 1,000
  ● Unread messages: 5
  ● Contact form submission
```

**Analytics Notifications:**

```
Performance Alerts:
  ○ Traffic increased 25% this week
  ○ Content "[Title]" trending (500+ views)
  ○ Monthly report ready
  ○ Goal achieved: 10,000 monthly visitors
```

**Task & Reminder Notifications:**

```
Reminders:
  ● Event "[Event Name]" starts tomorrow
  ● Scheduled content publishing in 1 hour
  ● Weekly report due today
  ● Follow-up required: [Task]
```

### 28.4 Notification Actions

**Reading Notifications:**

1. Click notification in dropdown → marks as read automatically
2. Click [View] button → navigates to related content
3. Click [Mark as Read] → marks single notification as read
4. Click [Mark All as Read] → clears all unread notifications

**Managing Individual Notifications:**

Hover over notification to reveal actions:
- **View** - Navigate to related content
- **Mark as Read** - Clear notification
- **Delete** - Remove from list
- **Snooze** - Remind me later (30 min, 1 hour, tomorrow)

**Bulk Actions:**

```
Select multiple notifications:
  ☑ Select All
  ☐ New contact message received
  ☑ User "Jane Smith" created event
  ☑ Newsletter subscriber +12 today

Actions:
  [Mark as Read] [Delete Selected] [Export]
```

### 28.6 Custom Notification Rules

**Creating Custom Rules:**

For advanced users, create custom notification rules:

```
Navigate to: Profile → Notification Preferences → Custom Rules
```

**Example Rule 1: Content Monitoring**

```
Rule Name: High-Priority Content Alerts

Trigger:
  When: New content is published
  Content Type: News & Announcements
  Category: COVID-19 Response
  
Action:
  ☑ Send instant email notification
  ☑ Show desktop notification
  ☑ Send SMS (if configured)
  
Recipients:
  ☑ Me
  ☑ Director
  ☑ Communications Team
```

**Example Rule 2: Traffic Spike Alert**

```
Rule Name: Traffic Surge Detection

Trigger:
  When: Website traffic increases by
  Threshold: [50]% compared to previous [day/week/month]
  
Action:
  ☑ Send email notification
  ☑ Create in-app alert
  ☐ Post to Slack channel
  
Message Template:
  "Traffic spike detected! Current visitors: {visitor_count}
   ({percentage_increase}% increase)"
```

**Example Rule 3: Contact Response Time**

```
Rule Name: Urgent Contact Messages

Trigger:
  When: Contact message received
  Subject contains: "urgent", "emergency", "asap"
  Status: Unread
  Time elapsed: > 1 hour
  
Action:
  ☑ Send immediate email to on-call staff
  ☑ SMS notification
  ☑ High-priority in-app alert
  
Escalation:
  If still unread after [2] hours, notify: [Director]
```

### 28.10 Role-Based Notifications

Different roles receive different notifications:

**Super Admin Notifications:**
- All system alerts
- All user management activities
- All content activities
- Security alerts
- Backup status
- Performance alerts

**Admin Notifications:**
- User management (except Super Admin actions)
- Content activities
- Communication updates
- Some system alerts

**Editor Notifications:**
- Content they created/edited
- Comments on their content
- Publishing schedule reminders
- Media uploads
- Contact messages (if enabled)

**Moderator Notifications:**
- Content requiring moderation
- Comments for review
- Contact messages assigned to them
- Newsletter updates

**Viewer Notifications:**
- Reports ready
- Analytics updates
- Limited system notifications

**Customization:**

All users can customize within their role's permissions:
```
Navigate to: Profile → Notification Preferences
Select which notification types to receive
Configure delivery method (in-app, email, push)
```

### 28.13 Best Practices

✅ **Notification Hygiene:**
- Regularly clear read notifications
- Unsubscribe from unnecessary notification types
- Use quiet hours to avoid after-hours alerts
- Configure daily digest for non-urgent updates
- Keep critical alerts instant

✅ **Prioritization:**
- Enable all security and system alerts
- Prioritize notifications relevant to your role
- Disable noisy, low-value notifications
- Use custom rules for important content
- Set up escalation for urgent matters

✅ **Response Times:**
- Check notifications at least twice daily
- Respond to contact messages within 4 hours
- Address system alerts immediately
- Review daily digest each morning
- Set reminders for follow-up actions

✅ **Email Management:**
- Create email filters for AHC notifications
- Use a dedicated admin email if possible
- Enable daily digest to reduce inbox clutter
- Archive notification emails after acting
- Unsubscribe from redundant notifications

✅ **Team Coordination:**
- Ensure coverage for critical notifications
- Assign notification responsibilities by role
- Use escalation rules for urgent matters
- Document notification response procedures
- Regular team check-ins on notification load

### 28.14 Troubleshooting

**Not Receiving Notifications:**

```
Checklist:

1. Check Notification Preferences
   - Profile → Notification Preferences
   - Ensure notification types are enabled
   - Verify email address is correct

2. Check Email Spam/Junk Folder
   - Add notifications@ahc.org to safe senders
   - Check spam folder for missed notifications

3. Browser Permissions
   - Desktop notifications require browser permission
   - Check browser settings → Notifications
   - Allow notifications for ahc.org

4. Email Server Issues
   - Ask IT to check email delivery logs
   - Verify SMTP settings in admin panel
   - Test with "Send Test Notification"

5. Clear Browser Cache
   - Old cached data may block notifications
   - Clear cache and cookies
   - Refresh admin panel
```

**Too Many Notifications:**

```
Solutions:

1. Adjust Notification Preferences
   - Disable low-priority notifications
   - Enable daily digest for non-urgent items
   - Use quiet hours for off-work periods

2. Create Custom Rules
   - Filter by importance
   - Batch similar notifications
   - Set thresholds for alerts

3. Delegate Responsibilities
   - Assign specific notification types to team members
   - Share notification load across staff
   - Use role-based filtering

4. Optimize Triggers
   - Increase thresholds for performance alerts
   - Reduce frequency of routine updates
   - Consolidate similar notifications
```

**Notification Delays:**

```
Causes & Solutions:

Cause: Email server queue backup
Solution: Check SMTP configuration, increase server capacity

Cause: High system load
Solution: Monitor server performance, optimize database

Cause: Browser tab inactive
Solution: Keep admin panel tab active or enable push notifications

Cause: Internet connection issues
Solution: Check network connectivity, try different browser

Cause: Browser blocking notifications
Solution: Check browser notification permissions
```

### 28.15 Common Workflows

**Workflow 1: Morning Notification Review**

**Daily Routine (8:00 AM):**

1. Open admin panel
2. Click notification bell icon
3. Review overnight notifications (sorted by priority):
   - **Critical alerts first** (red)
   - Security notifications
   - System errors
   - Failed backups
   
4. Check **contact messages** (urgent responses needed)
   - Mark urgent ones for immediate follow-up
   - Assign to appropriate team members
   
5. Review **content notifications**
   - New published articles (quality check)
   - Scheduled content going live
   - Comments awaiting moderation
   
6. Check **analytics notifications**
   - Traffic alerts
   - Trending content
   - Milestone achievements
   
7. Mark all read notifications as read
8. Create tasks for follow-up items
9. Check daily digest email for summary

**Workflow 2: Setting Up Notifications for New User**

**When: New staff member joins**

1. Create user account (Chapter 22)
2. Guide user to set up notifications:
   
   **Step 1: Basic Preferences**
   - Navigate to Profile → Notification Preferences
   - Enable in-app notifications
   - Enter email address
   - Enable email notifications
   
   **Step 2: Select Relevant Types**
   - Based on role, enable appropriate categories:
     - Editor: Content, Comments, Publishing schedule
     - Admin: All except system (unless IT)
     - Moderator: Contact messages, Comments
   
   **Step 3: Configure Delivery**
   - Instant notifications for critical items
   - Daily digest for routine updates
   - Set quiet hours: 6 PM to 8 AM
   
   **Step 4: Test**
   - Send test notification
   - Verify in-app notification appears
   - Check email received
   - Confirm desktop notification (if enabled)

3. Add to email distribution lists
4. Document preferences in user profile
5. Schedule 30-day review of notification settings

**Workflow 3: Managing High-Priority Alert**

**Scenario: Critical system alert received**

**Alert:** "Server disk space critically low (95% full)"

**Immediate Response (within minutes):**

1. Notification appears:
   - Desktop pop-up: "CRITICAL: Disk space 95% full"
   - Email: Urgent system alert
   - SMS: Critical alert (if configured)

2. Click notification → view details
   - Current disk usage: 95%
   - Available space: 2.3 GB
   - Growth rate: 500 MB/day
   - Estimated full: 4-5 days

3. Assess severity:
   - Critical: Yes (>90% threshold)
   - Impact: Site may crash when full
   - Urgency: High (immediate action required)

4. Take immediate action:
   - Clear temporary files
   - Delete old backups
   - Remove unnecessary media
   - Archive old logs

5. Monitor result:
   - Check disk space: Now 78%
   - Crisis averted temporarily

6. Plan long-term solution:
   - Upgrade disk space
   - Implement automated cleanup
   - Set earlier warning threshold (80%)
   - Schedule regular maintenance

7. Document incident:
   - What happened
   - Actions taken
   - Resolution
   - Prevention steps

8. Update notification rules:
   - Add 80% warning threshold
   - Enable predictive alerts
   - Add escalation to IT team

---

*End of Chapter 28 - Notifications Management*
