# PART 5: TROUBLESHOOTING & SUPPORT

---

## Chapter 31: Common Issues & Solutions

This chapter provides solutions to frequently encountered issues when using the AHC platform.

### 31.1 Login & Authentication Issues

#### Issue: Cannot Log In - "Invalid Credentials"

**Symptoms:**
- Error message: "These credentials do not match our records"
- Unable to access admin panel

**Solutions:**

1. **Verify credentials:**
   - Check username/email is correct
   - Ensure caps lock is OFF
   - Try copy-pasting password to avoid typos

2. **Reset password:**
   ```
   1. Click "Forgot Password?" on login page
   2. Enter your email address
   3. Check email for reset link (check spam folder)
   4. Click link and create new password
   5. Password must meet requirements:
      - At least 8 characters
      - Contains uppercase and lowercase letters
      - Contains at least one number
      - Contains at least one special character
   ```

3. **Account locked:**
   - After 5 failed login attempts, account locks for 15 minutes
   - Wait 15 minutes and try again
   - Or contact system administrator

4. **Browser issues:**
   - Clear browser cache and cookies
   - Try incognito/private browsing mode
   - Try a different browser

#### Issue: Session Keeps Timing Out

**Symptoms:**
- Logged out unexpectedly while working
- "Your session has expired" message

**Solutions:**

1. **Extend session timeout:**
   ```
   Admin: Settings → Advanced → Session Settings
   Session Lifetime: [120] minutes (default: 120)
   ```

2. **Browser settings:**
   - Allow cookies from ahc.org domain
   - Don't use "Block all cookies" mode
   - Add ahc.org to exceptions if using privacy extensions

3. **Keep session active:**
   - Open admin panel in dedicated browser window
   - Avoid using "Back" button extensively
   - Save work frequently

#### Issue: Two-Factor Authentication Not Working

**Symptoms:**
- 2FA code not accepted
- "Invalid or expired code" error

**Solutions:**

1. **Time sync issue:**
   - Ensure device time is synchronized
   - Check device timezone is correct
   - Use network time synchronization

2. **Code expired:**
   - 2FA codes expire after 30 seconds
   - Generate new code and enter immediately

3. **Backup codes:**
   ```
   If unable to access 2FA app:
   1. Use one of your backup codes
   2. Enter backup code instead of 2FA code
   3. Each backup code can only be used once
   ```

4. **Reset 2FA:**
   - Contact system administrator
   - Provide identity verification
   - Administrator can disable 2FA for your account

### 31.2 Content Management Issues

#### Issue: Cannot Save Content - Validation Errors

**Symptoms:**
- Red error messages on form
- "The given data was invalid" error
- Content not saving

**Solutions:**

1. **Check required fields:**
   ```
   Common required fields:
   ✓ Title (not empty, max 255 characters)
   ✓ Slug (unique, lowercase, no spaces)
   ✓ Content/Description
   ✓ Category or Type
   ✓ Status
   ✓ Publish Date
   ```

2. **Fix validation errors:**
   - Read error messages carefully
   - Fix each highlighted field
   - Scroll through entire form (errors may be at bottom)

3. **Slug conflicts:**
   ```
   Error: "The slug has already been taken"
   
   Solution:
   - Change slug to be unique
   - Example: "health-event" → "health-event-2025"
   - System may auto-append number
   ```

4. **Character limits:**
   - Title: max 255 characters
   - Slug: max 255 characters
   - Meta description: recommended 155 characters
   - Check character counter below fields

#### Issue: Rich Text Editor Not Working

**Symptoms:**
- Toolbar buttons don't respond
- Cannot format text
- Blank editor area

**Solutions:**

1. **Refresh editor:**
   - Save current work (if possible)
   - Refresh page (F5)
   - Content may auto-save

2. **Browser compatibility:**
   ```
   Supported browsers:
   ✓ Chrome 90+
   ✓ Firefox 88+
   ✓ Edge 90+
   ✓ Safari 14+
   
   Not supported:
   ✗ Internet Explorer
   ✗ Very old browser versions
   ```

3. **JavaScript errors:**
   - Open browser console (F12)
   - Look for JavaScript errors (red text)
   - Disable browser extensions temporarily
   - Try incognito/private mode

4. **Content recovery:**
   ```
   If editor crashes with unsaved content:
   1. Check browser console for auto-saved draft
   2. Look for "Auto-saved at HH:MM" message
   3. Click "Restore" if available
   4. Or check "Drafts" in content list
   ```

#### Issue: Featured Image Not Displaying

**Symptoms:**
- Image uploads successfully but doesn't show on frontend
- Broken image icon
- Alt text shown instead of image

**Solutions:**

1. **Check image upload:**
   ```
   1. Go back to edit content
   2. Verify image is selected in "Featured Image" field
   3. Click [Select Image] and choose again
   4. Save content
   ```

2. **Image file issues:**
   - Ensure image format is supported (JPG, PNG, WebP)
   - Check image isn't corrupted
   - Try re-uploading image
   - File size under 5 MB recommended

3. **Cache issue:**
   ```
   1. Clear cache: Settings → Advanced → Clear Cache
   2. Hard refresh frontend (Ctrl+Shift+R)
   3. Check in incognito mode
   ```

4. **File permissions:**
   - Contact administrator
   - Storage folder may have permission issues
   - Administrator checks /storage/app/public/media/

### 31.3 Media Upload Problems

#### Issue: Cannot Upload Images - Error Messages

**Symptoms:**
- "File upload failed" error
- "File too large" error
- Upload progress bar stalls

**Solutions:**

1. **File size limit:**
   ```
   Maximum file sizes:
   - Images: 5 MB
   - Videos: 100 MB
   - Documents: 10 MB
   
   Solution:
   - Compress images using TinyPNG, Squoosh
   - Resize images to appropriate dimensions
   - For videos, consider YouTube/Vimeo embedding
   ```

2. **File format not supported:**
   ```
   Supported formats:
   Images:  JPG, JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF
   Videos:  MP4, WebM, MOV
   Documents: PDF, DOC, DOCX, XLS, XLSX
   
   Unsupported: BMP, TIFF, RAW, EXE, ZIP (for security)
   ```

3. **Internet connection:**
   - Check internet connection is stable
   - Try uploading smaller files first
   - Upload one file at a time instead of batch
   - Avoid uploading on slow connections

4. **Browser issues:**
   - Try different browser
   - Disable browser extensions
   - Clear browser cache
   - Update browser to latest version

#### Issue: Uploaded Images Look Pixelated

**Symptoms:**
- Images appear blurry or low quality
- Images look worse than original

**Solutions:**

1. **Upload higher resolution:**
   ```
   Recommended image dimensions:
   - Featured images: 1200×630 px minimum
   - Gallery images: 1920×1080 px
   - Thumbnails: 600×400 px minimum
   - Profile photos: 500×500 px minimum
   ```

2. **Avoid over-compression:**
   - Don't compress images multiple times
   - Use quality setting 80-90% when compressing
   - Keep original images backed up

3. **Upload original:**
   - Upload highest quality version
   - System will auto-generate optimized versions
   - Don't upload already-compressed images

#### Issue: Video Not Playing on Frontend

**Symptoms:**
- Video player shows loading spinner indefinitely
- "Video format not supported" error
- Video shows but doesn't play

**Solutions:**

1. **Video encoding:**
   ```
   Recommended video settings:
   - Format: MP4 (H.264 codec)
   - Resolution: 1080p or 720p
   - Bitrate: 5-10 Mbps
   - Audio: AAC codec
   
   Convert using HandBrake or FFmpeg
   ```

2. **File size:**
   - Videos over 100 MB may not upload
   - Consider external hosting (YouTube, Vimeo)
   - Use YouTube embed for large videos

3. **Browser compatibility:**
   - MP4 works in all modern browsers
   - WebM requires newer browsers
   - MOV may not work in some browsers

4. **Use external hosting:**
   ```
   Best practice for videos:
   1. Upload to YouTube or Vimeo
   2. Copy embed code or URL
   3. In AHC editor, click "Embed Video" button
   4. Paste YouTube URL
   5. Video embeds automatically
   ```

### 31.4 Email Delivery Issues

#### Issue: Contact Form Submissions Not Received

**Symptoms:**
- Form submits successfully but no email received
- "Message sent" confirmation shown but nothing arrives

**Solutions:**

1. **Check spam folder:**
   - Look in spam/junk folder
   - Add noreply@ahc.org to contacts
   - Mark as "Not Spam"

2. **Verify email settings:**
   ```
   Admin: Settings → Email Configuration
   
   Check:
   ✓ Notification email addresses are correct
   ✓ SMTP settings are configured
   ✓ Email notifications are enabled
   ✓ Test email sends successfully
   ```

3. **Check contact messages in admin:**
   ```
   Even if email fails, submissions are saved:
   1. Admin Panel → Contact Messages
   2. View all submissions
   3. Toggle to "Not Replied" to see new messages
   ```

4. **SMTP issues:**
   - Contact system administrator
   - SMTP server may be down
   - Email credentials may have changed
   - Check SMTP logs for errors

#### Issue: Newsletter Emails Not Sending

**Symptoms:**
- Newsletter shows "Sent" but subscribers don't receive it
- Some subscribers receive, others don't

**Solutions:**

1. **Verify subscriber status:**
   ```
   Admin Panel → Newsletter Subscribers
   
   Filters: [Active ▼]
   
   Only "Active" subscribers receive emails
   - Unsubscribed: won't receive
   - Bounced: email address invalid
   ```

2. **Email queue:**
   ```
   Newsletters send in batches to avoid spam filters:
   
   Admin Panel → Newsletter → Sent Newsletters
   
   Status indicators:
   - Queued: waiting to send
   - Sending: currently processing
   - Sent: completed
   - Failed: check error logs
   
   Large lists (1000+) may take 30-60 minutes
   ```

3. **Spam filters:**
   ```
   To avoid spam filters:
   ✓ Ensure valid "From" email address
   ✓ Include unsubscribe link (automatic)
   ✓ Avoid spam trigger words (FREE, URGENT, !!!)
   ✓ Use proper email authentication (SPF, DKIM)
   ✓ Send from verified domain
   ✓ Don't send too frequently
   ```

4. **Test before sending:**
   ```
   Best practice:
   1. Create newsletter
   2. Send test to yourself
   3. Check email appearance
   4. Click all links to verify
   5. Wait 5-10 minutes to ensure delivery
   6. Then send to full list
   ```

### 31.5 Performance & Loading Issues

#### Issue: Admin Panel Loading Slowly

**Symptoms:**
- Pages take 5+ seconds to load
- Dashboard widgets slow to appear
- Operations feel sluggish

**Solutions:**

1. **Clear cache:**
   ```
   Admin Panel → Settings → Advanced
   
   Clear Cache Options:
   ☑ Application cache
   ☑ View cache
   ☑ Route cache
   ☐ Configuration cache (be careful)
   
   [Clear Selected Caches]
   ```

2. **Browser performance:**
   - Close unnecessary browser tabs
   - Clear browser cache and cookies
   - Disable heavy browser extensions
   - Try Chrome/Edge for best performance

3. **Large datasets:**
   ```
   If managing thousands of records:
   - Use filters to limit results
   - Use pagination (show 20-50 items per page)
   - Archive old content
   - Use search instead of scrolling
   ```

4. **Internet connection:**
   - Check internet speed
   - Close bandwidth-heavy applications
   - Use wired connection instead of WiFi if possible

5. **Contact administrator:**
   - Server may need optimization
   - Database indexing may help
   - More server resources may be needed

#### Issue: Frontend Pages Loading Slowly

**Symptoms:**
- Public website pages load slowly
- Images load slowly or progressively
- Mobile site especially slow

**Solutions:**

1. **Image optimization:**
   ```
   For content creators:
   - Upload appropriately sized images
   - Don't upload 5MB images for small thumbnails
   - Use WebP format when possible
   - Compress before uploading
   ```

2. **Cache clearing:**
   ```
   Admin: Settings → Advanced → Clear Cache
   Then wait 2-3 minutes for cache to rebuild
   ```

3. **CDN and caching:**
   - Contact administrator about CDN
   - Browser caching should be enabled
   - Static assets should be cached

4. **Check from different device:**
   - Test on different internet connection
   - Use Google PageSpeed Insights
   - Check if issue is local or global

### 31.6 Mobile Responsiveness Issues

#### Issue: Content Not Displaying Properly on Mobile

**Symptoms:**
- Text overlapping on phone
- Images too large for screen
- Buttons cut off or unclickable

**Solutions:**

1. **Preview on actual devices:**
   ```
   Before publishing:
   1. Use mobile preview in browser (F12, toggle device)
   2. Test on actual phone/tablet
   3. Check both portrait and landscape
   4. Test on different screen sizes
   ```

2. **Image sizing:**
   - Don't set fixed pixel widths in content
   - Use responsive images (automatic in AHC)
   - Don't use very wide images (max 1920px)

3. **Table responsiveness:**
   ```
   For tables in content:
   - Keep tables simple (max 4-5 columns)
   - Use table responsive option in editor
   - Consider alternative layout for complex data
   - Use lists instead of tables when possible
   ```

4. **Embedded content:**
   - YouTube embeds are responsive (automatic)
   - For iframes, ensure responsive embed code
   - Test all embedded content on mobile

#### Issue: Admin Panel Difficult to Use on Tablet

**Symptoms:**
- Buttons too small to tap accurately
- Datatables hard to navigate
- Form fields overlapping

**Solutions:**

1. **Tablet recommendations:**
   ```
   Minimum screen size: 10" tablet recommended
   Orientation: Landscape mode works best
   Browser: Use Safari on iPad, Chrome on Android
   ```

2. **Desktop mode:**
   - Enable "Desktop Site" in mobile browser
   - Admin panel optimized for desktop
   - Some features work better on desktop

3. **Simplified tasks:**
   ```
   Mobile/tablet: Good for
   ✓ Viewing dashboard
   ✓ Quick edits
   ✓ Reading content
   ✓ Viewing reports
   ✓ Checking messages
   
   Desktop: Better for
   ✓ Creating new content
   ✓ Complex formatting
   ✓ Bulk operations
   ✓ Settings configuration
   ✓ Report generation
   ```

### 31.7 Browser Compatibility Issues

#### Issue: Features Not Working in Certain Browsers

**Symptoms:**
- Buttons don't respond
- Forms don't submit
- Pages display incorrectly

**Solutions:**

1. **Supported browsers:**
   ```
   Fully supported:
   ✓ Google Chrome 90+ (recommended)
   ✓ Microsoft Edge 90+
   ✓ Mozilla Firefox 88+
   ✓ Safari 14+ (macOS/iOS)
   
   Limited support:
   ⚠ Safari 13 (mostly works)
   ⚠ Firefox 80-87 (mostly works)
   
   Not supported:
   ✗ Internet Explorer (any version)
   ✗ Very old browser versions
   ```

2. **Update browser:**
   ```
   Check browser version:
   Chrome: Menu → Help → About Google Chrome
   Firefox: Menu → Help → About Firefox
   Edge: Menu → Help and feedback → About
   Safari: Safari → About Safari
   
   Update if version is outdated
   ```

3. **Browser-specific issues:**
   
   **Safari issues:**
   - Enable JavaScript: Preferences → Security
   - Allow cookies: Preferences → Privacy
   - Disable content blockers for ahc.org

   **Firefox issues:**
   - Check tracking protection isn't blocking features
   - Disable strict mode for ahc.org
   - Allow popups from ahc.org

4. **Use recommended browser:**
   - Chrome offers best compatibility
   - Edge (Chromium) also excellent
   - Keep browser updated

### 31.8 Search & Filter Issues

#### Issue: Search Not Returning Expected Results

**Symptoms:**
- Searching for content returns no results
- Results don't include recent content
- Search seems incomplete

**Solutions:**

1. **Search index rebuild:**
   ```
   Admin Panel → Settings → Advanced
   
   Search Index:
     Last rebuilt: 2025-12-01 10:30 AM
     [Rebuild Search Index]
   
   Wait 5-10 minutes for large sites
   ```

2. **Search tips:**
   ```
   Effective searching:
   ✓ Use specific keywords
   ✓ Try different word forms
   ✓ Use quotation marks for exact phrases
   ✓ Check spelling
   
   Search looks in:
   - Titles
   - Content
   - Excerpts
   - Categories
   - Tags (if applicable)
   ```

3. **Filters not working:**
   ```
   If filters don't apply:
   1. Clear all filters first
   2. Apply one filter at a time
   3. Click "Apply Filters" button
   4. Refresh page if filters stick
   ```

4. **Date range issues:**
   - Ensure "From" date is before "To" date
   - Check date format (MM/DD/YYYY or DD/MM/YYYY)
   - Use date picker instead of typing

### 31.9 Permission & Access Issues

#### Issue: "Access Denied" or "Unauthorized" Errors

**Symptoms:**
- Cannot access certain admin pages
- "You do not have permission" message
- Menu items missing

**Solutions:**

1. **Check your role:**
   ```
   View your profile:
   Top right → [Your Name] → Profile
   
   Your role determines access:
   - Administrator: Full access
   - Editor: Content management
   - Author: Limited content creation
   - Viewer: Read-only access
   ```

2. **Request access:**
   ```
   If you need additional permissions:
   1. Contact system administrator
   2. Specify what you need access to
   3. Explain why (justification)
   4. Administrator can grant permissions
   ```

3. **Temporary restrictions:**
   - Account may be under review
   - Access may be temporarily revoked
   - Contact administrator for clarification

### 31.10 Data Export Issues

#### Issue: Cannot Export Data or Download Fails

**Symptoms:**
- Export button doesn't work
- Download starts but file is corrupt
- Export times out

**Solutions:**

1. **Large datasets:**
   ```
   If exporting thousands of records:
   - Use date range filters to limit data
   - Export in smaller batches
   - Export may take several minutes
   - Don't close browser during export
   ```

2. **File format:**
   ```
   Choose appropriate format:
   - Excel (.xlsx): Best for data analysis
   - CSV (.csv): Universal compatibility
   - PDF: For reports, read-only
   
   Try different format if one fails
   ```

3. **Browser download settings:**
   - Check browser isn't blocking download
   - Allow popups from ahc.org
   - Check download folder isn't full
   - Try different browser

4. **Server timeout:**
   ```
   If export times out:
   1. Reduce data range
   2. Export one section at a time
   3. Contact administrator for manual export
   ```

## Chapter 32: Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)

Organized answers to common questions about using the AHC platform.

### 32.1 General Questions

**Q: What browsers work best with the AHC platform?**

A: Google Chrome (version 90+) and Microsoft Edge (version 90+) offer the best experience. Firefox 88+ and Safari 14+ also work well. Internet Explorer is not supported.

**Q: Can I use AHC on my mobile phone?**

A: Yes! The public frontend is fully mobile-responsive. The admin panel works on tablets (10"+) but is optimized for desktop use. For best admin experience, use a laptop or desktop computer.

**Q: How often is the website backed up?**

A: The AHC platform is automatically backed up daily. Database backups occur every 24 hours, and media files are backed up weekly. Contact your system administrator for specific backup schedules.

**Q: Is my data secure on the AHC platform?**

A: Yes. AHC uses industry-standard security practices:
- HTTPS encryption for all data transmission
- Secure password hashing
- Role-based access control
- Regular security updates
- Daily backups
- Activity logging

**Q: Can I access AHC from home or do I need to be in the office?**

A: You can access AHC from anywhere with an internet connection. Use the same login URL: https://admin.ahc.org/login

**Q: What happens if I forget my password?**

A: Click "Forgot Password?" on the login page, enter your email, and you'll receive a password reset link. If you don't receive the email within 5 minutes, check your spam folder or contact your administrator.

### 32.2 Content Management Questions

**Q: How long does it take for content to appear on the website after publishing?**

A: Published content appears immediately on the frontend. If you don't see it, try:
- Hard refresh your browser (Ctrl+Shift+R)
- Wait 1-2 minutes for cache to clear
- Check that Status is set to "Published" not "Draft"
- Verify Publish Date isn't set in the future

**Q: Can I schedule content to publish automatically at a future date?**

A: Yes! When creating or editing content:
1. Set Status to "Published"
2. Set Publish Date to your desired future date/time
3. Content will automatically go live at that time

**Q: What's the difference between "Draft" and "Archived" status?**

A:
- **Draft**: Work in progress, not visible to public, actively being edited
- **Published**: Live on website, visible to public
- **Archived**: No longer active but kept for records, not visible to public

**Q: Can I revert to a previous version of content?**

A: Yes! Content versioning is available:
1. Edit the content
2. Click "Version History" button
3. View previous versions
4. Click "Restore" on desired version

**Q: Is there a limit to how much content I can create?**

A: There's no strict limit on number of articles, events, or pages. However, best practices recommend:
- Archive old content (2+ years old)
- Delete duplicate or test content
- Keep total active content under 10,000 items for best performance

**Q: Can I embed videos directly into content?**

A: Yes! You can:
- Embed YouTube or Vimeo videos (recommended for large videos)
- Upload MP4 videos directly (recommended under 100 MB)
- Use the "Embed Video" button in the rich text editor

**Q: How do I add a button or call-to-action in my content?**

A: In the rich text editor:
1. Type button text
2. Select the text
3. Click link icon
4. Enter URL
5. Click "Button" style in formatting toolbar
6. Choose button color

Or use HTML in source mode:
```html
<a href="URL" class="btn btn-primary">Button Text</a>
```

**Q: Can multiple people edit the same content simultaneously?**

A: While technically possible, it's not recommended. The last person to save will overwrite others' changes. Best practice:
- Communicate with team before editing
- Check "Last Modified By" before editing
- Use comments/notes to coordinate

### 32.3 Media & Images Questions

**Q: What image file formats are supported?**

A: Supported image formats:
- JPG/JPEG (recommended for photos)
- PNG (recommended for graphics with transparency)
- WebP (recommended for best compression)
- GIF (for simple animations)

**Q: What's the maximum file size for uploads?**

A:
- Images: 5 MB maximum
- Videos: 100 MB maximum
- Documents: 10 MB maximum

For larger files, compress before uploading or use external hosting.

**Q: Do I need to resize images before uploading?**

A: The system auto-generates thumbnails and optimized sizes, but for best results:
- Featured images: upload at 1200×630 px minimum
- Gallery images: 1920×1080 px recommended
- Don't upload images larger than 4000 px width
- Compress images to under 1-2 MB before uploading

**Q: Can I edit images within the AHC platform?**

A: Limited editing is available:
- Crop images
- Add alt text
- Rename files

For advanced editing (color correction, filters, text overlay), use external tools like Photoshop, GIMP, or Canva, then upload the edited image.

**Q: How do I make images accessible for visually impaired users?**

A: Always add descriptive alt text:
1. Upload or select image
2. Enter alt text describing the image
3. Example: "Group of healthcare professionals standing in hospital lobby"
4. Keep alt text concise (max 125 characters)
5. Don't start with "Image of..." (redundant)

**Q: What happens to images if I delete content that uses them?**

A: Images remain in the media library even if you delete content. This prevents accidentally breaking images used in multiple places. To free up space, manually delete unused images from Media Library.

### 32.4 User Management Questions

**Q: How do I create an account for a new team member?**

A: If you're an Administrator:
1. Admin Panel → User Management → Users
2. Click [Create New User]
3. Fill in details (name, email, role)
4. Send invitation email
5. User receives email to set password

**Q: What's the difference between user roles?**

A:
- **Administrator**: Full access to everything, manage settings and users
- **Editor**: Create, edit, publish all content, manage media
- **Author**: Create and edit own content, cannot publish without approval
- **Viewer**: Read-only access, can view admin panel but can't change anything

**Q: Can I change my own password?**

A: Yes:
1. Top right → [Your Name] → Profile
2. Click [Change Password]
3. Enter current password
4. Enter new password twice
5. Save

**Q: Can a user have multiple roles?**

A: No, each user is assigned one primary role. If you need permissions from multiple roles, contact administrator to create a custom role or adjust your current role's permissions.

**Q: How do I deactivate a user who left the organization?**

A: Administrators can:
1. User Management → Users
2. Find the user
3. Click [Edit]
4. Set Status to "Inactive"
5. Save (user cannot log in but data is preserved)

Or permanently delete (cannot be undone):
1. Click [Delete] on user row
2. Confirm deletion
3. User's content remains, attributed to "[Deleted User]"

**Q: What happens if I lock myself out with Two-Factor Authentication?**

A: Use backup codes you saved when enabling 2FA. If you lost backup codes, contact your system administrator - they can disable 2FA for your account after verifying your identity.

### 32.5 Email & Notifications Questions

**Q: How do I stop receiving email notifications?**

A: Adjust notification preferences:
1. Top right → [Your Name] → Notifications
2. Click [Notification Preferences]
3. Uncheck notification types you don't want
4. Save preferences

**Q: Why am I not receiving email notifications?**

A: Check:
- Email address is correct in your profile
- Notifications are enabled in preferences
- Emails aren't going to spam folder
- Add noreply@ahc.org to contacts
- Contact administrator if still not receiving

**Q: Can I receive a daily summary instead of individual notifications?**

A: Yes! In notification preferences:
1. Select "Daily Digest" mode
2. Choose time to receive summary (e.g., 8:00 AM)
3. Uncheck individual notifications
4. You'll receive one email per day with all updates

**Q: How do I send a newsletter to subscribers?**

A: (Requires Editor or Administrator role)
1. Admin Panel → Newsletter → Create Newsletter
2. Compose content using rich text editor
3. Send test to yourself first
4. Review and send to all active subscribers
5. Monitor delivery in Sent Newsletters section

**Q: Can I see who received my newsletter?**

A: Yes:
1. Admin Panel → Newsletter → Sent Newsletters
2. Click on newsletter name
3. View delivery statistics:
   - Total sent
   - Delivered successfully
   - Bounced (failed)
   - Opened (if tracking enabled)
   - Clicked links (if tracking enabled)

### 32.6 Search & Navigation Questions

**Q: How do I quickly find content I created?**

A: Use filters:
1. Navigate to News, Events, or Pages
2. Filter by Author: select your name
3. Or use search with your name
4. Or sort by "Last Modified" to see recent edits

**Q: Can I bookmark frequently accessed pages in admin panel?**

A: Yes, bookmark in browser:
- Press Ctrl+D (Windows) or Cmd+D (Mac)
- Or bookmark key pages like Dashboard, News, Contact Messages
- Browser bookmarks work for all admin URLs

**Q: What's the fastest way to navigate the admin panel?**

A: Use keyboard shortcuts (if enabled):
- `Ctrl+K`: Quick search/command palette
- `Ctrl+S`: Save current form
- `Esc`: Close modal
- See Appendix B for full keyboard shortcut list

**Q: How does the search function work?**

A: The search feature:
- Searches titles, content, excerpts
- Supports partial word matching
- Not case-sensitive
- Use quotation marks for exact phrases
- Results ranked by relevance

### 32.7 Troubleshooting Questions

**Q: What should I do if I encounter an error message?**

A:
1. Take a screenshot of the error
2. Note what you were doing when error occurred
3. Try refreshing the page (F5)
4. Try logging out and back in
5. If error persists, contact support with:
   - Screenshot of error
   - Steps to reproduce
   - Browser and version
   - Time error occurred

**Q: The page is stuck loading. What should I do?**

A:
1. Wait 30 seconds (large datasets may take time)
2. Check internet connection
3. Refresh page (F5)
4. Try different browser
5. Clear browser cache
6. Contact administrator if issue persists

**Q: I accidentally deleted something. Can it be recovered?**

A: Depends on what was deleted:
- **Content**: Check Archived status filter - may be archived not deleted
- **Permanently deleted**: Contact administrator immediately - may be recoverable from daily backup
- **Media files**: If deleted from Media Library, may not be recoverable
- **Users**: Administrator can restore from backup

**Q: Why do my changes not appear on the live website?**

A: Check:
1. Status is set to "Published" not "Draft"
2. Publish Date isn't in the future
3. Clear cache: Settings → Advanced → Clear Cache
4. Hard refresh frontend (Ctrl+Shift+R)
5. Wait 2-3 minutes for cache to rebuild
6. Check in incognito/private browsing mode

### 32.8 Best Practices Questions

**Q: How often should I log in to check the admin panel?**

A: Recommended frequency by role:
- **Administrator**: Daily
- **Editor**: Daily or every other day
- **Author**: As needed when creating content
- **All roles**: Check notifications regularly

**Q: What's the best time to publish content?**

A: For maximum engagement:
- **News articles**: 9-11 AM on weekdays
- **Events**: As soon as details confirmed
- **Newsletters**: Tuesday-Thursday, 8-10 AM
- Avoid weekends unless urgent

**Q: Should I save drafts frequently?**

A: Yes! Best practices:
- Click [Save Draft] every 5-10 minutes
- System auto-saves every 2 minutes (if enabled)
- Don't rely solely on auto-save
- Complex content: save after each major section

**Q: How do I keep content organized?**

A: Organization tips:
- Use clear, descriptive titles
- Apply appropriate categories
- Use consistent naming conventions
- Archive old content (2+ years)
- Delete test/duplicate content
- Tag content for easy filtering
- Use featured images for visual recognition

**Q: What makes good quality content for AHC?**

A:
- **Clear headings**: Break content into scannable sections
- **Concise**: Get to the point quickly
- **Actionable**: Tell readers what to do next
- **Accurate**: Fact-check all information
- **Accessible**: Use simple language, alt text on images
- **Formatted**: Use lists, bold, links appropriately
- **Proofread**: Check spelling and grammar
- **Mobile-friendly**: Test on phone before publishing

### 32.9 Technical Questions

**Q: Does AHC have an API I can use?**

A: Yes, AHC provides a REST API for developers. Access:
- Settings → API Settings
- Generate API key
- View API documentation
- Requires Administrator role

**Q: Can I integrate AHC with other systems?**

A: Yes, integration options:
- **Webhooks**: Send data to external services on events
- **API**: Pull AHC data into other applications
- **Social media**: Auto-post to Facebook, Twitter/X, LinkedIn
- **Email marketing**: Integrate with Mailchimp
- **Analytics**: Google Analytics integration
- Contact administrator for custom integrations

**Q: Is AHC accessible for users with disabilities?**

A: Yes, AHC follows WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility standards:
- Keyboard navigation support
- Screen reader compatible
- Color contrast compliant
- Alt text for images
- ARIA labels on interactive elements
- Semantic HTML structure

**Q: Can I use custom HTML/CSS in content?**

A: Limited custom code:
- **Rich text editor**: Use "Source" mode to add HTML
- **Allowed tags**: Most common HTML tags
- **Not allowed**: `<script>`, `<iframe>` (security risk)
- **CSS**: Inline styles allowed
- For complex custom code, contact developer

**Q: Does the platform support multiple languages?**

A: Currently, AHC supports English content. Multi-language support is planned for future updates. Contact administrator for timeline.

## Chapter 33: Getting Support

When you need help beyond this user guide, here's how to get support for the AHC platform.

### 33.1 Support Channels

**Internal Support (First Stop):**

```
For AHC platform users:

1. System Administrator
   - Name: [Your IT Administrator]
   - Email: it-support@ahc.org
   - Phone: [Extension]
   - Hours: Monday-Friday, 9 AM - 5 PM
   - Response time: Within 4 business hours

2. Content Team Lead
   - For content-related questions
   - Editorial guidance
   - Best practices
   - Content approval questions

3. Help Desk
   - General IT support
   - Password resets
   - Account access
   - Hardware/software issues
```

**External Technical Support:**

```
For system administrators:

Development Team:
- Email: dev-support@ahc-platform.com
- Emergency: +1-XXX-XXX-XXXX
- Available: 24/7 for critical issues
- Response: <1 hour for critical, <24 hours for normal
```

### 33.2 Before Contacting Support

**Gather Information:**

Before reaching out, collect:

```
✓ What you were trying to do
✓ What you expected to happen
✓ What actually happened
✓ Error message (exact text or screenshot)
✓ Steps to reproduce the issue
✓ Browser and version
✓ Your username and role
✓ Time/date issue occurred
✓ Whether issue affects others or just you
```

**Try Basic Troubleshooting:**

```
1. Refresh page (F5)
2. Clear browser cache
3. Try incognito/private mode
4. Try different browser
5. Log out and log back in
6. Check Chapter 31 for common solutions
```

### 33.3 Reporting Bugs

**How to Report a Bug:**

```
Use the bug report template:

Subject: [BUG] Brief description

1. Description:
   [Describe the bug in detail]

2. Steps to Reproduce:
   a. Go to...
   b. Click on...
   c. Enter...
   d. See error

3. Expected Behavior:
   [What should happen]

4. Actual Behavior:
   [What actually happened]

5. Screenshots:
   [Attach screenshots if applicable]

6. Environment:
   - Browser: Chrome 120
   - OS: Windows 11
   - User Role: Editor
   - Time: 2025-12-10 10:30 AM

7. Impact:
   - Severity: [Low / Medium / High / Critical]
   - Affects: [Just me / Multiple users / All users]
   - Workaround: [If you found one]
```

**Bug Severity Levels:**

```
Critical:
- System completely unusable
- Data loss occurring
- Security vulnerability
- Affects all users
→ Contact emergency support immediately

High:
- Major feature not working
- Affects many users
- No workaround available
→ Report within 1 hour, expect same-day response

Medium:
- Feature partially working
- Workaround available
- Affects limited users
→ Report within 1 day, expect 2-3 day response

Low:
- Minor inconvenience
- Cosmetic issue
- Rare occurrence
→ Report as convenient, expect 1-2 week response
```

### 33.4 Feature Requests

**Requesting New Features:**

If you have ideas for improving AHC:

```
Feature Request Process:

1. Check Roadmap:
   - Ask administrator for product roadmap
   - Feature may already be planned

2. Submit Request:
   Email: feature-requests@ahc.org
   
   Include:
   - Feature description
   - Why it's needed (use case)
   - Who would benefit
   - How you'd use it
   - Examples from other systems (if applicable)

3. Review Process:
   - Features reviewed quarterly
   - Prioritized by impact and effort
   - Top requests added to roadmap
   - You'll be notified of status

4. Timeline:
   - High priority: 1-3 months
   - Medium priority: 3-6 months
   - Low priority: 6-12 months
   - Some features may not be approved
```

**Feature Request Template:**

```
Subject: [FEATURE] Feature name

1. Feature Description:
   [What you want to be able to do]

2. Problem Statement:
   [What problem does this solve?]

3. Current Workaround:
   [How do you handle this now?]

4. Proposed Solution:
   [How you envision it working]

5. Benefits:
   - Time saved: [estimate]
   - Users impacted: [number]
   - Frequency of use: [daily/weekly/monthly]

6. Priority:
   [Nice to have / Would be helpful / Critical need]

7. Examples:
   [Screenshots from other systems if applicable]
```

### 33.5 Emergency Procedures

**For Critical Issues:**

```
When to Use Emergency Support:

- Website completely down
- Data breach or security incident
- Data loss occurring
- System-wide outage
- Any issue affecting public-facing website

Emergency Contact:
Phone: [Emergency number - available 24/7]
Email: emergency@ahc.org (monitored 24/7)

What to Communicate:
1. "This is an emergency"
2. Brief description of issue
3. Impact (how many users, what's affected)
4. Your contact information
5. When it started

Stay Available:
- Keep phone/email accessible
- Support may call you back
- May need you to provide access or info
```

**Escalation Path:**

```
If issue isn't being resolved:

Level 1: System Administrator
 ↓ (No response in 4 hours)
Level 2: IT Manager
 ↓ (No response in 8 hours)
Level 3: CTO/Director of Technology
 ↓ (Critical issues only)
Level 4: External Development Team
```

### 33.6 Training & Onboarding

**Available Training:**

```
New User Onboarding:
- 30-minute intro session
- Role-specific training
- Hands-on practice environment
- Q&A with experienced user
- Contact: training@ahc.org

Ongoing Training:
- Monthly "Lunch & Learn" sessions
- Advanced features workshops
- Quarterly "What's New" updates
- One-on-one coaching available

Training Materials:
- This user guide (comprehensive)
- Video tutorials (if available)
- Quick reference cards (see Appendix F)
- FAQ database
```

**Requesting Training:**

```
Email: training@ahc.org

Include:
- Your name and role
- Topic(s) you want to learn
- Current skill level
- Preferred training format (video, in-person, documentation)
- Urgency
- Preferred date/time

Response: Within 2 business days
```

Please submit feedback:

Include:

- Your suggestion
- Your role (helps us prioritize)

Your input helps everyone!
```

### 33.8 Self-Service Resources

**Help Yourself First:**

```
Before contacting support, try:

1. This User Guide
   - Comprehensive 400+ page manual
   - Search PDF for keywords
   - Check Table of Contents
   - Review relevant chapter

2. FAQ (Chapter 32)
   - Common questions answered
   - Organized by topic
   - Updated regularly

3. Troubleshooting (Chapter 31)
   - Solutions to common issues
   - Step-by-step fixes
   - Multiple solutions per problem

4. Quick Reference Cards (Appendix F)
   - One-page guides
   - Common tasks
   - Shortcuts and tips

5. Admin Panel Help
   - Look for [?] help icons
   - Tooltips on hover
   - Inline documentation

6. Ask Colleagues
   - Someone may have solved it
   - Share knowledge internally
   - Build team expertise
```

```
(If available)

### 33.9 Feedback & Suggestions

**We Value Your Input:**

```
Share your experience:

What's Working Well:
- Features you love
- Time-saving discoveries
- Positive experiences
→ Email: feedback@ahc.org

What Could Be Better:
- Pain points
- Confusing workflows
- Feature gaps
→ Email: feedback@ahc.org

Response:
- All feedback reviewed
- May follow up for details
- Updates shared in quarterly reviews
```

### 33.10 Support Best Practices

**For Faster Resolution:**

```
✓ Be specific: Exact error messages, not "it doesn't work"
✓ Provide context: What were you trying to accomplish?
✓ Include screenshots: Visual proof helps immensely
✓ List what you tried: Shows effort, eliminates suggestions
✓ Be available: Respond promptly to support questions
✓ Test suggestions: Try proposed solutions and report back
✓ Confirm resolution: Let support know when fixed
✓ Say thanks: Support teams appreciate recognition
```

**What NOT to Do:**

```
✗ "It's broken" with no details
✗ Contact multiple support channels simultaneously
✗ Demand immediate response for low-priority issues
✗ Get angry at support staff
✗ Assume support already knows your issue
✗ Ignore follow-up questions
✗ Mark resolved issues as urgent
```

**Building Good Support Relationships:**

```
- Be respectful and patient
- Understand support has priorities
- Provide complete information upfront
- Be open to troubleshooting steps
- Share feedback (positive and negative)
- Participate in training opportunities
- Help other users when you can
```

# PART 6: APPENDICES

## Appendix A: Glossary of Terms

Alphabetical listing of terms used throughout the AHC platform and this guide.

**2FA (Two-Factor Authentication):** Security feature requiring two forms of identification to log in (password + code from authenticator app).

**Administrator:** User role with full access to all platform features, settings, and user management.

**AHC:** African Health Collaborative - the organization and platform.

**Alt Text (Alternative Text):** Descriptive text added to images for accessibility, used by screen readers and when images fail to load.

**API (Application Programming Interface):** Set of protocols allowing external applications to interact with AHC data.

**Archive:** Status for content no longer active but kept for records; not visible to public.

**Author:** User role that can create and edit own content but typically cannot publish without approval.

**Backup:** Copy of database and files saved for disaster recovery.

**Badge:** Visual indicator (colored label) showing status, type, or category.

**Bounce Rate:** Percentage of email addresses that couldn't receive emails (invalid/non-existent).

**Breadcrumb:** Navigation trail showing current page location in site hierarchy (e.g., Home > News > Article).

**Cache:** Temporary storage of data for faster loading; occasionally needs clearing.

**CMS (Content Management System):** Software for creating, managing, and publishing digital content (AHC platform is a CMS).

**CDN (Content Delivery Network):** Network of servers distributing content for faster global access.

**CTA (Call-to-Action):** Button or link prompting user action (e.g., "Learn More", "Register Now").

**CSV (Comma-Separated Values):** File format for exporting/importing data, opens in Excel.

**Dashboard:** Main admin panel overview page showing statistics and quick links.

**Datatable:** Interactive table with sorting, filtering, pagination, and search features.

**Draft:** Content status for work in progress; not visible to public.

**Editor (role):** User role that can create, edit, and publish content; manage media.

**Editor (tool):** Rich text editor for formatting content (like Microsoft Word for web).

**Embed:** Insert external content (videos, maps) into pages.

**Excerpt:** Brief summary of content shown in listings and previews.

**Featured Image:** Main image representing content, shown in listings and social shares.

**Filter:** Tool to narrow down lists by specific criteria (date, status, category, etc.).

**Frontend:** Public-facing website that visitors see.

**Backend:** Admin panel where authorized users manage content.

**GDPR (General Data Protection Regulation):** European privacy law requiring user consent for data collection.

**Health Pillar:** Core focus area of AHC work (e.g., Maternal Health, Nutrition).

**Hover:** Moving mouse cursor over element without clicking.

**HTML (HyperText Markup Language):** Code used to structure web content.

**HTTPS:** Secure version of HTTP protocol, encrypts data transmission.

**Icon:** Small graphic symbol representing action or item.

**Inactive:** User status preventing login; account exists but is disabled.

**IP Address:** Unique numerical identifier for devices on network.

**Keyword:** Word or phrase used in search or SEO.

**LinkedIn:** Professional networking platform; AHC leaders can have profile links.

**Markdown:** Lightweight markup language for formatting text.

**Media Library:** Central repository for all uploaded images, videos, and documents.

**Meta Description:** Brief description of page content for search engines (155 characters recommended).

**Migration:** Database update script that modifies table structure.

**Modal:** Popup window overlaying main page for forms or information.

**Newsletter:** Regular email publication sent to subscribers.

**Notification:** Alert about system events or actions requiring attention.

**Pagination:** Dividing content into multiple pages (Page 1, 2, 3...).

**Partner:** Organization collaborating with AHC.

**Permalink:** Permanent URL for specific content page.

**Permission:** Authorization to perform specific action in system.

**Pillar:** See "Health Pillar".

**Plugin:** Add-on extending platform functionality.

**Preview:** View how content will look before publishing.

**Publish:** Make content live and visible to public.

**Published:** Content status indicating live on website.

**Query:** Database request to retrieve specific data.

**Queue:** List of tasks (like emails) waiting to be processed.

**REST API:** Type of API using HTTP requests to access data.

**Rich Text Editor:** WYSIWYG editor for formatting content visually.

**Role:** Set of permissions defining what user can do in system.

**Responsive:** Design adapting to different screen sizes (desktop, tablet, mobile).

**Scheduled:** Content set to publish automatically at future date/time.

**Screen Reader:** Software reading on-screen content aloud for visually impaired users.

**Search Index:** Database structure optimizing content search.

**SEO (Search Engine Optimization):** Practices improving website visibility in search engines.

**Session:** Period of active user login; expires after inactivity.

**Slug:** URL-friendly version of title (lowercase, hyphens, no spaces).

**SMTP (Simple Mail Transfer Protocol):** Standard for sending emails.

**Sort:** Arrange list by specific column (ascending/descending).

**SSL Certificate:** Digital certificate enabling HTTPS encryption.

**Status:** Current state of content (Draft, Published, Archived).

**Subscriber:** Person who signed up to receive newsletters.

**Tag:** Label or keyword categorizing content.

**Template:** Pre-designed layout for consistent content creation.

**Thumbnail:** Small version of image used in listings.

**Timezone:** Geographic region's time standard; affects scheduled publishing.

**Toggle:** Switch between two states (on/off, yes/no).

**Tooltip:** Small text box appearing on hover, providing additional information.

**Two-Factor Authentication:** See "2FA".

**UI (User Interface):** Visual elements users interact with.

**Unpublish:** Change content from Published to Draft, removing from public view.

**Unsubscribe:** Action removing subscriber from newsletter list.

**Upload:** Transfer file from local computer to server.

**URL (Uniform Resource Locator):** Web address (e.g., https://ahc.org/news).

**User:** Person with account and access to platform.

**Version:** Saved state of content at specific point in time.

**Version Control:** System tracking and managing changes over time.

**Viewer:** User role with read-only access to admin panel.

**Webhook:** Automated message sent to external service when event occurs.

**Widget:** Small component displaying specific information on dashboard.

**WYSIWYG (What You See Is What You Get):** Editor showing content as it will appear when published.

**XLSX:** Microsoft Excel file format for spreadsheets.

---

## Appendix B: Keyboard Shortcuts

Speed up your workflow with these keyboard shortcuts in the AHC admin panel.

*Note: Shortcuts may vary by browser and operating system. Most use Ctrl on Windows/Linux, Cmd (⌘) on Mac.*

### B.1 Global Shortcuts

**Navigation:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + K      Quick search / Command palette
Ctrl/Cmd + /      Toggle sidebar
Ctrl/Cmd + [      Navigate back
Ctrl/Cmd + ]      Navigate forward
Alt + H           Go to home/dashboard
Esc               Close modal/dialog
```

**General:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + S      Save current form/content
Ctrl/Cmd + Enter  Submit form/publish
Ctrl/Cmd + Z      Undo
Ctrl/Cmd + Y      Redo
Ctrl/Cmd + F      Find in page
F5 or Ctrl/Cmd+R  Refresh page
Ctrl+Shift+R      Hard refresh (clear cache)
```

### B.2 Content Editor Shortcuts

**Text Formatting:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + B      Bold text
Ctrl/Cmd + I      Italic text
Ctrl/Cmd + U      Underline text
Ctrl/Cmd + K      Insert/edit link
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift+S    Strikethrough
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift+X    Clear formatting
```

**Paragraph Formatting:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + Alt + 1-6  Heading 1-6
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + 7  Numbered list
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + 8  Bulleted list
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + 9  Blockquote
Ctrl/Cmd + ]          Increase indent
Ctrl/Cmd + [          Decrease indent
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + E  Align center
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + L  Align left
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + R  Align right
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + J  Justify
```

**Special Functions:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + A  Insert link
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + M  Insert media/image
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + K  Insert code block
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + Q  Insert quote
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + V  Paste without formatting
Tab                   Indent (in lists)
Shift + Tab           Outdent (in lists)
```

### B.3 Datatable Shortcuts

**When focused in datatable:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + F      Focus search box
Arrow Up/Down     Navigate rows
Enter             Open/edit selected row
Delete            Delete selected row (with confirmation)
Ctrl/Cmd + A      Select all rows
Shift + Click     Select range of rows
Ctrl/Cmd + Click  Select multiple rows
```

### B.4 Media Library Shortcuts

```
Ctrl/Cmd + U      Upload new file
Ctrl/Cmd + A      Select all media items
Shift + Click     Select multiple items
Delete            Delete selected items
Enter             Open selected item details
Esc               Deselect all
Arrow keys        Navigate grid
```

### B.5 Browser Shortcuts

**Useful for admin work:**
```
Ctrl/Cmd + T      New tab
Ctrl/Cmd + W      Close tab
Ctrl/Cmd + Shift+T    Reopen closed tab
Ctrl/Cmd + Tab    Switch tabs (forward)
Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Tab    Switch tabs (backward)
Ctrl/Cmd + L      Focus address bar
Ctrl/Cmd + D      Bookmark page
Ctrl/Cmd + +      Zoom in
Ctrl/Cmd + -      Zoom out
Ctrl/Cmd + 0      Reset zoom
F11               Fullscreen toggle
F12               Open developer tools
```

### B.6 Form Shortcuts

```
Tab               Next field
Shift + Tab       Previous field
Enter             Submit form (when not in textarea)
Esc               Cancel/close form
Alt + S           Save (if configured)
```

### B.7 Accessibility Shortcuts

**For screen reader users:**
```
Tab               Navigate to next interactive element
Shift + Tab       Navigate to previous interactive element
Enter or Space    Activate button/link
Arrow keys        Navigate menus and options
Esc               Close dialogs and menus
```

### B.8 Custom Shortcuts

*Note: These may be configured by your system administrator*

```
Alt + N           Create new content
Alt + D           Go to dashboard
Alt + M           View messages
Alt + U           User management
Alt + S           Settings
```

### B.9 Browser DevTools (For Debugging)

```
F12               Open DevTools
Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+I  Open DevTools
Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+J  Open Console
Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+C  Inspect element
Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+M  Toggle device toolbar (mobile view)
```

### B.10 Tips for Using Shortcuts

**Learn Gradually:**
- Don't try to memorize all at once
- Start with most frequent actions (Ctrl+S, Ctrl+B, Ctrl+K)
- Add 1-2 new shortcuts per week
- Practice until muscle memory develops

**Visual Reminders:**
- Many buttons show shortcuts in tooltips (hover to see)
- Keep this reference handy initially
- Print quick reference card (see Appendix F)

**Customize:**
- Some platforms allow shortcut customization
- Ask administrator about configuration options

## Appendix D: Security Checklist

Use this checklist to maintain security best practices when using the AHC platform.

### D.1 Password Security

**Strong Password Checklist:**
```
☐ At least 12 characters long
☐ Includes uppercase letters (A-Z)
☐ Includes lowercase letters (a-z)
☐ Includes numbers (0-9)
☐ Includes special characters (!@#$%^&*)
☐ Not used on any other website
☐ Not based on personal information (name, birthday)
☐ Not a common word or phrase
☐ Changed within last 90 days

Examples of STRONG passwords:
✓ K9#mPw@2025$Health!
✓ Tr33s&C10uds#Collaborative
✓ AHC!2025@SecurePass#99

Examples of WEAK passwords:
✗ password123
✗ ahchealth
✗ JohnSmith2025
✗ qwerty123
```

**Password Management:**
```
☐ Use a password manager (LastPass, 1Password, Bitwarden)
☐ Never save passwords in browser on shared computers
☐ Don't write passwords on sticky notes
☐ Don't share passwords via email or messaging
☐ Use unique password for AHC (don't reuse)
```

### D.2 Two-Factor Authentication (2FA)

**2FA Setup Checklist:**
```
☐ Enable 2FA on your account
☐ Use authenticator app (Google Authenticator, Authy)
☐ Save backup codes in secure location
☐ Print backup codes and store offline
☐ Test 2FA login before logging out
☐ Re-enable 2FA if you get new phone
```

**2FA Best Practices:**
```
☐ Don't use SMS for 2FA (if avoidable - can be intercepted)
☐ Keep backup codes separate from primary device
☐ Set up 2FA on password manager app too
☐ Review trusted devices periodically
☐ Remove old/unused trusted devices
```

### D.3 Account Security

**Login Security:**
```
☐ Always log out when finished (especially shared computers)
☐ Don't select "Remember Me" on public computers
☐ Close browser completely after logging out (on shared PCs)
☐ Don't share your login credentials with anyone
☐ Report suspicious login alerts immediately
☐ Review login history monthly
☐ Log out from all devices if phone/laptop stolen
```

**Session Security:**
```
☐ Don't leave computer unattended while logged in
☐ Lock screen when stepping away (Windows+L or Ctrl+Cmd+Q)
☐ Don't use AHC on public WiFi without VPN
☐ Close admin panel when not actively using
☐ Be aware of people viewing your screen
```

### D.4 Content Security

**Before Publishing:**
```
☐ Remove sensitive information (personal data, phone numbers)
☐ Don't include passwords or credentials in content
☐ Verify links go to legitimate websites
☐ Scan uploaded files for viruses
☐ Remove metadata from images (location, camera info)
☐ Don't publish internal documents publicly
☐ Double-check recipient list for newsletters
```

**Media Security:**
```
☐ Only upload images you have rights to use
☐ Remove faces/identifiable info if needed (GDPR)
☐ Don't upload documents with tracked changes visible
☐ Check PDF properties for hidden author info
☐ Compress files before uploading (reduces malware risk)
```

### D.5 Email Security

**Email Best Practices:**
```
☐ Verify sender before clicking links in emails
☐ Don't click suspicious password reset emails
☐ Check URL before entering credentials
☐ Look for HTTPS and padlock icon
☐ Hover over links to see actual destination
☐ Don't download unexpected attachments
☐ Report phishing attempts to IT
```

**Phishing Red Flags:**
```
🚩 Urgent language ("Act now!" "Your account will be suspended!")
🚩 Grammar/spelling errors
🚩 Sender email doesn't match organization domain
🚩 Requests for password or personal information
🚩 Unexpected attachments
🚩 Suspicious links (hover to check actual URL)
🚩 Too good to be true offers
```

### D.6 Browser Security

**Browser Checklist:**
```
☐ Keep browser updated to latest version
☐ Enable automatic updates
☐ Use only secure browsers (Chrome, Firefox, Edge, Safari)
☐ Don't use Internet Explorer (no longer supported)
☐ Clear cache/cookies monthly
☐ Review and remove unnecessary browser extensions
☐ Only install extensions from official stores
☐ Check extension permissions (don't grant excessive access)
```

**Privacy Settings:**
```
☐ Enable "Do Not Track" if desired
☐ Block third-party cookies
☐ Use HTTPS-only mode
☐ Disable auto-fill for passwords on shared computers
☐ Clear browsing data when using public computers
```

### D.7 Device Security

**Computer Security:**
```
☐ Install antivirus software and keep updated
☐ Enable firewall
☐ Keep operating system updated
☐ Enable automatic security updates
☐ Use screen lock with password
☐ Enable disk encryption (BitLocker, FileVault)
☐ Don't install pirated software
☐ Run malware scans weekly
```

**Mobile Security:**
```
☐ Keep phone OS updated
☐ Use strong PIN/password (not 1234 or 0000)
☐ Enable biometric lock (fingerprint/face)
☐ Enable "Find My Device" feature
☐ Install apps only from official stores
☐ Review app permissions periodically
☐ Don't jailbreak/root device
☐ Enable remote wipe capability
```

### D.8 Network Security

**Office Network:**
```
☐ Connect to secure company WiFi
☐ Verify network name before connecting
☐ Use VPN if required by organization
☐ Don't access admin panel on guest WiFi
```

**Public WiFi:**
```
☐ Avoid accessing AHC admin on public WiFi
☐ If must use public WiFi, use VPN
☐ Don't enter passwords on public WiFi without VPN
☐ Turn off auto-connect to WiFi networks
☐ Forget public WiFi networks after use
☐ Use mobile hotspot instead when possible
```

### D.9 Data Protection

**Personal Data:**
```
☐ Only collect necessary user data
☐ Don't store sensitive data unnecessarily
☐ Delete old/unnecessary contact information
☐ Comply with privacy regulations (GDPR, etc.)
☐ Honor unsubscribe requests immediately
☐ Provide privacy policy link in communications
☐ Don't share email lists with third parties
```

**Backups:**
```
☐ Know backup schedule (ask administrator)
☐ Test restore process periodically (administrator task)
☐ Keep backups encrypted
☐ Store backups off-site/cloud
☐ Keep multiple backup versions
☐ Document backup/restore procedures
```

### D.10 Incident Response

**If Account Compromised:**
```
1. ☐ Change password immediately
2. ☐ Enable/reset Two-Factor Authentication
3. ☐ Log out from all devices
4. ☐ Review recent account activity
5. ☐ Check for unauthorized content changes
6. ☐ Contact administrator immediately
7. ☐ Report to security team: security@ahc.org
8. ☐ Change passwords on other sites if reused (don't reuse!)
9. ☐ Monitor account closely for next 30 days
10. ☐ File incident report if required
```

**If You Suspect Data Breach:**
```
1. ☐ Don't panic, act quickly
2. ☐ Document what you observed
3. ☐ Do NOT attempt to "fix" or investigate yourself
4. ☐ Report to IT/Security team IMMEDIATELY
5. ☐ Follow organization's incident response plan
6. ☐ Don't discuss publicly until cleared by management
7. ☐ Preserve evidence (screenshots, logs)
8. ☐ Cooperate fully with investigation
```

### D.11 Monthly Security Review

**Once Per Month:**
```
☐ Review login history
☐ Check for suspicious account activity
☐ Update password if 90+ days old
☐ Review active sessions and trusted devices
☐ Update browser and OS
☐ Run full antivirus scan
☐ Review and remove unnecessary browser extensions
☐ Clear browser cache and cookies
☐ Check notification preferences
☐ Review permissions for users you manage (if applicable)
```

### D.12 Annual Security Tasks

**Once Per Year:**
```
☐ Change password (even if strong)
☐ Regenerate 2FA backup codes
☐ Review all notification settings
☐ Audit content for outdated/sensitive information
☐ Remove old/unused user accounts (administrators)
☐ Review role permissions (administrators)
☐ Update emergency contact information
☐ Review and update security policies
☐ Complete security awareness training
☐ Test disaster recovery plan (administrators)
```

---

## Appendix E: Content Style Guide

Guidelines for creating consistent, high-quality content on the AHC platform.

### E.1 Writing Style

**General Principles:**

```
✓ Clear and concise
✓ Professional but approachable
✓ Action-oriented
✓ Inclusive and respectful
✓ Evidence-based
✓ Accessible to general audience
```

**Voice and Tone:**

```
AHC Voice:
- Authoritative yet compassionate
- Expert but not condescending
- Hopeful and solution-oriented
- Collaborative (we, our, together)
- Culturally sensitive

Tone Varies by Content:
- News: Informative, objective
- Events: Exciting, inviting
- Health info: Caring, clear, actionable
- Partnerships: Collaborative, professional
- Appeals/CTAs: Urgent but not alarmist
```

**Person and Perspective:**

```
Use "we" for AHC actions:
✓ "We are launching a new initiative..."
✓ "Our partners include..."

Use "you" to address readers:
✓ "You can register for the event..."
✓ "Learn how you can get involved..."

Avoid:
✗ "One should consider..." (too formal)
✗ Excessive use of "I" (unless personal story)
```

### E.2 Grammar and Mechanics

**Capitalization:**

```
Proper Nouns:
✓ African Health Collaborative (full name)
✓ AHC (abbreviation)
✓ Names of programs, events, partners
✓ Geographic locations (Africa, Kenya, etc.)

Titles and Headings:
✓ Use title case for main headings
✓ Sentence case for subheadings

Health Conditions:
✗ Don't capitalize unless part of proper name
✓ diabetes, malaria, tuberculosis
✓ Ebola (named after Ebola River)
```

**Punctuation:**

```
Oxford Comma:
✓ Use it: "health, education, and nutrition"
✗ Not: "health, education and nutrition"

Quotation Marks:
- Use for direct quotes
- Periods and commas inside quotes (US style)
- Colons and semicolons outside quotes

Hyphens and Dashes:
- Hyphen (-): compound modifiers (long-term impact)
- En dash (–): ranges (2020–2025)
- Em dash (—): breaks in thought—like this—for emphasis
```

**Numbers:**

```
Spell out:
- Numbers one through nine
- Numbers starting sentences

Use numerals:
- 10 and above
- Dates, times, ages, percentages
- Statistics and data

Examples:
✓ "Five key principles"
✓ "The program serves 1,245 families"
✓ "Event starts at 2:00 PM"
✓ "Children under 5 years old"
✓ "15% increase"
```

**Abbreviations:**

```
First Use:
- Spell out, then abbreviation in parentheses
- Example: "World Health Organization (WHO)"
- Subsequent uses: just "WHO"

Common AHC Abbreviations:
- AHC (African Health Collaborative)
- NGO (Non-Governmental Organization)
- WHO (World Health Organization)
- USAID (United States Agency for International Development)

Don't abbreviate:
- In titles/headings
- If only used once
- If not widely known
```

### E.3 Formatting Standards

**Headings:**

```
Hierarchy:
H1: Page Title (only one per page)
H2: Main sections
H3: Subsections
H4: Sub-subsections
H5-H6: Rarely needed

Best Practices:
✓ Use headings in order (don't skip levels)
✓ Keep headings descriptive
✓ Use title case for H1-H2
✓ Sentence case acceptable for H3-H6
✓ Don't end headings with periods

Examples:
H1: New Maternal Health Initiative Launches in Kenya
H2: Program Overview
H3: Target communities
```

**Paragraphs:**

```
Structure:
- One main idea per paragraph
- 2-4 sentences ideal
- Maximum 5-6 sentences
- Use shorter paragraphs for web (easier to scan)

Opening:
- Lead with most important information
- First sentence should hook reader
- Answer "who, what, when, where, why" early
```

**Lists:**

```
Use Bulleted Lists for:
- Unordered items
- Features or benefits
- Options or choices

Use Numbered Lists for:
- Sequential steps
- Rankings
- Chronological items

List Punctuation:
- If items are complete sentences, use periods
- If fragments, no punctuation
- Be consistent within list

Examples:
Steps to Register:
1. Visit the registration page
2. Complete the form
3. Submit and await confirmation

Program Benefits:
- Free health screenings
- Educational workshops
- Community support groups
```

**Links:**

```
Link Text:
✓ Use descriptive text: "View our annual report"
✗ Avoid: "Click here" or "Read more"
✓ Make link text meaningful standalone
✓ Keep link text concise (2-6 words)

Link Formatting:
- Underline links in body text
- Use button style for primary CTAs
- Open external links in new tab
- Ensure links are keyboard accessible
```

### E.4 SEO Best Practices

**Titles:**

```
Guidelines:
- 50-60 characters ideal
- Include primary keyword near beginning
- Make compelling and accurate
- Unique for each page
- Front-load important words

Examples:
✓ "Maternal Health Services in Rural Kenya | AHC"
✓ "2025 Annual Health Conference - Register Now"
✗ "Page" (not descriptive)
✗ "This is our really long title about maternal health services that goes on and on" (too long)
```

**Meta Descriptions:**

```
Guidelines:
- 150-160 characters ideal
- Summarize page content
- Include call-to-action
- Include target keyword naturally
- Unique for each page

Examples:
✓ "Join AHC's annual conference on March 15-17, 2025. Learn from health experts, network with peers, and discover innovative solutions. Register today!"
```

**Keywords:**

```
Best Practices:
- Use primary keyword in title, first paragraph, and at least one heading
- Don't stuff keywords (write naturally)
- Use related terms and synonyms
- Focus on user intent, not just keywords
- Keep keyword density around 1-2%
```

**URLs/Slugs:**

```
Guidelines:
- Use lowercase
- Separate words with hyphens
- Keep short and descriptive
- Include primary keyword
- Avoid stop words (a, the, and) if possible
- No special characters

Examples:
✓ maternal-health-initiative
✓ 2025-annual-conference
✗ page.php?id=123
✗ This_Is_The_Page_About_Health
```

### E.5 Accessibility Guidelines

**Alt Text for Images:**

```
Guidelines:
- Describe what's in the image
- Keep under 125 characters
- Don't start with "Image of..." (redundant)
- For decorative images, use empty alt=""
- Include text shown in image if important

Examples:
✓ "Healthcare workers administering vaccines to children in rural clinic"
✓ "Chart showing 40% increase in maternal health visits, 2020-2025"
✗ "IMG_1234.jpg"
✗ "Click here to learn more"
```

**Readability:**

```
Best Practices:
✓ Use simple, clear language
✓ Define technical terms
✓ Keep sentences under 25 words
✓ Use active voice
✓ Break up long blocks of text
✓ Use headings to organize
✓ Aim for 8th-grade reading level

Test Readability:
- Use tools like Hemingway Editor
- Target Flesch Reading Ease score 60+
- Avoid jargon when possible
```

**Color and Contrast:**

```
Text Contrast:
- Ensure sufficient contrast ratio
- Normal text: at least 4.5:1
- Large text: at least 3:1
- Don't rely on color alone to convey meaning

Link Visibility:
- Make links clearly distinguishable
- Underline links in body text
- Use high-contrast colors
```

### E.6 Content Types

**News Articles:**

```
Structure:
- Headline (compelling, newsworthy)
- Excerpt (1-2 sentence summary)
- Lead paragraph (who, what, when, where, why)
- Body (details, quotes, context)
- Conclusion (impact, next steps, CTA)
- Featured image (relevant, high-quality)

Tone:
- Objective and factual
- Cite sources
- Include quotes from stakeholders
- Present balanced perspective

Example Opening:
"The African Health Collaborative announced today the launch of a $5 million maternal health initiative serving rural communities in Kenya. The three-year program will provide prenatal care, skilled birth attendance, and postnatal support to an estimated 50,000 women."
```

**Event Listings:**

```
Essential Information:
✓ Event name (clear and specific)
✓ Date and time (include timezone)
✓ Location (address or "Virtual")
✓ Brief description
✓ Who should attend
✓ Registration details
✓ Cost (or "Free")
✓ Contact information

Formatting:
- Put key details at top
- Use headings for sections
- Include clear CTA ("Register Now")
- Add calendar download link
- List speakers/agenda if applicable
```

**Partner Profiles:**

```
Standard Sections:
- Partner name and logo
- Mission/overview (1-2 paragraphs)
- Focus areas
- Geographic reach
- Partnership details (how we collaborate)
- Website and contact
- Featured image

Tone:
- Professional and collaborative
- Highlight mutual benefits
- Show respect and appreciation
- Include specific achievements
```

**Health Information:**

```
Requirements:
- Medically accurate (cite sources)
- Reviewed by qualified professional
- Clear and accessible language
- Culturally appropriate
- Actionable advice
- Include disclaimer if needed

Structure:
- Overview of condition/topic
- Symptoms or signs
- Prevention strategies
- Treatment options
- When to seek help
- Resources and links
- References/sources

Disclaimer Example:
"This information is for educational purposes only and does not replace professional medical advice. Consult a healthcare provider for personalized guidance."
```

### E.7 Prohibited Content

**Never Include:**

```
✗ Discriminatory or hate speech
✗ Personal attacks
✗ Misinformation or unverified claims
✗ Copyrighted content without permission
✗ Private personal information
✗ Political endorsements
✗ Religious proselytizing
✗ Commercial advertising (unless partner)
✗ Graphic medical imagery without warning
✗ Anything illegal or promoting illegal activity
```

### E.8 Editorial Checklist

**Before Publishing:**

```
Content:
☐ Accurate and fact-checked
☐ Clear and concise
☐ Appropriate tone for audience
☐ No sensitive/private information
☐ Sources cited where needed

Format:
☐ Proper headings hierarchy
☐ Short paragraphs (2-4 sentences)
☐ Bullet points for scannability
☐ Link text descriptive
☐ No broken links

SEO:
☐ Title 50-60 characters
☐ Meta description 150-160 characters
☐ Slug URL-friendly
☐ Primary keyword included naturally
☐ Alt text on all images

Accessibility:
☐ Alt text on images (descriptive)
☐ Sufficient color contrast
☐ Heading structure logical
☐ Links make sense out of context
☐ Reading level appropriate

Media:
☐ Featured image selected
☐ Images compressed and optimized
☐ All media has alt text
☐ Videos have captions (if applicable)

Technical:
☐ Category assigned
☐ Tags added (if used)
☐ Publish date set correctly
☐ Author credited
☐ Status set (Draft/Published)

Final Check:
☐ Proofread for spelling/grammar
☐ Preview on desktop
☐ Preview on mobile
☐ Test all links
☐ Reviewed by second person (if possible)
```

## Appendix F: Quick Reference Cards

One-page guides for common tasks. Print and keep handy!

### F.1 Quick Start Card

```
AHC PLATFORM - QUICK START GUIDE
=================================

LOGIN
-----
URL: https://admin.ahc.org/login
1. Enter username and password

DASHBOARD
---------
- Overview stats
- Recent activity
- Quick links to common tasks
- Notifications badge (top right)

CREATE NEWS ARTICLE
-------------------
1. Admin Panel → Content → News
2. Click [Create New]
3. Enter Title (auto-generates slug)
4. Select Category
5. Upload Featured Image
6. Write content in editor
7. Add excerpt (summary)
8. Set Status: Draft or Published
9. Set Publish Date
10. [Save] or [Save & Publish]

KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS
------------------
Ctrl+S - Save
Ctrl+B - Bold
Ctrl+I - Italic
Ctrl+K - Insert link
Ctrl+Z - Undo
Esc - Close modal

HELP & SUPPORT
--------------
User Guide: [Location]
Support: support@ahc.org
Phone: [Number]

### F.2 Rich Text Editor Card

```
RICH TEXT EDITOR REFERENCE
==========================

TOOLBAR BUTTONS
---------------
[B] Bold - Ctrl+B
[I] Italic - Ctrl+I
[U] Underline - Ctrl+U
[🔗] Link - Ctrl+K
[📷] Image
[🎬] Video embed
[≡] Headings (H2-H6)
[•] Bullet list
[1.] Numbered list
[⮜] Decrease indent
[⮞] Increase indent
["] Blockquote
[<>] Source code view

FORMATTING TIPS
---------------
• Use H2 for main sections
• Use H3 for subsections
• Keep paragraphs short (2-4 sentences)
• Use lists for scannability
• Bold for emphasis (sparingly)
• Add links to related content

INSERT LINK
-----------
1. Select text
2. Click 🔗 or Ctrl+K
3. Enter URL
4. Check "Open in new tab" for external links
5. Click Insert

INSERT IMAGE
------------
1. Click 📷 button
2. Choose from Media Library or Upload New
3. Select image
4. Add alt text (required)
5. Choose alignment
6. Click Insert

EMBED VIDEO
-----------
1. Click 🎬 button
2. Paste YouTube or Vimeo URL
3. Video embeds automatically
4. Adjust size if needed

SOURCE CODE VIEW
----------------
• Click <> button
• Edit HTML directly
• Use for advanced formatting
• Click <> again to return
```

### F.3 Media Upload Card

```
MEDIA UPLOAD QUICK GUIDE
========================

SUPPORTED FORMATS
-----------------
Images: JPG, PNG, WebP, GIF
Videos: MP4, WebM, MOV
Documents: PDF, DOC, DOCX, XLS, XLSX

FILE SIZE LIMITS
----------------
Images: Max 5 MB
Videos: Max 100 MB
Documents: Max 10 MB

RECOMMENDED IMAGE SIZES
-----------------------
Featured Images: 1200×630 px minimum
Gallery Images: 1920×1080 px
Thumbnails: Auto-generated
Profile Photos: 500×500 px minimum

UPLOAD METHODS
--------------
Method 1: Drag & Drop
- Drag files directly into upload area
- Can drag multiple files

Method 2: Click to Browse
- Click [Select Files]
- Choose from computer
- Shift+Click for multiple files

AFTER UPLOAD
------------
1. Add alt text (required for images)
2. Add title (optional, uses filename by default)
3. Review and organize
4. Use in content

IMAGE BEST PRACTICES
--------------------
✓ Compress before uploading (TinyPNG, Squoosh)
✓ Use descriptive filenames
✓ Always add alt text
✓ Choose appropriate format:
  - JPG for photos
  - PNG for graphics with transparency
  - WebP for best compression
✓ Remove metadata (location data, etc.)

ALT TEXT EXAMPLES
-----------------
✓ "Healthcare workers administering vaccines in rural clinic"
✓ "Chart showing 40% increase in clinic visits"
✗ "IMG_1234.jpg"
✗ "Image"

TROUBLESHOOTING
---------------
File won't upload:
- Check file size under limit
- Verify file format supported
- Try renaming file (remove special characters)
- Try smaller file
- Try different browser

Image looks low quality:
- Upload higher resolution
- Don't upload already-compressed images
- Use quality 80-90% when compressing
```

### F.4 Content Publishing Card

```
CONTENT PUBLISHING CHECKLIST
=============================

BEFORE PUBLISHING
-----------------
Content:
☐ Title clear and compelling
☐ Content proofread (spelling, grammar)
☐ Facts verified and accurate
☐ Sources cited
☐ No sensitive information

Formatting:
☐ Headings used properly (H2, H3)
☐ Short paragraphs
☐ Lists for scannability
☐ Important points bolded
☐ All links working

Media:
☐ Featured image selected
☐ All images have alt text
☐ Images optimized (compressed)
☐ Videos embedded properly

SEO:
☐ Title 50-60 characters
☐ Slug URL-friendly
☐ Excerpt written (1-2 sentences)
☐ Meta description (if applicable)
☐ Category assigned

Technical:
☐ Status set to "Published"
☐ Publish date correct
☐ Author assigned
☐ Preview on desktop
☐ Preview on mobile

PUBLISHING STEPS
----------------
1. Review all checklist items above
2. Click [Preview] to see final appearance
3. Set Status to "Published"
4. Verify Publish Date:
   - Now: Publishes immediately
   - Future: Schedules for later
5. Click [Save & Publish]
6. Confirm success message
7. View on frontend ([View] button)
8. Share on social media (if applicable)

AFTER PUBLISHING
----------------
☐ Verify content appears on website
☐ Check all links work
☐ Test on mobile device
☐ Share with team
☐ Monitor for comments/feedback
☐ Check analytics after 24 hours

UNPUBLISH CONTENT
-----------------
1. Find content in list
2. Click [Edit]
3. Change Status to "Draft"
4. [Save]
5. Content removed from public site

SCHEDULE CONTENT
----------------
1. Create content normally
2. Set Status to "Published"
3. Set Publish Date to future date/time
4. [Save]
5. Content auto-publishes at that time
```

```
COMMON TASKS - QUICK REFERENCE
===============================

RESET PASSWORD (Self)
---------------------
1. Top right → [Your Name] → Profile

VIEW CONTACT MESSAGES
---------------------

2. Filter: [Not Replied ▼] for new messages
3. Click message to read
4. Toggle replied status button
5. Respond via email outside platform

CREATE EVENT
------------
1. Admin Panel → Events
2. [Create New Event]
3. Enter event name, date, time, location
4. Add description
5. Upload featured image
6. Set registration details
7. [Save & Publish]

UPLOAD TO MEDIA LIBRARY
-----------------------
1. Admin Panel → Media Library
2. Click [Upload]
3. Drag files or click to browse
4. Add alt text to images
5. [Save]

SEND NEWSLETTER
---------------
1. Admin Panel → Newsletter
2. [Create Newsletter]
3. Enter subject line
4. Compose content
5. [Send Test] to yourself
6. Review test email
7. [Send to All Subscribers]

EXPORT DATA
-----------

2. Apply filters if needed
3. Click [Export]
4. Choose format (Excel, CSV, PDF)
5. Wait for download
6. Open exported file

SEARCH CONTENT
--------------

---------------

MANAGE USER
-----------

CLEAR CACHE
-----------

2. Scroll to Cache Management
3. Select cache types to clear
4. [Clear Selected Caches]

### F.6 Troubleshooting Card

```
TROUBLESHOOTING QUICK FIXES
===========================

CAN'T LOG IN
------------
→ Check caps lock is OFF
→ Try "Forgot Password"
→ Check email spam folder for reset link
→ Wait 15 min if account locked
→ Try different browser
→ Contact admin if still locked

PAGE LOADING SLOWLY
-------------------
→ Close unnecessary browser tabs
→ Clear browser cache (Ctrl+Shift+Del)
→ Try different browser
→ Check internet connection
→ Contact admin if persists

CONTENT NOT SAVING
------------------
→ Check required fields filled
→ Look for red error messages
→ Check character limits
→ Try [Save Draft] instead
→ Copy content, refresh, paste
→ Try different browser

IMAGE WON'T UPLOAD
------------------
→ Check file under 5 MB
→ Verify format: JPG, PNG, WebP
→ Rename file (remove special characters)
→ Try compressing image
→ Try different browser

CHANGES NOT SHOWING ON SITE
----------------------------
→ Hard refresh browser (Ctrl+Shift+R)
→ Clear cache: Settings → Advanced
→ Wait 2-3 minutes
→ Check in incognito mode
→ Verify Status is "Published"
→ Check Publish Date isn't future

EDITOR NOT WORKING
------------------
→ Refresh page (F5)
→ Try different browser
→ Disable browser extensions temporarily
→ Clear browser cache
→ Try incognito mode

EMAIL NOT RECEIVED
------------------
→ Check spam/junk folder
→ Add noreply@ahc.org to contacts
→ Check in admin: Contact Messages
→ Verify email address correct
→ Contact administrator

ACCESS DENIED ERROR
-------------------
→ Check your user role
→ You may not have permission
→ Request access from administrator
→ Log out and log back in

FORGOT 2FA CODE
---------------
→ Generate new code in authenticator app
→ Use backup code
→ Contact administrator to disable 2FA

NEED MORE HELP?
---------------
Email: support@ahc.org
Phone: [Support Number]
User Guide: Chapter 31 (Troubleshooting)
```

## Using This Guide

**For Quick Reference:**
- Use the table of contents to find specific topics
- Check the Quick Reference Cards (Appendix F)
- Review the FAQ (Chapter 32)
- Use Ctrl+F to search PDF

**For Learning:**
- Read relevant chapters for your role
- Follow step-by-step instructions
- Practice in the platform as you learn
- Bookmark frequently referenced sections

**For Training:**
- Use as training manual for new users
- Reference during onboarding sessions
- Share relevant chapters with team members

## Keeping This Guide Current

This guide is a living document. As the AHC platform evolves:
- Features may be added or changed
- Screenshots may become outdated
- New best practices may emerge

**Provide Feedback:**
If you find errors, outdated information, or have suggestions for improvement, please contact: documentation@ahc.org

## Final Tips for Success

1. **Start Small**: Don't try to learn everything at once. Master basic tasks first.

2. **Practice Regularly**: Hands-on experience is the best teacher.

3. **Ask for Help**: Don't hesitate to contact support or colleagues.

4. **Share Knowledge**: Help others learn what you've mastered.

5. **Stay Curious**: Explore features you haven't used yet.

6. **Follow Best Practices**: Quality over quantity in content creation.

7. **Keep Security in Mind**: Protect your account and user data.

8. **Provide Feedback**: Help improve the platform for everyone.

## Thank You

Thank you for being part of the African Health Collaborative platform. Your work in managing and sharing health information makes a real difference in communities across Africa.

Whether you're publishing news, organizing events, managing partnerships, or supporting users, your contributions help advance AHC's mission of improving health outcomes throughout the continent.
